################################################################################
# FILE: .github/workflows/build.yml
################################################################################
name: Build

on:
  push:
    branches: [master]
  pull_request:
  workflow_dispatch:

concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 45
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-java@v6
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: 21
      - uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v6
      - run: chmod +x gradlew
      - run: ./gradlew --no-daemon testDebugUnitTest lintVitalRelease assembleDebug
      - name: Room schemas unchanged
        run: |
          if git diff -U0 app/databases/schemas | grep -E '^[-+][[:space:]]*"identityHash"'; then exit 1; fi
      - if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: test-reports
          path: app/build/reports/tests
          if-no-files-found: ignore

  release:
    needs: test
    if: github.event_name == 'push' && github.ref == 'refs/heads/master'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 45
    permissions:
      contents: write
    env:
      VERSION_NAME: 1.0.${{ github.run_number }}
      KEYSTORE_FILE: ${{ github.workspace }}/release.keystore
      STORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.STORE_PASSWORD }}
      KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD }}
      KEY_ALIAS: ${{ secrets.KEY_ALIAS }}
      MAPS_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.MAPS_API_KEY }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-java@v6
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: 21
      - uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v6
      - run: echo "VERSION_CODE=$((20261000 + GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER))" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
      - name: Decode keystore
        env:
          KEYSTORE_BASE64: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}
        run: |
          test -n "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" || { echo "KEYSTORE_BASE64 secret missing"; exit 1; }
          echo "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" | base64 -d > "$KEYSTORE_FILE"
      - run: chmod +x gradlew
      - run: ./gradlew --no-daemon assembleRelease
      - name: Collect APK
        run: |
          apk=$(find app/build/outputs/apk/release -name '*.apk' | head -n1)
          "$ANDROID_HOME"/build-tools/$(ls "$ANDROID_HOME"/build-tools | sort -V | tail -n1)/apksigner verify "$apk"
          mv "$apk" "Positional-v${VERSION_NAME}.apk"
      - uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v3
        with:
          tag_name: v${{ env.VERSION_NAME }}
          name: Positional v${{ env.VERSION_NAME }}
          target_commitish: ${{ github.sha }}
          generate_release_notes: true
          prerelease: false
          make_latest: true
          files: Positional-v${{ env.VERSION_NAME }}.apk
      - if: always()
        run: rm -f "$KEYSTORE_FILE"

# FILE: PRIVACY.md
################################################################################
# Privacy Policy

Positional is free, open source software distributed through GitHub Releases. It has no accounts, analytics, advertising or crash reporting.

## Data on your device

Location, trails, measurements, saved places and settings are stored only on your device and are removed when you uninstall the app or clear its data.

## Third party services

The map panels use the Google Maps SDK, and the location panels can use Google Play services and the Android Geocoder. These services may receive your location, IP address and device information under Google's privacy policy. When OpenStreetMap is the map source, map tiles are downloaded from the OpenStreetMap Foundation's tile servers, which receive your IP address, the app name and version, and the tiles around the area you view, under the [OSMF privacy policy](https://osmfoundation.org/wiki/Privacy_Policy). All other features work offline.

## Children

The app does not knowingly collect personal information from anyone, including children.

## Changes

Changes to this policy are published with new releases.

## Contact

Use the [issue tracker](https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional/issues).

################################################################################
# FILE: README.md
################################################################################
# Positional

![banner](screenshots/banner.png)

A location, compass, clock, level, trail and measurement toolkit for Android. Community fork distributed only through GitHub Releases; not published on Google Play.

[![Build](https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional/actions/workflows/build.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional/actions/workflows/build.yml)
[![Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/kusl/hamza417-positional)](https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional/releases/latest)
![API](https://img.shields.io/badge/API-24%2B-138D75?logo=android)
[![License: GPL v3](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-GPLv3-blue.svg)](LICENSE)

## Features

- **GPS**: coordinates (DD, DM, DMS, UTM), altitude, speed, accuracy, address, map with custom pins.
- **Compass**: magnetic azimuth, skins, calibration help.
- **Clock**: local and time zone time, sun and moon position, rise, set and twilight times.
- **Level**: bubble level from the accelerometer.
- **Trail**: record and review paths with markers.
- **Measure** and **Direction**: distances between points and bearings to targets.
- Home screen widgets: analog clock (Android 12+, ticked by the launcher with no background service), sun time, moon phase and twilight (Glance, hourly refresh).

## Install

1. Download the latest APK from [Releases](https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional/releases/latest), or
2. Add `https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional` to [Obtainium](https://github.com/ImranR98/Obtainium) for automatic updates.

Android 7.0 (API 24) or newer. Clock widgets and silent background updates through Obtainium need Android 12 or newer.

Releases are signed with this fork's own key and cannot be installed over the Play Store build; uninstall that first.

## Support

Report bugs and requests in this repository's [issues](https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional/issues). Do not contact the original author about this fork.

## Differences from upstream

- No Google Play licensing, update or rating prompts.
- No links to upstream chat groups, developer profiles or other apps.
- UI is being migrated to Jetpack Compose (see below).
- Release builds come from CI on every push to `master`.
- Legal pages rewritten for this fork; no license checks, analytics or crash reporting.
- Dead code, unused resources and source comments removed; unit tests for menu and dialog state, panel catalog, panel settings (map rotation, bloom, needle and pin fallbacks), location pin colours, map preference bindings, series statistics and sample windows, fix latency, location sharing, math, utilities, launcher icons, settings labels, accent palette, coordinate input, help pages, arts catalog, address search, time zone catalog, trail name rules, search result encoding, sun and moon refresh distance, settings theme and corner values, location provider choice, map source choice, Google map type and style, OpenStreetMap style and geometry, tracking pins, map animation interpolation, delayed map tasks, measure storage, widget day and night changes, needle physics, tilt, clock motion, sensor accuracy, twilight times, geodesic distance and path length, distance units, the speedometer dial and a check that every drawable drawn by Compose is a vector or bitmap.

Every map (GPS, trail and measure panels and address search) can show either Google Maps or OpenStreetMap (Settings > Map source). Automatic uses OpenStreetMap when the build has no Maps API key and Google Maps otherwise. The SDK gives no signal when a key is rejected or over quota, so if the Google map stays blank pick OpenStreetMap. OpenStreetMap is drawn with MapLibre Native from `tile.openstreetmap.org`, keeps up to 128 MB of tiles in MapLibre's cache, and darkens tiles in night mode. Google-only options (satellite, labels, traffic, buildings, high contrast, geodesic trails) have no effect on it; traffic is drawn only on the GPS map. With compass rotation the map follows your position only when the pin leaves the screen, so the heading keeps turning the map. With OpenStreetMap the GPS panel only asks for Google Play services when the fused location provider is selected. Location uses Google Play services or the Android provider as set in Settings. Both are cold flows (`LocationSource`) collected by `LocationViewModel` only while a panel that shows location is started (they stop 5 s after the last one stops, so the level and settings panels and the background use no location), request one fix per second, and fall back from GPS to network to passive as providers are switched; with approximate location only, the Android provider uses the network. The last position, used by widgets and as a map starting point, is saved only after moving 50 m.

## Compose migration

New UI is written in Jetpack Compose (Material 3, Compose BOM) and hosted in `ComponentActivity` subclasses through `setPositionalContent`; no AppCompat, Material Components or fragments are used. Panels are `PanelController` classes (no fragments): each owns a child lifecycle that follows the activity while the panel is shown and is destroyed when another panel is picked, so sensors, maps, location observers and coroutines stop with it. Shared theme, components, dialogs and screens live in `ui/compose/`. A panel shows a dialog by putting a `PanelDialog` value in its `DialogHost` and draws it in `DialogContent`. View models expose `StateFlow` (no `LiveData`); preferences reach panels as view model `StateFlow`s built with `preference`, which panels apply with `bind` while started; map views follow their own settings through `MapPreferences` flows collected while resumed (in a child scope cancelled on pause that also runs their delayed tasks), applying only values that changed (including changes made while paused), so no panel, view model or map view registers a preference listener. Text a panel shows (rows, summaries, addresses) is built by its view model, created by `PanelController.panelModel` with the shared `LocationViewModel`, and collected by the screen with `collectAsStateWithLifecycle`; panel controllers keep only sensors, maps, needle animation, preference toggles and actions, and use `PanelController.observe` for map updates. View model flows stop 5 s after the last screen stops collecting, so location, the GPS target summary and address lookups run only while a panel that shows them is visible. Saved lists and the selected measure observe Room through `Flow` and write with `NonCancellable` so edits survive closing a screen. Sensor panels register sensors only while resumed (at game rate, not fastest), animate needles only until they settle, and pause the clock with the frame clock when the app is in the background. Map panels share `MapPanelScaffold` (toolbar, tools, bottom sheet handle, landscape side column) and the map views (`GoogleMapView` and `OsmMapView` bases) follow the panel lifecycle and share pin, accuracy circle, line, heading sensor and camera code; each trail or measure is one line updated in place; the GPS panel reverse geocodes with the non-blocking `Geocoder` API on Android 13+ and only when the position moved at least 25 m.

Foreground service, notification and battery optimization prompts removed. Text without an explicit style no longer inherits a fixed line height, so large wrapped headings do not overlap | Done |
| 10a | Map source setting (automatic, Google Maps, OpenStreetMap); GPS panel on MapLibre with OpenStreetMap tiles, tile cache, pin, accuracy circle, target line, follow, compass and bearing rotation behind a `GpsMap` interface; pin bitmaps shared by both maps; APK limited to arm64-v8a, armeabi-v7a and x86_64 | Done |
| 10b | Trail and measure panels on OpenStreetMap through `TrailMap` and `MeasureMap` interfaces on a shared `OsmMapView` base (camera, heading sensor, pin, accuracy circle, lines, trail markers, measure guide line, wrap to fit); trail auto center stops while paused instead of re-posting forever; tracking pins shared with the Google maps; unused measure label overlay removed | Done |
| 10c | Address search map on OpenStreetMap through a `PickerMap` interface; map views share an `OsmMapView` base and a `MapHost` lifecycle contract; Google Play services prompt only when Google Maps or the fused provider is in use | Done |
| 11 | All code in Kotlin: Room entities keep their column names and nullability so existing databases open unchanged (CI fails if an exported schema hash changes); map callbacks trimmed to the events panels use; Google marker, circle and line animations as extensions on shared interpolation (accuracy circles no longer wrap radii above 360 m like angles); stale schema exports removed | Done |
| 12 | View models on `StateFlow`; `lifecycle-livedata` removed; Google GPS, trail, measure and picker maps on a shared `GoogleMapView` base mirroring OpenStreetMap; target start point stored when a target is set instead of on every fix; map bearing not saved on every compass frame; Google trail auto center stops while paused | Done |
| 13 | Location providers as lifecycle-bound flows (`AndroidLocationSource`, `FusedLocationSource`) replacing the started services and `LocationBus`; fused updates every second instead of every 100 ms; the Android provider registers once instead of re-registering every second and switches provider on `PROVIDERS_CHANGED`; approximate location permission is honoured; last position saved in one write after moving 50 m instead of three writes per fix | Done |
| 14a | Panel dialogs as state (`PanelDialog` values rendered by each panel) instead of stored composables; map search result returned through the previous back stack entry's `SavedStateHandle`, so it survives configuration changes; sub screens collect with `collectAsStateWithLifecycle`; clock second hand drawn at full size again; empty trail art scaled to width | Done |
| 14b | Panel info in view models (`ClockViewModel`, `CompassViewModel`, `DirectionViewModel`, `GpsViewModel`, trail and measure summaries) built on `Dispatchers.Default` and collected with `collectAsStateWithLifecycle`, so it survives rotation and panel switches; GPS address lookup moved into the view model; sun and moon report recomputed once a minute or after moving 1 km instead of on every fix; the clock no longer turns on location with a custom location; view models react to unit, coordinate format and clock format changes through `SharedPreferences.changes`; localized context cached per language | Done |
| 15 | Settings and level panel state in view models (`SettingsViewModel`, `LevelViewModel`) built from `SharedPreferences.changes` and collected with `collectAsStateWithLifecycle`; preference writes moved out of the panel controllers, which keep only permission requests, activity recreation, window flags and navigation; the settings screen no longer rebuilds its state (and queries every launcher alias) on unrelated preference changes | Done |
| 16 | Compass, clock, direction, GPS, trail and measure panel settings as view model `StateFlow`s built with `preference` (`SharedPreferences.watch`, only distinct values); panels apply them with `PanelController.bind` while started; panels and view models no longer register preference listeners; clock time zone, custom location, location provider, GPS target, selected trail and measure follow preferences through flows; trail map clear removed (switching trails redraws from the points flow); duplicate accent recreate in the settings panel removed | Done |
| 17 | Map view preference listeners (Google and OpenStreetMap bases) replaced by `MapPreferences` flows bound to the map lifecycle: look, compass rotation, pin, bearing, target, auto center, geodesic and wrap settings react only to their own keys (the OpenStreetMap GPS map no longer redraws the target line on every preference write); unused north only preference removed; location pin colours as constants so panel settings load in JVM tests | Done |
| 18 | Map view timers (heading sensor delay, trail auto center) as `DelayedTask` coroutines in a scope that lives only while the map is resumed, so pausing cancels them together with the preference flows; `Handler` runnables and the unused pause hook removed | Done |
| 19 | Sensor display rotation through `ContextCompat.getDisplayOrDefault` instead of the deprecated `WindowManager.defaultDisplay`; remaining deprecation suppressions reviewed | Next |

## Building

Requirements: JDK 21, Android SDK (compile and target SDK 37), Android Gradle Plugin 9.2+, Kotlin 2.4 with the Compose compiler plugin.

```sh
./gradlew testDebugUnitTest lintVitalRelease assembleDebug
```

Optional `local.properties` keys (environment variables of the same name take precedence):

| Key | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `MAPS_API_KEY` | Google Maps API key |
| `KEYSTORE_FILE` | Path to the release keystore |
| `STORE_PASSWORD`, `KEY_ALIAS`, `KEY_PASSWORD` | Release signing |
| `VERSION_CODE`, `VERSION_NAME` | Override the app version |

`./gradlew assembleRelease` produces an unsigned APK when no keystore is configured.

## CI and releases

`.github/workflows/build.yml` runs on every push and pull request:

1. Unit tests, release lint checks (the same fatal checks that gate `assembleRelease`) and a debug build; the build fails if an exported Room schema identity hash changed.
2. On pushes to `master` only: a signed release APK, published as a full GitHub release tagged `v1.0.<run number>`.

Required secrets: `KEYSTORE_BASE64`, `STORE_PASSWORD`, `KEY_PASSWORD`, `KEY_ALIAS`. Optional: `MAPS_API_KEY`.

```sh
base64 -w0 release.keystore
```

Dependabot opens weekly update PRs for Gradle dependencies, the Gradle wrapper and GitHub Actions. If a PR fails at `checkDebugAarMetadata`, raise `compileSdk` first.

## Project layout

```
app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/
  activities/   launcher and main activity
  ui/navigation/ routes and sub screen destinations
  constants/    static catalogs (help pages, pins, launcher art)
  ui/compose/   Compose theme, shared components, dialogs and screens
  sensors/      lifecycle-aware gravity and heading sensors
  ui/panels/    panel controllers
  extensions/   panel controller base, sensor panel, map callbacks
  decorations/  Google Maps and OpenStreetMap views, marker helpers
  database/     Room entities, DAOs and databases
  location/     location sources as lifecycle-bound flows
  widgets/      app widget providers
  math/         vectors, angles, rotation physics, distances, unit conversion
  util/         formatting and Android helpers
app/src/test/   JVM unit tests
docs/llm/       LLM context files (tracked, excluded from the dump)
```

`export.sh` writes `docs/llm/dump.txt` for LLM context: every tracked file except `docs/llm/`, with contents of source, build and config files only.

## Credits and license

Based on Positional by Hamza Rizwan. Licensed under the [GNU GPL v3](LICENSE). See [PRIVACY.md](PRIVACY.md).

apply plugin: 'com.android.application'
apply plugin: 'com.google.devtools.ksp'
apply plugin: 'org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.compose'

def localProperties = new Properties()
def localPropertiesFile = rootProject.file('local.properties')
if (localPropertiesFile.exists()) {
    localPropertiesFile.withInputStream { localProperties.load(it) }
}

def env = { String key -> System.getenv(key) ?: localProperties.getProperty(key) }
def mapsApiKey = env('MAPS_API_KEY') ?: ''
def keystorePath = env('KEYSTORE_FILE')
def hasReleaseKey = keystorePath && file(keystorePath).exists()

android {
    namespace 'app.simple.positional'
    compileSdk = 37

    defaultConfig {
        applicationId 'app.simple.positional'
        minSdk = 24
        targetSdk = 37
        versionCode = (env('VERSION_CODE') ?: '20260512') as int
        versionName = env('VERSION_NAME') ?: 'v2026.05.12'
        testInstrumentationRunner 'androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner'
        vectorDrawables.useSupportLibrary = true
        manifestPlaceholders = [MAPS_API_KEY: mapsApiKey]
        resValue 'string', 'versionName', versionName
        buildConfigField 'boolean', 'HAS_MAPS_KEY', mapsApiKey.isEmpty() ? 'false' : 'true'

        ndk {
            abiFilters 'arm64-v8a', 'armeabi-v7a', 'x86_64'
        }

        ksp {
            arg('room.schemaLocation', "$projectDir/databases/schemas")
        }
    }

    signingConfigs {
        if (hasReleaseKey) {
            release {
                storeFile file(keystorePath)
                storePassword env('STORE_PASSWORD')
                keyAlias env('KEY_ALIAS')
                keyPassword env('KEY_PASSWORD')
            }
        }
    }

    buildTypes {
        release {
            minifyEnabled true
            shrinkResources true
            proguardFiles getDefaultProguardFile('proguard-android-optimize.txt'), 'proguard-rules.pro'
            if (hasReleaseKey) {
                signingConfig signingConfigs.release
            }
        }
        debug {
            applicationIdSuffix '.debug'
            versionNameSuffix '_debug'
        }
    }

    dependenciesInfo {
        includeInApk = false
        includeInBundle = false
    }

    compileOptions {
        coreLibraryDesugaringEnabled true
        sourceCompatibility JavaVersion.VERSION_21
        targetCompatibility JavaVersion.VERSION_21
    }

    kotlin {
        compilerOptions {
            jvmTarget = 'JVM_21'
        }
    }

    buildFeatures {
        buildConfig true
        compose true
        resValues true
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/activities/main/LauncherActivity.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.activities.main

import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Bundle
import app.simple.positional.extensions.activity.BaseActivity
import app.simple.positional.preferences.FragmentPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelCatalog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SplashScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.setPositionalContent

class LauncherActivity : BaseActivity() {
    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setShortcutScreen()
        if (MainPreferences.getSkipSplashScreen()) {
            openMain()
        } else {
            setPositionalContent { SplashScreen(::openMain) }
        }
    }

    private fun openMain() {
        if (isFinishing || isDestroyed) return
        startActivity(Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).setFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP).setAction(intent.action))
        finish()
    }

    private fun setShortcutScreen() {
        when (intent.action) {
            "open_clock" -> FragmentPreferences.setCurrentTag(PanelCatalog.CLOCK)
            "open_compass" -> FragmentPreferences.setCurrentTag(PanelCatalog.COMPASS)
            "action_map_panel_full", "open_gps" -> FragmentPreferences.setCurrentTag(PanelCatalog.LOCATION)
            "open_level" -> FragmentPreferences.setCurrentTag(PanelCatalog.LEVEL)
        }
    }
}

import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.TimeZoneRoute
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.TrailsRoute
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.WebRoute
import app.simple.positional.callbacks.BottomBarHost
import app.simple.positional.extensions.activity.BaseActivity
import app.simple.positional.preferences.FragmentPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelCatalog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelSwitcher
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.setPositionalContent
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.Compass
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.Direction
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.GPS
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.Level
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.Measure
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.Settings
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.Time
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.Trail
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension.getLocationStatus
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationPrompt.displayLocationSettingsRequest
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.LocationViewModel
import com.google.android.gms.maps.MapsInitializer

class MainActivity : BaseActivity(), BottomBarHost, AppNavigator {
    private var panel by mutableStateOf(PanelCatalog.LOCATION)
    private var barVisible by mutableStateOf(true)
    private var navController: NavHostController? = null
    private val location: LocationViewModel by viewModels()

    private val permissions = registerForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()) { result ->
        if (result.values.any { it }) {
            showPrompt()
            location.restart()
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(this, R.string.no_location_permission_alert, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        MapsInitializer.initialize(applicationContext, MapsInitializer.Renderer.LATEST) { }
        panel = PanelCatalog.resolve(FragmentPreferences.getCurrentTag())
        setPositionalContent { topInset -> AppNavHost(topInset) }
        checkLocationPermission()
        MainPreferences.setLaunchCount(MainPreferences.getLaunchCount() + 1)
    }

    private fun select(tag: String) {
        panel = tag
        barVisible = true
        FragmentPreferences.setCurrentTag(tag)
    }

    @Composable
    private fun AppNavHost(topInset: Dp) {
        val nav = rememberNavController()
        navController = nav
        val controller = remember(panel) { createPanel(panel, this) }
        DisposableEffect(controller) { onDispose { controller.destroy() } }
        NavHost(nav, startDestination = Routes.PANELS, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(colorResource(R.color.mainBackground))) {
            composable(Routes.PANELS) { entry ->
                SearchResultEffect(entry, controller::onSearchResult)
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    controller.Host(topInset)
                    PanelSwitcher(panel, barVisible, ::select, Modifier.align(Alignment.BottomEnd))
                }
            }
            composable(Routes.ACCENT) { AccentRoute(this@MainActivity, topInset) }
            composable(Routes.ARTS) { ArtsRoute() }
            composable(Routes.TIMEZONE) { TimeZoneRoute(this@MainActivity, topInset) }
            composable(Routes.MEASURES) { MeasuresRoute(this@MainActivity, topInset) }
            composable(Routes.TRAILS) { TrailsRoute(this@MainActivity, topInset) }
            composable(Routes.DIRECTIONS) { DirectionsRoute(this@MainActivity, it, topInset) }
            composable(Routes.CUSTOM_LOCATIONS) { CustomLocationsRoute(this@MainActivity, it, topInset) }
            composable(Routes.MAP_SEARCH) { MapSearchRoute(this@MainActivity) }
            composable(Routes.WEB, arguments = listOf(navArgument(Routes.PAGE_ARGUMENT) { type = NavType.StringType })) { entry ->
                WebRoute(entry.arguments?.getString(Routes.PAGE_ARGUMENT), this@MainActivity)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun navigate(route: String) {
        navController?.navigate(route) { launchSingleTop = true }
    }

    override fun back() {
        navController?.popBackStack()
    }

    override fun deliver(result: SearchResult) {
        val nav = navController ?: return
        nav.previousBackStackEntry?.savedStateHandle?.set(Routes.SEARCH_RESULT, result.encode())
        nav.popBackStack()
    }

    override fun showBottomBar(visible: Boolean) {
        barVisible = visible
    }

    private fun hasLocationPermission() = locationPermissions.any {
        ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, it) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
    }

    private fun checkLocationPermission() {
        when {
            hasLocationPermission() -> showPrompt()
            MainPreferences.getShowPermissionDialog() -> permissions.launch(locationPermissions)
            else -> Toast.makeText(this, R.string.location_permission_denied, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    private fun showPrompt() {
        if (!getLocationStatus(this)) displayLocationSettingsRequest(this)
    }

    private companion object {
        val locationPermissions = arrayOf(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)
    }
}

private fun createPanel(tag: String, activity: ComponentActivity): PanelController = when (tag) {
    PanelCatalog.CLOCK -> Time(activity)
    PanelCatalog.COMPASS -> Compass(activity)
    PanelCatalog.DIRECTION -> Direction(activity)
    PanelCatalog.TRAIL -> Trail(activity)
    PanelCatalog.MEASURE -> Measure(activity)
    PanelCatalog.LEVEL -> Level(activity)
    PanelCatalog.SETTINGS -> Settings(activity)
    else -> GPS(activity)
}

package app.simple.positional.constants

import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import app.simple.positional.R

enum class HtmlPage(val asset: String, @param:StringRes val title: Int? = null) {
    DISCLAIMER("disclaimer", R.string.disclaimer),
    PRIVACY_POLICY("privacy_policy", R.string.privacy_policy),
    TERMS("terms_and_conditions", R.string.terms_of_use),
    PERMISSIONS("required_permissions", R.string.permissions),
    CREDITS("credits", R.string.credits),
    INTERNET_USES("internet_uses", R.string.internet_uses),
    PHYSICAL_PROPERTIES("physical_properties"),
    CUSTOM_COORDINATES_HELP("custom_coordinates_help"),
    MEDIA_KEYS("media_keys"),
    TRAIL_HELP("trail_help");

    val url: String get() = "file:///android_asset/html/$asset.html"

    companion object {
        fun forTitle(@StringRes title: Int): HtmlPage? = entries.firstOrNull { it.title == title }

        fun forName(name: String?): HtmlPage? = entries.firstOrNull { it.name == name }
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/constants/LocationPins.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.constants

import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences

object LocationPins {
    private const val FILL_ALPHA = 0x4D000000
    private const val OPAQUE = 0xFF000000.toInt()

    val locationsPins = intArrayOf(
        R.drawable.ic_pin_01,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_02,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_03,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_04,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_05,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_06,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_07,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_08,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_09,
        R.drawable.ic_pin_10,
    )

    private val circleColors = intArrayOf(
        0x1B9CFF,
        0x5F0B95,
        0xFF8859,
        0x4C9ACC,
        0xED765E,
        0xEB2A2E,
        0x943B5E,
        0x8B3B94,
        0xF38630,
        0xFF695B,
    )

    fun pin(skin: Int): Int = locationsPins.getOrElse(skin) { locationsPins[0] }

    fun fillColor(skin: Int): Int = rgb(skin) or FILL_ALPHA

    fun strokeColor(skin: Int): Int = rgb(skin) or OPAQUE

    private fun rgb(skin: Int): Int = circleColors.getOrElse(skin) { circleColors[0] }

    fun getLocationPin(): Int = pin(GPSPreferences.getPinSkin())

    fun getCircleFillColor(): Int = fillColor(GPSPreferences.getPinSkin())

    fun getCircleStrokeColor(): Int = strokeColor(GPSPreferences.getPinSkin())
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/database/converters/MeasurePointConverter.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.database.converters

import androidx.room.TypeConverter
import app.simple.positional.model.MeasurePoint

class MeasurePointConverter {
    @TypeConverter
    fun fromMeasurePoints(points: ArrayList<MeasurePoint>?): String? =
        points?.joinToString("") { it.toDatabase() + ";" }

    @TypeConverter
    fun toMeasurePoints(data: String?): ArrayList<MeasurePoint>? =
        data?.split(';')?.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }?.mapTo(ArrayList()) { MeasurePoint.fromDatabase(it) }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/database/dao/DirectionDao.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.database.dao

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Delete
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import app.simple.positional.model.DirectionModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Dao
interface DirectionDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM directions ORDER BY date_added DESC LIMIT 999")
    fun observeAll(): Flow<List<DirectionModel>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertDirection(direction: DirectionModel)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteDirection(direction: DirectionModel)
}

package app.simple.positional.database.instances

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import app.simple.positional.database.dao.DirectionDao
import app.simple.positional.model.DirectionModel

@Database(entities = [DirectionModel::class], exportSchema = true, version = 1)
abstract class DirectionDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun directionDao(): DirectionDao

    companion object {
        @Volatile
        private var instance: DirectionDatabase? = null

        fun getInstance(context: Context): DirectionDatabase = instance ?: synchronized(this) {
            instance ?: Room.databaseBuilder(context.applicationContext, DirectionDatabase::class.java, "directions")
                .fallbackToDestructiveMigration(dropAllTables = true)
                .build()
                .also { instance = it }
        }
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/database/instances/TrailPointDatabase.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.database.instances

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import app.simple.positional.database.dao.TrailPointDao
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailPoint

@Database(entities = [TrailPoint::class], exportSchema = true, version = 3)
abstract class TrailPointDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun trailDataDao(): TrailPointDao

    companion object {
        private var name: String? = null
        private var instance: TrailPointDatabase? = null

        @Synchronized
        fun getInstance(context: Context, trail: String): TrailPointDatabase {
            instance?.takeIf { name == trail }?.let { return it }
            instance?.close()
            return Room.databaseBuilder(context.applicationContext, TrailPointDatabase::class.java, "$trail.db")
                .fallbackToDestructiveMigration(dropAllTables = true)
                .build()
                .also {
                    instance = it
                    name = trail
                }
        }

        @Synchronized
        fun close(trail: String) {
            if (name != trail) return
            instance?.close()
            instance = null
            name = null
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/GoogleMapView.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import android.animation.ValueAnimator
import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.content.res.Configuration
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.location.Location
import android.view.View
import androidx.annotation.ColorInt
import androidx.annotation.DrawableRes
import androidx.annotation.RawRes
import androidx.lifecycle.DefaultLifecycleObserver
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.decorations.utils.DelayedTask
import app.simple.positional.decorations.utils.animateColors
import app.simple.positional.decorations.utils.animateTo
import app.simple.positional.decorations.utils.child
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapsCallbacks
import app.simple.positional.sensors.HeadingSensor
import app.simple.positional.util.BitmapHelper.toBitmap
import app.simple.positional.util.ColorUtils.resolveAttrColor
import com.google.android.gms.maps.CameraUpdateFactory
import com.google.android.gms.maps.GoogleMap
import com.google.android.gms.maps.MapView
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.BitmapDescriptorFactory
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.CameraPosition
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Circle
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.CircleOptions
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.CustomCap
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.JointType
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLngBounds
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.MapStyleOptions
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Marker
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.MarkerOptions
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.PatternItem
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Polyline
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.PolylineOptions
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

data class MapLook(val label: Boolean, val satellite: Boolean, val highContrast: Boolean, val buildings: Boolean, val traffic: Boolean)

object GoogleMapStyle {
    fun type(satellite: Boolean, label: Boolean): Int = when {
        !satellite -> GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_NORMAL
        label -> GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_HYBRID
        else -> GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_SATELLITE
    }

    @RawRes
    fun raw(label: Boolean, highContrast: Boolean, night: Boolean): Int = when {
        highContrast -> if (label) R.raw.map_high_contrast_labelled else R.raw.map_high_contrast_non_labelled
        night -> if (label) R.raw.maps_dark_labelled else R.raw.maps_dark_no_label
        else -> if (label) R.raw.maps_light_labelled else R.raw.maps_light_no_label
    }
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
abstract class GoogleMapView(context: Context) : MapView(context), MapHost, HeadingSensor.Listener {
    override val view: View get() = this
    protected var map: GoogleMap? = null
    protected var mapCallbacks: MapsCallbacks? = null
    protected var location: Location? = null
    protected var heading = 0f
    protected var headingAccuracy = -1
    protected var animating = false
    protected var resumedScope: CoroutineScope? = null
        private set
    protected val accent = context.resolveAttrColor(R.attr.colorAppAccent)
    private var owner: LifecycleOwner? = null
    private var sensorOn = false
    private val headingSensor = HeadingSensor(context, this)
    private val registerTask = DelayedTask(SENSOR_DELAY, ::registerHeading)
    private var pin: Marker? = null
    private var pinKey: Any? = null
    private var circle: Circle? = null
    private var pinAnimator: ValueAnimator? = null
    private var circleAnimator: ValueAnimator? = null
    private var colorAnimator: ValueAnimator? = null
    private val startCap by lazy { cap(R.drawable.ic_trail_start) }
    private val endCap by lazy { cap(R.drawable.seekbar_thumb) }
    private val preferences by lazy { MapPreferences().also(::bindPreferences) }

    protected abstract val compassRotation: Boolean
    protected open val lookKeys: Set<String> = emptySet()
    protected open val compassKey: String? = null
    protected abstract fun look(): MapLook
    protected abstract fun startCamera(): CameraPosition
    protected abstract fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition)
    protected open fun onReady() {}
    protected open fun onClick(latLng: LatLng) {
        mapCallbacks?.onMapClicked()
    }
    protected open fun onGesture() {}
    protected open fun onCameraMoved() {}
    protected open fun onHeadingChanged() {}
    protected open fun onHeadingAccuracyChanged() {}
    protected open fun onResumed() {}
    protected open fun onCompassRotation(enabled: Boolean) {}

    protected open fun bindPreferences(preferences: MapPreferences) {
        if (lookKeys.isNotEmpty()) preferences.watch(*lookKeys.toTypedArray(), read = ::look, apply = ::applyLook)
        compassKey?.let { key ->
            preferences.watch(key, read = { compassRotation }) { enabled ->
                if (enabled) registerHeadingLater() else unregisterHeading()
                onCompassRotation(enabled)
            }
        }
    }

    override val lifecycleObserver = object : DefaultLifecycleObserver {
        override fun onStart(owner: LifecycleOwner) = this@GoogleMapView.onStart()

        override fun onResume(owner: LifecycleOwner) {
            this@GoogleMapView.owner = owner
            this@GoogleMapView.onResume()
            resumedScope = owner.lifecycleScope.child().also { preferences.launchIn(it) }
            registerHeadingLater()
            onResumed()
        }

        override fun onPause(owner: LifecycleOwner) {
            unregisterHeading()
            resumedScope?.cancel()
            resumedScope = null
            this@GoogleMapView.onPause()
        }

        override fun onStop(owner: LifecycleOwner) = this@GoogleMapView.onStop()

        override fun onDestroy(owner: LifecycleOwner) {
            listOf(pinAnimator, circleAnimator, colorAnimator).forEach { it?.cancel() }
            this@GoogleMapView.owner = null
            this@GoogleMapView.onDestroy()
        }
    }

    init {
        alpha = 0f
        onCreate(null)
        getMapAsync { ready(it) }
    }

    private fun ready(map: GoogleMap) {
        this.map = map
        map.uiSettings.isCompassEnabled = false
        map.uiSettings.isMapToolbarEnabled = false
        map.uiSettings.isMyLocationButtonEnabled = false
        applyLook(look())
        map.moveCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newCameraPosition(startCamera()))
        map.setOnMapClickListener { onClick(it) }
        map.setOnMapLongClickListener { mapCallbacks?.onMapLongClicked(it) }
        map.setOnCameraMoveStartedListener { reason ->
            if (reason == GoogleMap.OnCameraMoveStartedListener.REASON_GESTURE) {
                animating = false
                onGesture()
            }
        }
        map.setOnCameraMoveListener { onCameraMoved() }
        map.setOnCameraIdleListener { onCameraIdle(map.cameraPosition) }
        onReady()
        animate().alpha(1f).setDuration(FADE).start()
    }

    private fun applyLook(look: MapLook) {
        val map = map ?: return
        val night = (resources.configuration.uiMode and Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK) == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES
        map.mapType = GoogleMapStyle.type(look.satellite, look.label)
        runCatching { map.setMapStyle(MapStyleOptions.loadRawResourceStyle(context, GoogleMapStyle.raw(look.label, look.highContrast, night))) }
        map.isBuildingsEnabled = look.buildings
        map.isTrafficEnabled = look.traffic
    }

    override fun onHeading(azimuth: Float) {
        heading = azimuth
        onHeadingChanged()
    }

    override fun onAccuracy(type: Int, accuracy: Int) {
        if (headingAccuracy == accuracy) return
        headingAccuracy = accuracy
        onHeadingAccuracyChanged()
    }

    protected fun registerHeadingLater() {
        registerTask.schedule(resumedScope)
    }

    protected fun unregisterHeading() {
        registerTask.cancel()
        if (sensorOn) owner?.let(headingSensor::onPause)
        sensorOn = false
    }

    private fun registerHeading() {
        val current = owner ?: return
        if (sensorOn || !compassRotation || !headingSensor.isAvailable) return
        headingSensor.onResume(current)
        sensorOn = true
    }

    protected fun drawPin(key: Any, position: LatLng, centered: Boolean, rotation: Float?, bitmap: () -> Bitmap) {
        val map = map ?: return
        val anchor = if (centered) 0.5f else 1f
        val marker = pin
        if (marker == null) {
            pin = map.addMarker(
                MarkerOptions()
                    .position(position)
                    .anchor(0.5f, anchor)
                    .flat(centered)
                    .rotation(rotation ?: heading)
                    .icon(BitmapDescriptorFactory.fromBitmap(bitmap()))
            )
            pinKey = key
            return
        }
        if (pinKey != key) {
            marker.setIcon(BitmapDescriptorFactory.fromBitmap(bitmap()))
            pinKey = key
        }
        marker.setAnchor(0.5f, anchor)
        marker.isFlat = centered
        pinAnimator?.cancel()
        pinAnimator = marker.animateTo(position, rotation)
    }

    protected fun rotatePin(rotation: Float) {
        pin?.rotation = rotation
    }

    protected fun invalidatePin() {
        pinKey = null
    }

    protected fun drawAccuracy(position: LatLng, radius: Double, @ColorInt fill: Int, @ColorInt stroke: Int) {
        val map = map ?: return
        val current = circle
        if (current == null) {
            circle = map.addCircle(
                CircleOptions().center(position).radius(radius).fillColor(fill).strokeColor(stroke).strokeWidth(CIRCLE_STROKE).clickable(false)
            )
            return
        }
        circleAnimator?.cancel()
        circleAnimator = current.animateTo(position, radius)
        if (current.fillColor != fill || current.strokeColor != stroke) {
            colorAnimator?.cancel()
            colorAnimator = current.animateColors(fill, stroke)
        }
    }

    protected fun line(
        points: List<LatLng>,
        @ColorInt color: Int = accent,
        width: Float = LINE_WIDTH,
        geodesic: Boolean = false,
        pattern: List<PatternItem>? = null,
    ): Polyline? {
        val map = map ?: return null
        val options = PolylineOptions()
            .addAll(points)
            .width(width)
            .color(color)
            .jointType(JointType.ROUND)
            .geodesic(geodesic)
            .startCap(startCap)
            .endCap(endCap)
        if (pattern != null) options.pattern(pattern)
        return map.addPolyline(options)
    }

    private fun cap(@DrawableRes drawable: Int) = CustomCap(BitmapDescriptorFactory.fromBitmap(drawable.toBitmap(context, CAP_SIZE)))

    protected fun target(): LatLng? = map?.cameraPosition?.target

    protected fun isVisible(position: LatLng): Boolean = map?.projection?.visibleRegion?.latLngBounds?.contains(position) ?: true

    protected fun camera(target: LatLng, zoom: Float, tilt: Float, bearing: Float) = CameraPosition(target, zoom, tilt, bearing)

    protected fun moveCamera(position: CameraPosition) {
        map?.moveCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newCameraPosition(position))
    }

    protected fun animateTo(position: CameraPosition) {
        val map = map ?: return
        animating = true
        map.animateCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newCameraPosition(position), CAMERA_SPEED, object : GoogleMap.CancelableCallback {
            override fun onCancel() {
                animating = false
            }

            override fun onFinish() {
                animating = false
            }
        })
    }

    protected fun fit(points: List<LatLng>, animate: Boolean): Boolean {
        val map = map ?: return false
        if (points.size < 2) return false
        val bounds = LatLngBounds.builder().apply { points.forEach { include(it) } }.build()
        val update = CameraUpdateFactory.newLatLngBounds(bounds, (FIT_PADDING * resources.displayMetrics.density).roundToInt())
        return runCatching { if (animate) map.animateCamera(update) else map.moveCamera(update) }.isSuccess
    }

    protected fun zoom(zoomIn: Boolean) {
        map?.animateCamera(if (zoomIn) CameraUpdateFactory.zoomIn() else CameraUpdateFactory.zoomOut())
    }

    protected fun rotate(bearing: Float, animate: Boolean) {
        val map = map ?: return
        val update = CameraUpdateFactory.newCameraPosition(CameraPosition.Builder(map.cameraPosition).bearing(bearing).build())
        if (animate) map.animateCamera(update) else map.moveCamera(update)
    }

    protected companion object {
        const val CAMERA_SPEED = 1000
        const val SENSOR_DELAY = 500L
        const val FIT_PADDING = 64
        const val FADE = 300L
        const val LINE_WIDTH = 10f
        const val CIRCLE_STROKE = 3f
        const val CAP_SIZE = 30
    }
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
abstract class GooglePanelMap(context: Context) : GoogleMapView(context), PanelMap {
    override fun setCallbacks(callbacks: MapsCallbacks) {
        mapCallbacks = callbacks
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/GpsMap.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Point
import android.location.Location
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.lifecycle.DefaultLifecycleObserver
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapsCallbacks
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.BitmapHelper.addLinearGradient
import app.simple.positional.util.BitmapHelper.toBitmapKeepingSize
import app.simple.positional.util.MapSource
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.CameraPosition
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Polyline

interface MapHost {
    val view: View
    val lifecycleObserver: DefaultLifecycleObserver
}

interface PanelMap : MapHost {
    fun setCallbacks(callbacks: MapsCallbacks)
    fun updateLocation(location: Location)
    fun resetCamera(zoom: Float)
}

interface GpsMap : PanelMap {
    var isMapMovementEnabled: Boolean
    var onTouch: ((MotionEvent) -> Unit)?
    fun screenPoint(latLng: LatLng): Point?
    fun setTargetMarker(latLng: LatLng?)
    fun moveCameraToTarget()
    fun zoomIn()
    fun zoomOut()
    fun registerWithRunnable()
    fun unregister()

    companion object {
        fun create(context: Context): GpsMap = if (MapSource.isOsm()) OsmGpsMap(context) else GoogleGpsMap(context)
    }
}

object GpsPin {
    private val customGradient = intArrayOf(0xFFFF1B50.toInt(), 0xFFE11677.toInt())

    fun bitmap(context: Context, custom: Boolean, moving: Boolean): Bitmap {
        val icon = if (moving && !custom) R.drawable.ic_bearing_marker else LocationPins.getLocationPin()
        val bitmap = icon.toBitmapKeepingSize(context, GPSPreferences.getPinSize(), GPSPreferences.getPinOpacity())
        return if (custom) bitmap.addLinearGradient(customGradient) else bitmap
    }
}

object GpsMapPreferences {
    private val targetKeys = arrayOf(
        GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarker,
        GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarkerLatitude,
        GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarkerLongitude,
        GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarkerStartLatitude,
        GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarkerStartLongitude,
    )

    fun bind(preferences: MapPreferences, onPin: () -> Unit, onBearing: () -> Unit, onTarget: () -> Unit) {
        preferences.watch(GPSPreferences.pinSkin, GPSPreferences.pinSize, GPSPreferences.pinOpacity, read = ::pinStyle) { onPin() }
        preferences.watch(GPSPreferences.useBearingRotation, read = GPSPreferences::isBearingRotation) { onBearing() }
        preferences.watch(*targetKeys, read = ::targetState) { onTarget() }
    }

    private fun pinStyle() = Triple(GPSPreferences.getPinSkin(), GPSPreferences.getPinSize(), GPSPreferences.getPinOpacity())

    private fun targetState() = Triple(
        GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet(),
        GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates().toList(),
        GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerStartCoordinates().toList(),
    )
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class GoogleGpsMap(context: Context) : GooglePanelMap(context), GpsMap {
    override var isMapMovementEnabled = true
    override var onTouch: ((MotionEvent) -> Unit)? = null
    override val compassRotation get() = GPSPreferences.isCompassRotation()
    override val compassKey get() = GPSPreferences.compassRotation
    override val lookKeys: Set<String> = setOf(
        GPSPreferences.GPSLabelMode,
        GPSPreferences.GPSSatellite,
        GPSPreferences.highContrastMap,
        GPSPreferences.showBuilding,
        GPSPreferences.trafficMode,
    )
    private val custom = MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()
    private val customPoint = MainPreferences.getCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }
    private var targetLine: Polyline? = null

    override fun look() = MapLook(
        GPSPreferences.isLabelOn(),
        GPSPreferences.isSatelliteOn(),
        GPSPreferences.getHighContrastMap(),
        GPSPreferences.getShowBuildingsOnMap(),
        GPSPreferences.isTrafficShown(),
    )

    override fun startCamera(): CameraPosition {
        val start = current() ?: MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }
        return camera(start, GPSPreferences.getMapZoom(), GPSPreferences.getMapTilt(), GPSPreferences.getMapBearing())
    }

    override fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition) {
        GPSPreferences.setMapZoom(position.zoom)
        GPSPreferences.setMapTilt(position.tilt)
        if (!compassRotation) GPSPreferences.setMapBearing(position.bearing)
    }

    override fun onGesture() {
        isMapMovementEnabled = false
    }

    override fun onReady() {
        drawPin()
        drawTarget()
    }

    override fun onHeadingChanged() {
        if (!animating) rotate(heading, animate = false)
    }

    override fun dispatchTouchEvent(ev: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        onTouch?.invoke(ev)
        return super.dispatchTouchEvent(ev)
    }

    override fun updateLocation(location: Location) {
        val first = this.location == null
        this.location = location
        drawPin()
        drawTarget()
        val point = current() ?: return
        if (first) {
            moveCamera(camera(point, GPSPreferences.getMapZoom(), GPSPreferences.getMapTilt(), bearing()))
        } else if (isMapMovementEnabled && !animating && !GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerMode() && (!compassRotation || !isVisible(point))) {
            follow(GPSPreferences.getMapZoom())
        }
    }

    override fun screenPoint(latLng: LatLng): Point? = map?.projection?.toScreenLocation(latLng)

    override fun resetCamera(zoom: Float) = follow(zoom)

    override fun setTargetMarker(latLng: LatLng?) {
        val target = latLng ?: target() ?: return
        GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerLatitude(target.latitude.toFloat())
        GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerLongitude(target.longitude.toFloat())
        GPSPreferences.setTargetMarker(true)
    }

    override fun moveCameraToTarget() {
        val target = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates()
        animateTo(camera(LatLng(target[0].toDouble(), target[1].toDouble()), GPSPreferences.getMapZoom(), GPSPreferences.getMapTilt(), bearing()))
    }

    override fun zoomIn() = zoom(zoomIn = true)

    override fun zoomOut() = zoom(zoomIn = false)

    override fun registerWithRunnable() = registerHeadingLater()

    override fun unregister() = unregisterHeading()

    private fun current(): LatLng? = if (custom) customPoint else location?.let { LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude) }

    private fun bearing(): Float = when {
        GPSPreferences.isCompassRotation() -> heading
        GPSPreferences.isBearingRotation() -> location?.bearing ?: 0f
        else -> 0f
    }

    private fun follow(zoom: Float) {
        val point = current() ?: target() ?: return
        animateTo(camera(point, zoom, GPSPreferences.getMapTilt(), bearing()))
    }

    private fun drawPin() {
        val point = current() ?: return
        val fix = location
        val moving = !custom && (fix?.speed ?: 0f) > 0f
        drawPin(moving, point, centered = moving, rotation = if (moving) fix?.bearing ?: 0f else 0f) {
            GpsPin.bitmap(context, custom, moving)
        }
        if (custom || fix == null) return
        drawAccuracy(
            point,
            if (moving) fix.speed.toDouble() else fix.accuracy.toDouble(),
            if (moving) ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.bearing_circle_color) else LocationPins.getCircleFillColor(),
            if (moving) ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.bearing_circle_stroke) else LocationPins.getCircleStrokeColor(),
        )
    }

    private fun drawTarget() {
        val current = current()
        if (!GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet() || current == null) {
            targetLine?.remove()
            targetLine = null
            return
        }
        val target = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }
        val start = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerStartCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }
        val points = if (custom) listOf(current, target) else listOf(start, current, target)
        targetLine?.let { it.points = points } ?: run { targetLine = line(points) }
    }

    override fun onCompassRotation(enabled: Boolean) = rotate(bearing(), animate = true)

    override fun bindPreferences(preferences: MapPreferences) {
        super.bindPreferences(preferences)
        GpsMapPreferences.bind(preferences, onPin = ::redrawPin, onBearing = { rotate(bearing(), animate = true) }, onTarget = ::drawTarget)
    }

    private fun redrawPin() {
        invalidatePin()
        drawPin()
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/MapPreferences.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filter
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.launchIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.merge
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.onEach

class MapPreferences {
    private val flows = mutableListOf<Flow<Any?>>()

    fun <T> add(initial: T, values: Flow<T>, apply: (T) -> Unit) {
        var applied = initial
        flows += values.filter { it != applied }.onEach {
            applied = it
            apply(it)
        }
    }

    fun <T> watch(vararg keys: String, read: () -> T, apply: (T) -> Unit) = add(read(), SharedPreferences.watch(*keys, read = read), apply)

    fun launchIn(scope: CoroutineScope): Job = flows.merge().launchIn(scope)
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/OsmGpsMap.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Point
import android.location.Location
import android.view.MotionEvent
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import org.maplibre.android.camera.CameraPosition

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class OsmGpsMap(context: Context) : OsmPanelMap(context), GpsMap {
    override var isMapMovementEnabled = true
    override var onTouch: ((MotionEvent) -> Unit)? = null
    override val compassRotation get() = GPSPreferences.isCompassRotation()
    override val compassKey get() = GPSPreferences.compassRotation
    private val custom = MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()
    private val customPoint = MainPreferences.getCoordinates().let { it[0].toDouble() to it[1].toDouble() }

    override fun startCamera(): CameraPosition {
        val start = if (custom) customPoint else MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates().let { it[0].toDouble() to it[1].toDouble() }
        return camera(start.first, start.second, GPSPreferences.getMapZoom(), GPSPreferences.getMapTilt(), GPSPreferences.getMapBearing())
    }

    override fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition) {
        GPSPreferences.setMapZoom(position.zoom.toFloat())
        GPSPreferences.setMapTilt(position.tilt.toFloat())
        if (!compassRotation) GPSPreferences.setMapBearing(position.bearing.toFloat())
    }

    override fun onGesture() {
        isMapMovementEnabled = false
    }

    override fun onStyleReady() {
        drawPin()
        drawTarget()
    }

    override fun onHeadingChanged() {
        if (!animating) rotate(heading, animate = false)
    }

    override fun dispatchTouchEvent(ev: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        onTouch?.invoke(ev)
        return super.dispatchTouchEvent(ev)
    }

    override fun updateLocation(location: Location) {
        val first = this.location == null
        this.location = location
        drawPin()
        drawTarget()
        if (first) {
            moveCamera(camera(location.latitude, location.longitude, GPSPreferences.getMapZoom(), GPSPreferences.getMapTilt(), bearing()))
        } else if (isMapMovementEnabled && !animating && !GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerMode() && (!compassRotation || current()?.let { !isVisible(it.first, it.second) } == true)) {
            follow(GPSPreferences.getMapZoom())
        }
    }

    override fun screenPoint(latLng: LatLng): Point? = screenPointOf(latLng.latitude, latLng.longitude)

    override fun resetCamera(zoom: Float) = follow(zoom)

    override fun setTargetMarker(latLng: LatLng?) {
        val target = latLng?.let { it.latitude to it.longitude } ?: target() ?: return
        GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerLatitude(target.first.toFloat())
        GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerLongitude(target.second.toFloat())
        GPSPreferences.setTargetMarker(true)
    }

    override fun moveCameraToTarget() {
        val target = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates()
        animateTo(camera(target[0].toDouble(), target[1].toDouble(), GPSPreferences.getMapZoom(), GPSPreferences.getMapTilt(), bearing()))
    }

    override fun zoomIn() = zoom(zoomIn = true)

    override fun zoomOut() = zoom(zoomIn = false)

    override fun registerWithRunnable() = registerHeadingLater()

    override fun unregister() = unregisterHeading()

    private fun current(): Pair<Double, Double>? = if (custom) customPoint else location?.let { it.latitude to it.longitude }

    private fun bearing(): Float = when {
        GPSPreferences.isCompassRotation() -> heading
        GPSPreferences.isBearingRotation() -> location?.bearing ?: 0f
        else -> 0f
    }

    private fun follow(zoom: Float) {
        val point = current() ?: target() ?: return
        animateTo(camera(point.first, point.second, zoom, GPSPreferences.getMapTilt(), bearing()))
    }

    private fun drawPin() {
        val point = current() ?: return
        val fix = location
        val moving = !custom && (fix?.speed ?: 0f) > 0f
        drawPin(moving, point.first, point.second, centered = moving, rotation = if (moving) fix?.bearing ?: 0f else 0f) {
            GpsPin.bitmap(context, custom, moving)
        }
        if (custom || fix == null) return
        drawAccuracy(
            point.first,
            point.second,
            if (moving) fix.speed.toDouble() else fix.accuracy.toDouble(),
            if (moving) ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.bearing_circle_color) else LocationPins.getCircleFillColor(),
            if (moving) ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.bearing_circle_stroke) else LocationPins.getCircleStrokeColor(),
        )
    }

    private fun drawTarget() {
        val current = current()
        if (!GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet() || current == null) {
            source(OsmStyle.LINE, OsmStyle.EMPTY_FEATURES)
            return
        }
        val target = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates().let { it[0].toDouble() to it[1].toDouble() }
        val start = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerStartCoordinates().let { it[0].toDouble() to it[1].toDouble() }
        source(OsmStyle.LINE, OsmStyle.line(if (custom) listOf(current, target) else listOf(start, current, target)))
    }

    override fun onCompassRotation(enabled: Boolean) = rotate(bearing(), animate = true)

    override fun bindPreferences(preferences: MapPreferences) {
        super.bindPreferences(preferences)
        GpsMapPreferences.bind(preferences, onPin = ::redrawPin, onBearing = { rotate(bearing(), animate = true) }, onTarget = ::drawTarget)
    }

    private fun redrawPin() {
        invalidatePin()
        drawPin()
    }
}
