# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/OsmMapView.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.content.res.Configuration
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Point
import android.location.Location
import android.view.View
import androidx.annotation.ColorInt
import androidx.lifecycle.DefaultLifecycleObserver
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.decorations.utils.DelayedTask
import app.simple.positional.decorations.utils.child
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapsCallbacks
import app.simple.positional.sensors.HeadingSensor
import app.simple.positional.util.ColorUtils.resolveAttrColor
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import org.maplibre.android.MapLibre
import org.maplibre.android.camera.CameraPosition
import org.maplibre.android.camera.CameraUpdateFactory
import org.maplibre.android.geometry.LatLngBounds
import org.maplibre.android.maps.MapLibreMap
import org.maplibre.android.maps.MapView
import org.maplibre.android.maps.Style
import org.maplibre.android.offline.OfflineManager
import org.maplibre.android.style.layers.Property
import org.maplibre.android.style.layers.PropertyFactory
import org.maplibre.android.style.layers.PropertyValue
import org.maplibre.android.style.sources.GeoJsonSource
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlin.math.roundToInt
import org.maplibre.android.geometry.LatLng as LibreLatLng

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
abstract class OsmMapView(context: Context) : MapView(OsmTiles.init(context)), MapHost, HeadingSensor.Listener {
    override val view: View get() = this
    protected var map: MapLibreMap? = null
    protected var mapCallbacks: MapsCallbacks? = null
    protected var location: Location? = null
    protected var heading = 0f
    protected var headingAccuracy = -1
    protected var animating = false
    protected var resumedScope: CoroutineScope? = null
        private set
    private var style: Style? = null
    private var pinKey: Any? = null
    private var owner: LifecycleOwner? = null
    private var sensorOn = false
    private val headingSensor = HeadingSensor(context, this)
    private val registerTask = DelayedTask(SENSOR_DELAY, ::registerHeading)
    private val accent = context.resolveAttrColor(R.attr.colorAppAccent)
    private val preferences by lazy { MapPreferences().also(::bindPreferences) }

    protected abstract val compassRotation: Boolean
    protected open val compassKey: String? = null
    protected abstract fun startCamera(): CameraPosition
    protected abstract fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition)
    protected open fun onStyleReady() {}
    protected open fun onClick(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        mapCallbacks?.onMapClicked()
    }
    protected open fun onGesture() {}
    protected open fun onCameraMoved() {}
    protected open fun onHeadingChanged() {}
    protected open fun onResumed() {}
    protected open fun onCompassRotation(enabled: Boolean) {}

    protected open fun bindPreferences(preferences: MapPreferences) {
        compassKey?.let { key ->
            preferences.watch(key, read = { compassRotation }) { enabled ->
                if (enabled) registerHeadingLater() else unregisterHeading()
                onCompassRotation(enabled)
            }
        }
    }

    override val lifecycleObserver = object : DefaultLifecycleObserver {
        override fun onStart(owner: LifecycleOwner) = this@OsmMapView.onStart()

        override fun onResume(owner: LifecycleOwner) {
            this@OsmMapView.owner = owner
            this@OsmMapView.onResume()
            resumedScope = owner.lifecycleScope.child().also { preferences.launchIn(it) }
            registerHeadingLater()
            onResumed()
        }

        override fun onPause(owner: LifecycleOwner) {
            unregisterHeading()
            resumedScope?.cancel()
            resumedScope = null
            this@OsmMapView.onPause()
        }

        override fun onStop(owner: LifecycleOwner) = this@OsmMapView.onStop()

        override fun onDestroy(owner: LifecycleOwner) {
            this@OsmMapView.owner = null
            this@OsmMapView.onDestroy()
        }
    }

    init {
        onCreate(null)
        getMapAsync(::ready)
    }

    private fun ready(map: MapLibreMap) {
        this.map = map
        map.uiSettings.isCompassEnabled = false
        map.uiSettings.isLogoEnabled = false
        map.moveCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newCameraPosition(startCamera()))
        map.addOnMapClickListener {
            onClick(it.latitude, it.longitude)
            true
        }
        map.addOnMapLongClickListener {
            mapCallbacks?.onMapLongClicked(LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude))
            true
        }
        map.addOnCameraMoveStartedListener { reason ->
            if (reason == MapLibreMap.OnCameraMoveStartedListener.REASON_API_GESTURE) {
                animating = false
                onGesture()
            }
        }
        map.addOnCameraMoveListener { onCameraMoved() }
        map.addOnCameraIdleListener { onCameraIdle(map.cameraPosition) }
        val dark = (resources.configuration.uiMode and Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK) == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES
        map.setStyle(Style.Builder().fromJson(OsmStyle.json(dark))) {
            style = it
            pinKey = null
            layer(OsmStyle.LINE, PropertyFactory.lineColor(accent))
            layer(OsmStyle.GUIDE, PropertyFactory.lineColor(accent))
            onStyleReady()
        }
    }

    override fun onHeading(azimuth: Float) {
        heading = azimuth
        onHeadingChanged()
    }

    override fun onAccuracy(type: Int, accuracy: Int) {
        headingAccuracy = accuracy
    }

    protected fun registerHeadingLater() {
        registerTask.schedule(resumedScope)
    }

    protected fun unregisterHeading() {
        registerTask.cancel()
        if (sensorOn) owner?.let(headingSensor::onPause)
        sensorOn = false
    }

    private fun registerHeading() {
        val current = owner ?: return
        if (sensorOn || !compassRotation || !headingSensor.isAvailable) return
        headingSensor.onResume(current)
        sensorOn = true
    }

    protected fun layer(id: String, vararg properties: PropertyValue<*>) {
        style?.getLayer(id)?.setProperties(*properties)
    }

    protected fun source(id: String, json: String) {
        style?.getSourceAs<GeoJsonSource>(id)?.setGeoJson(json)
    }

    protected fun image(name: String, bitmap: Bitmap) {
        style?.addImage(name, bitmap)
    }

    protected fun hasStyle() = style != null

    protected fun drawPin(key: Any, latitude: Double, longitude: Double, centered: Boolean, rotation: Float, bitmap: () -> Bitmap) {
        if (style == null) return
        if (pinKey != key) {
            image(OsmStyle.PIN, bitmap())
            pinKey = key
        }
        source(OsmStyle.PIN, OsmStyle.point(latitude, longitude))
        layer(
            OsmStyle.PIN,
            PropertyFactory.iconAnchor(if (centered) Property.ICON_ANCHOR_CENTER else Property.ICON_ANCHOR_BOTTOM),
            PropertyFactory.iconRotate(rotation),
        )
    }

    protected fun invalidatePin() {
        pinKey = null
    }

    protected fun drawAccuracy(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, radius: Double, @ColorInt fill: Int, @ColorInt stroke: Int) {
        source(OsmStyle.ACCURACY, OsmStyle.circle(latitude, longitude, radius))
        layer(OsmStyle.ACCURACY, PropertyFactory.fillColor(fill), PropertyFactory.fillOutlineColor(stroke))
    }

    protected fun target(): Pair<Double, Double>? = map?.cameraPosition?.target?.let { it.latitude to it.longitude }

    protected fun isVisible(latitude: Double, longitude: Double): Boolean =
        map?.projection?.visibleRegion?.latLngBounds?.contains(LibreLatLng(latitude, longitude)) ?: true

    protected fun screenPointOf(latitude: Double, longitude: Double): Point? =
        map?.projection?.toScreenLocation(LibreLatLng(latitude, longitude))?.let { Point(it.x.roundToInt(), it.y.roundToInt()) }

    protected fun camera(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, zoom: Float, tilt: Float, bearing: Float): CameraPosition = CameraPosition.Builder()
        .target(LibreLatLng(latitude, longitude))
        .zoom(zoom.toDouble())
        .tilt(tilt.toDouble())
        .bearing(bearing.toDouble())
        .build()

    protected fun moveCamera(position: CameraPosition) {
        map?.moveCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newCameraPosition(position))
    }

    protected fun animateTo(position: CameraPosition) {
        val map = map ?: return
        animating = true
        map.animateCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newCameraPosition(position), CAMERA_SPEED, object : MapLibreMap.CancelableCallback {
            override fun onCancel() {
                animating = false
            }

            override fun onFinish() {
                animating = false
            }
        })
    }

    protected fun fit(points: List<Pair<Double, Double>>, animate: Boolean): Boolean {
        val map = map ?: return false
        if (points.size < 2) return false
        val bounds = LatLngBounds.Builder().includes(points.map { LibreLatLng(it.first, it.second) }).build()
        val update = CameraUpdateFactory.newLatLngBounds(bounds, (FIT_PADDING * resources.displayMetrics.density).roundToInt())
        if (animate) map.animateCamera(update) else map.moveCamera(update)
        return true
    }

    protected fun zoom(zoomIn: Boolean) {
        map?.animateCamera(if (zoomIn) CameraUpdateFactory.zoomIn() else CameraUpdateFactory.zoomOut())
    }

    protected fun rotate(bearing: Float, animate: Boolean) {
        val update = CameraUpdateFactory.bearingTo(bearing.toDouble())
        if (animate) map?.animateCamera(update) else map?.moveCamera(update)
    }

    private companion object {
        const val CAMERA_SPEED = 1000
        const val SENSOR_DELAY = 500L
        const val FIT_PADDING = 64
    }
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
abstract class OsmPanelMap(context: Context) : OsmMapView(context), PanelMap {
    override fun setCallbacks(callbacks: MapsCallbacks) {
        mapCallbacks = callbacks
    }
}

object OsmTiles {
    private const val CACHE_BYTES = 128L * 1024 * 1024
    private var ready = false

    fun init(context: Context): Context {
        if (!ready) {
            MapLibre.getInstance(context.applicationContext)
            OfflineManager.getInstance(context.applicationContext).setMaximumAmbientCacheSize(CACHE_BYTES, null)
            ready = true
        }
        return context
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/OsmMeasureMap.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.location.Location
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.model.Measure
import app.simple.positional.model.MeasurePoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MeasurePreferences
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import org.maplibre.android.camera.CameraPosition

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class OsmMeasureMap(context: Context) : OsmPanelMap(context), MeasureMap {
    override val compassRotation get() = MeasurePreferences.isCompassRotation()
    override val compassKey get() = MeasurePreferences.COMPASS_ROTATION
    private val measurePoints = mutableListOf<MeasurePoint>()
    override val points: List<MeasurePoint> get() = measurePoints
    private var restore: CameraPosition? = null

    override fun startCamera(): CameraPosition = camera(
        MeasurePreferences.getLastLatitude(),
        MeasurePreferences.getLastLongitude(),
        MeasurePreferences.getMapZoom(),
        MeasurePreferences.getMapTilt(),
        MeasurePreferences.getMapBearing(),
    )

    override fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition) {
        MeasurePreferences.setMapZoom(position.zoom.toFloat())
        MeasurePreferences.setMapTilt(position.tilt.toFloat())
        MeasurePreferences.setMapBearing(position.bearing.toFloat())
        position.target?.let {
            MeasurePreferences.setLastLatitude(it.latitude)
            MeasurePreferences.setLastLongitude(it.longitude)
        }
    }

    override fun onCameraMoved() {
        drawGuide()
        reportDistance()
    }

    override fun onStyleReady() {
        drawLines()
        drawPuck()
        reportDistance()
        if (MeasurePreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) wrap(animate = false)
    }

    override fun onHeadingChanged() = drawPuck()

    override fun updateLocation(location: Location) {
        this.location = location
        drawPuck()
    }

    override fun resetCamera(zoom: Float) {
        location?.let { moveTo(it.latitude, it.longitude, zoom) }
    }

    override fun showMeasure(measure: Measure) {
        measurePoints.clear()
        measurePoints.addAll(measure.points)
        drawLines()
        if (MeasurePreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) wrap(animate = false)
    }

    override fun addPoint() {
        val (latitude, longitude) = target() ?: return
        val point = MeasurePoint(latitude, longitude, measurePoints.size + 1)
        measurePoints.add(point)
        drawLines()
        mapCallbacks?.onLineAdded(point)
        reportDistance()
    }

    override fun removeLast() {
        val last = measurePoints.removeLastOrNull() ?: return
        drawLines()
        mapCallbacks?.onLineDeleted(last)
        reportDistance()
    }

    override fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, zoom: Float) {
        restore = null
        MeasurePreferences.setPolylinesWrapped(false)
        animateTo(camera(latitude, longitude, zoom, MeasurePreferences.getMapTilt(), MeasurePreferences.getMapBearing()))
    }

    override fun clear() {
        measurePoints.clear()
        drawLines()
    }

    private fun wrap(animate: Boolean) {
        if (restore == null) restore = map?.cameraPosition
        fit(measurePoints.map { it.latitude to it.longitude }, animate)
    }

    private fun drawLines() {
        source(OsmStyle.LINE, OsmStyle.line(measurePoints.map { it.latitude to it.longitude }))
        drawGuide()
    }

    private fun drawGuide() {
        val last = measurePoints.lastOrNull()
        val target = target()
        source(OsmStyle.GUIDE, if (last == null || target == null) OsmStyle.EMPTY_FEATURES else OsmStyle.line(listOf(last.latitude to last.longitude, target)))
    }

    private fun reportDistance() {
        target()?.let { mapCallbacks?.onCameraDistance(LatLng(it.first, it.second)) }
    }

    private fun drawPuck() {
        val fix = location ?: return
        val compass = MeasurePreferences.isCompassRotation()
        val pin = TrackPin.drawable(true, compass, headingAccuracy, fix.speed > 0f)
        drawPin(pin, fix.latitude, fix.longitude, centered = true, rotation = if (compass) heading else fix.bearing) {
            TrackPin.bitmap(context, pin)
        }
        drawAccuracy(
            fix.latitude,
            fix.longitude,
            fix.accuracy.toDouble(),
            ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.map_circle_color),
            ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.compass_pin_color),
        )
    }

    override fun onCompassRotation(enabled: Boolean) = drawPuck()

    override fun bindPreferences(preferences: MapPreferences) {
        super.bindPreferences(preferences)
        preferences.watch(MeasurePreferences.POLYLINES_WRAPPED, read = MeasurePreferences::arePolylinesWrapped) { wrapped ->
            if (wrapped) {
                wrap(animate = true)
            } else {
                restore?.let(::animateTo)
                restore = null
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/OsmStyle.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import kotlin.math.asin
import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.sin

object OsmStyle {
    const val PIN = "pin"
    const val ACCURACY = "accuracy"
    const val LINE = "line"
    const val GUIDE = "guide"
    const val MARKERS = "markers"
    const val ICON = "icon"
    const val EMPTY_FEATURES = """{"type":"FeatureCollection","features":[]}"""
    private const val TILES = "https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
    private const val ATTRIBUTION = "© OpenStreetMap contributors"
    private const val EARTH_RADIUS = 6_371_008.8
    private const val CIRCLE_STEPS = 64
    private const val EMPTY = """{"type":"geojson","data":$EMPTY_FEATURES}"""
    private const val DARK = ""","paint":{"raster-brightness-min":1,"raster-brightness-max":0,"raster-saturation":-0.5}"""
    private const val OVERLAP = """"icon-allow-overlap":true,"icon-ignore-placement":true"""

    fun json(dark: Boolean): String {
        val raster = if (dark) DARK else ""
        val sources = listOf(ACCURACY, LINE, GUIDE, MARKERS, PIN).joinToString(",") { "\"$it\":$EMPTY" }
        return """{"version":8,"sources":{"osm":{"type":"raster","tiles":["$TILES"],"tileSize":256,"maxzoom":19,"attribution":"$ATTRIBUTION"},$sources},""" +
            """"layers":[{"id":"osm","type":"raster","source":"osm"$raster},""" +
            """{"id":"$ACCURACY","type":"fill","source":"$ACCURACY"},""" +
            """{"id":"$LINE","type":"line","source":"$LINE","layout":{"line-join":"round","line-cap":"round"},"paint":{"line-width":4}},""" +
            """{"id":"$GUIDE","type":"line","source":"$GUIDE","paint":{"line-width":2,"line-dasharray":[2,2]}},""" +
            """{"id":"$MARKERS","type":"symbol","source":"$MARKERS","layout":{"icon-image":["get","$ICON"],"icon-anchor":"bottom",$OVERLAP}},""" +
            """{"id":"$PIN","type":"symbol","source":"$PIN","layout":{"icon-image":"$PIN","icon-rotation-alignment":"map",$OVERLAP}}]}"""
    }

    fun point(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) = """{"type":"Point","coordinates":[$longitude,$latitude]}"""

    fun line(points: List<Pair<Double, Double>>) =
        if (points.size < 2) EMPTY_FEATURES else """{"type":"LineString","coordinates":[${coordinates(points)}]}"""

    fun markers(markers: List<Triple<Double, Double, String>>): String {
        val features = markers.joinToString(",") { (latitude, longitude, icon) -> marker(latitude, longitude, icon) }
        return """{"type":"FeatureCollection","features":[$features]}"""
    }

    private fun marker(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, icon: String) =
        """{"type":"Feature","geometry":${point(latitude, longitude)},"properties":{"$ICON":"$icon"}}"""

    fun circle(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, radius: Double) =
        """{"type":"Polygon","coordinates":[[${coordinates(circlePoints(latitude, longitude, radius))}]]}"""

    fun circlePoints(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, radius: Double): List<Pair<Double, Double>> {
        val lat = Math.toRadians(latitude)
        val lng = Math.toRadians(longitude)
        val distance = radius / EARTH_RADIUS
        return (0..CIRCLE_STEPS).map { step ->
            val bearing = 2 * Math.PI * step / CIRCLE_STEPS
            val pointLat = asin(sin(lat) * cos(distance) + cos(lat) * sin(distance) * cos(bearing))
            val pointLng = lng + atan2(sin(bearing) * sin(distance) * cos(lat), cos(distance) - sin(lat) * sin(pointLat))
            Math.toDegrees(pointLat) to Math.toDegrees(pointLng)
        }
    }

    private fun coordinates(points: List<Pair<Double, Double>>) = points.joinToString(",") { "[${it.second},${it.first}]" }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/OsmTrailMap.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.location.Location
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.TrailIcons
import app.simple.positional.decorations.utils.DelayedTask
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailPoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.TrailPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.BitmapHelper.toBitmap
import org.maplibre.android.camera.CameraPosition

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class OsmTrailMap(context: Context) : OsmPanelMap(context), TrailMap {
    override val compassRotation get() = TrailPreferences.isCompassRotation()
    override val compassKey get() = TrailPreferences.compass
    private var points: List<TrailPoint> = emptyList()
    private val icons = mutableSetOf<Int>()
    private var restore: CameraPosition? = null
    private val autoCenter = DelayedTask(AUTO_CENTER_DELAY, ::recenter)

    override fun startCamera(): CameraPosition {
        val last = MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()
        return camera(last[0].toDouble(), last[1].toDouble(), TrailPreferences.getMapZoom(), TrailPreferences.getMapTilt(), TrailPreferences.getMapBearing())
    }

    override fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition) {
        TrailPreferences.setMapZoom(position.zoom.toFloat())
        TrailPreferences.setMapTilt(position.tilt.toFloat())
        TrailPreferences.setMapBearing(position.bearing.toFloat())
        scheduleAutoCenter()
    }

    override fun onCameraMoved() = autoCenter.cancel()

    override fun onStyleReady() {
        icons.clear()
        drawPoints()
        drawPuck()
        if (TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) wrap(animate = false)
    }

    override fun onHeadingChanged() = drawPuck()

    override fun onResumed() = scheduleAutoCenter()

    override fun updateLocation(location: Location) {
        val first = this.location == null
        this.location = location
        drawPuck()
        if (first && !TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) {
            moveCamera(camera(location.latitude, location.longitude, TrailPreferences.getMapZoom(), TrailPreferences.getMapTilt(), TrailPreferences.getMapBearing()))
        }
    }

    override fun resetCamera(zoom: Float) {
        location?.let { moveTo(it.latitude, it.longitude, zoom) }
    }

    override fun showPoints(points: List<TrailPoint>) {
        this.points = points
        drawPoints()
        if (TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) wrap(animate = false)
    }

    override fun removeLast() {
        val last = points.lastOrNull() ?: return
        mapCallbacks?.onLineDeleted(last)
        val previous = points.getOrNull(points.size - 2)
        if (TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped() || previous == null) return
        moveTo(previous.latitude, previous.longitude, TrailPreferences.getMapZoom())
    }

    override fun toggleWrap() {
        if (!TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) {
            wrap(animate = true)
            return
        }
        TrailPreferences.setWrapStatus(false)
        restore?.let(::animateTo)
    }

    override fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, zoom: Float) {
        TrailPreferences.setWrapStatus(false)
        animateTo(camera(latitude, longitude, zoom, TrailPreferences.getMapTilt(), TrailPreferences.getMapBearing()))
    }

    private fun wrap(animate: Boolean) {
        if (!TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) restore = map?.cameraPosition
        if (fit(points.map { it.latitude to it.longitude }, animate)) TrailPreferences.setWrapStatus(true)
    }

    private fun scheduleAutoCenter() {
        if (TrailPreferences.getMapAutoCenter()) autoCenter.schedule(resumedScope) else autoCenter.cancel()
    }

    private fun recenter() {
        val current = location ?: return
        if (TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) return
        moveTo(current.latitude, current.longitude, TrailPreferences.getMapZoom())
    }

    private fun drawPoints() {
        if (!hasStyle()) return
        points.map { it.iconPosition }.filter { it !in icons }.toSet().forEach {
            image(iconName(it), TrailIcons.icons.getOrElse(it) { TrailIcons.icons[0] }.toBitmap(context, ICON_SIZE))
            icons.add(it)
        }
        source(OsmStyle.LINE, OsmStyle.line(points.map { it.latitude to it.longitude }))
        source(OsmStyle.MARKERS, OsmStyle.markers(points.map { Triple(it.latitude, it.longitude, iconName(it.iconPosition)) }))
    }

    private fun drawPuck() {
        val point = location?.let { it.latitude to it.longitude }
            ?: MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates().let { it[0].toDouble() to it[1].toDouble() }
        val fix = location
        val compass = TrailPreferences.isCompassRotation()
        val pin = TrackPin.drawable(fix != null, compass, headingAccuracy, (fix?.speed ?: 0f) > 0f)
        drawPin(pin, point.first, point.second, centered = true, rotation = if (compass) heading else fix?.bearing ?: 0f) {
            TrackPin.bitmap(context, pin)
        }
        if (fix == null) return
        drawAccuracy(
            point.first,
            point.second,
            fix.accuracy.toDouble(),
            ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.map_circle_color),
            ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.compass_pin_color),
        )
    }

    private fun iconName(position: Int) = "trail-$position"

    override fun onCompassRotation(enabled: Boolean) = drawPuck()

    override fun bindPreferences(preferences: MapPreferences) {
        super.bindPreferences(preferences)
        preferences.watch(TrailPreferences.mapAutoCenter, read = TrailPreferences::getMapAutoCenter) { scheduleAutoCenter() }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val AUTO_CENTER_DELAY = 6000L
        const val ICON_SIZE = 50
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/PickerMap.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.MapSource
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.CameraPosition as GoogleCameraPosition
import org.maplibre.android.camera.CameraPosition

interface PickerMap : MapHost {
    var onIdle: ((Double, Double) -> Unit)?
    fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, animate: Boolean)

    companion object {
        const val ZOOM = 15F

        fun create(context: Context): PickerMap = if (MapSource.isOsm()) OsmPickerMap(context) else GooglePickerMap(context)
    }
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class GooglePickerMap(context: Context) : GoogleMapView(context), PickerMap {
    override val compassRotation = false
    override var onIdle: ((Double, Double) -> Unit)? = null
    private var pending: LatLng? = null

    override fun look() = MapLook(label = true, satellite = false, highContrast = false, buildings = true, traffic = false)

    override fun startCamera(): GoogleCameraPosition =
        camera(pending ?: MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }, PickerMap.ZOOM, 0f, 0f)

    override fun onCameraIdle(position: GoogleCameraPosition) {
        onIdle?.invoke(position.target.latitude, position.target.longitude)
    }

    override fun onClick(latLng: LatLng) {
        animateTo(camera(latLng, map?.cameraPosition?.zoom ?: PickerMap.ZOOM, 0f, 0f))
    }

    override fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, animate: Boolean) {
        val target = LatLng(latitude, longitude)
        pending = target
        val position = camera(target, PickerMap.ZOOM, 0f, 0f)
        if (animate) animateTo(position) else moveCamera(position)
    }
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class OsmPickerMap(context: Context) : OsmMapView(context), PickerMap {
    override val compassRotation = false
    override var onIdle: ((Double, Double) -> Unit)? = null
    private var pending: Pair<Double, Double>? = null

    override fun startCamera(): CameraPosition {
        val (latitude, longitude) = pending ?: MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates().let { it[0].toDouble() to it[1].toDouble() }
        return camera(latitude, longitude, PickerMap.ZOOM, 0f, 0f)
    }

    override fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition) {
        position.target?.let { onIdle?.invoke(it.latitude, it.longitude) }
    }

    override fun onClick(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        val zoom = map?.cameraPosition?.zoom?.toFloat() ?: PickerMap.ZOOM
        animateTo(camera(latitude, longitude, zoom, 0f, 0f))
    }

    override fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, animate: Boolean) {
        pending = latitude to longitude
        val position = camera(latitude, longitude, PickerMap.ZOOM, 0f, 0f)
        if (animate) animateTo(position) else moveCamera(position)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Color
import android.hardware.SensorManager
import android.location.Location
import androidx.annotation.DrawableRes
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.TrailIcons
import app.simple.positional.decorations.utils.DelayedTask
import app.simple.positional.model.Measure
import app.simple.positional.model.MeasurePoint
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailPoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MeasurePreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.TrailPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.BitmapHelper.toBitmap
import app.simple.positional.util.BitmapHelper.toBitmapKeepingSize
import app.simple.positional.util.MapSource
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.BitmapDescriptor
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.BitmapDescriptorFactory
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.CameraPosition
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Dash
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Gap
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Marker
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.MarkerOptions
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Polyline

interface TrailMap : PanelMap {
    fun showPoints(points: List<TrailPoint>)
    fun removeLast()
    fun toggleWrap()
    fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, zoom: Float)

    companion object {
        fun create(context: Context): TrailMap = if (MapSource.isOsm()) OsmTrailMap(context) else GoogleTrailMap(context)
    }
}

interface MeasureMap : PanelMap {
    val points: List<MeasurePoint>
    fun showMeasure(measure: Measure)
    fun addPoint()
    fun removeLast()
    fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, zoom: Float)
    fun clear()

    companion object {
        fun create(context: Context): MeasureMap = if (MapSource.isOsm()) OsmMeasureMap(context) else GoogleMeasureMap(context)
    }
}

object TrackPin {
    private const val SCALE = 2
    private const val HISTORICAL_SIZE = 60

    @DrawableRes
    fun drawable(hasLocation: Boolean, compass: Boolean, accuracy: Int, moving: Boolean): Int = when {
        !hasLocation -> R.drawable.ic_place_historical
        compass -> when (accuracy) {
            SensorManager.SENSOR_STATUS_ACCURACY_LOW -> R.drawable.ic_pin_low
            SensorManager.SENSOR_STATUS_ACCURACY_MEDIUM -> R.drawable.ic_pin_medium
            SensorManager.SENSOR_STATUS_ACCURACY_HIGH -> R.drawable.ic_pin_high
            else -> R.drawable.ic_pin_unreliable
        }
        moving -> R.drawable.ic_pin_bearing
        else -> R.drawable.ic_pin_no_speed
    }

    fun bitmap(context: Context, @DrawableRes drawable: Int): Bitmap =
        if (drawable == R.drawable.ic_place_historical) drawable.toBitmap(context, HISTORICAL_SIZE) else drawable.toBitmapKeepingSize(context, SCALE)
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
abstract class GoogleTrackMap(context: Context) : GooglePanelMap(context) {
    protected fun drawPuck() {
        val compass = compassRotation
        val fix = location
        val point = fix?.let { LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude) } ?: MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }
        val pin = TrackPin.drawable(fix != null, compass, headingAccuracy, (fix?.speed ?: 0f) > 0f)
        drawPin(pin, point, centered = true, rotation = if (compass) null else fix?.bearing ?: 0f) { TrackPin.bitmap(context, pin) }
        if (fix == null) return
        drawAccuracy(
            point,
            fix.accuracy.toDouble(),
            ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.map_circle_color),
            ContextCompat.getColor(context, R.color.compass_pin_color),
        )
    }

    override fun onHeadingChanged() {
        if (compassRotation) rotatePin(heading)
    }

    override fun onHeadingAccuracyChanged() = drawPuck()

    override fun onCompassRotation(enabled: Boolean) = drawPuck()
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class GoogleTrailMap(context: Context) : GoogleTrackMap(context), TrailMap {
    override val compassRotation get() = TrailPreferences.isCompassRotation()
    override val compassKey get() = TrailPreferences.compass
    override val lookKeys: Set<String> = setOf(
        TrailPreferences.trailLabelMode,
        TrailPreferences.trailSatellite,
        TrailPreferences.trailHighContrastMap,
        TrailPreferences.trailShowBuilding,
    )
    private var points: List<TrailPoint> = emptyList()
    private var path: Polyline? = null
    private val flags = mutableListOf<Pair<Long, Marker>>()
    private val icons = mutableMapOf<Int, BitmapDescriptor>()
    private var restore: CameraPosition? = null
    private val autoCenter = DelayedTask(AUTO_CENTER_DELAY, ::recenter)

    override fun look() = MapLook(
        TrailPreferences.isLabelOn(),
        TrailPreferences.isSatelliteOn(),
        TrailPreferences.getHighContrastMap(),
        TrailPreferences.getShowBuildingsOnMap(),
        traffic = false,
    )

    override fun startCamera(): CameraPosition {
        val start = location?.let { LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude) } ?: MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }
        return camera(start, TrailPreferences.getMapZoom(), TrailPreferences.getMapTilt(), TrailPreferences.getMapBearing())
    }

    override fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition) {
        TrailPreferences.setMapZoom(position.zoom)
        TrailPreferences.setMapTilt(position.tilt)
        TrailPreferences.setMapBearing(position.bearing)
        scheduleAutoCenter()
    }

    override fun onCameraMoved() = autoCenter.cancel()

    override fun onReady() {
        drawPoints()
        drawPuck()
        if (TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) wrap(animate = false)
    }

    override fun onResumed() = scheduleAutoCenter()

    override fun updateLocation(location: Location) {
        val first = this.location == null
        this.location = location
        drawPuck()
        if (first && !TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) {
            moveCamera(camera(LatLng(location.latitude, location.longitude), TrailPreferences.getMapZoom(), TrailPreferences.getMapTilt(), TrailPreferences.getMapBearing()))
        }
    }

    override fun resetCamera(zoom: Float) {
        location?.let { moveTo(it.latitude, it.longitude, zoom) }
    }

    override fun showPoints(points: List<TrailPoint>) {
        this.points = points
        drawPoints()
        if (TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) wrap(animate = false)
    }

    override fun removeLast() {
        val last = points.lastOrNull() ?: return
        mapCallbacks?.onLineDeleted(last)
        val previous = points.getOrNull(points.size - 2)
        if (TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped() || previous == null) return
        moveTo(previous.latitude, previous.longitude, TrailPreferences.getMapZoom())
    }

    override fun toggleWrap() {
        if (!TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) {
            wrap(animate = true)
            return
        }
        TrailPreferences.setWrapStatus(false)
        restore?.let(::animateTo)
    }

    override fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, zoom: Float) {
        TrailPreferences.setWrapStatus(false)
        animateTo(camera(LatLng(latitude, longitude), zoom, TrailPreferences.getMapTilt(), TrailPreferences.getMapBearing()))
    }

    private fun wrap(animate: Boolean) {
        if (!TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) restore = map?.cameraPosition
        if (fit(points.map { LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude) }, animate)) TrailPreferences.setWrapStatus(true)
    }

    private fun scheduleAutoCenter() {
        if (TrailPreferences.getMapAutoCenter()) autoCenter.schedule(resumedScope) else autoCenter.cancel()
    }

    private fun recenter() {
        val current = location ?: return
        if (TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) return
        moveTo(current.latitude, current.longitude, TrailPreferences.getMapZoom())
    }

    private fun drawPoints() {
        val map = map ?: return
        val positions = points.map { LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude) }
        path?.let { it.points = positions } ?: run { path = line(positions, geodesic = TrailPreferences.isTrailGeodesic()) }
        var kept = 0
        while (kept < flags.size && kept < points.size && flags[kept].first == points[kept].timeAdded) kept++
        while (flags.size > kept) flags.removeAt(flags.lastIndex).second.remove()
        points.drop(kept).forEach { point ->
            val marker = map.addMarker(MarkerOptions().position(LatLng(point.latitude, point.longitude)).icon(icon(point.iconPosition)))
            if (marker != null) flags += point.timeAdded to marker
        }
    }

    private fun icon(position: Int): BitmapDescriptor = icons.getOrPut(position) {
        BitmapDescriptorFactory.fromBitmap(TrailIcons.icons.getOrElse(position) { TrailIcons.icons[0] }.toBitmap(context, ICON_SIZE))
    }

    override fun bindPreferences(preferences: MapPreferences) {
        super.bindPreferences(preferences)
        preferences.watch(TrailPreferences.mapAutoCenter, read = TrailPreferences::getMapAutoCenter) { scheduleAutoCenter() }
        preferences.watch(TrailPreferences.geodesic, read = TrailPreferences::isTrailGeodesic) { path?.isGeodesic = it }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val AUTO_CENTER_DELAY = 6000L
        const val ICON_SIZE = 50
    }
}

@SuppressLint("ViewConstructor")
class GoogleMeasureMap(context: Context) : GoogleTrackMap(context), MeasureMap {
    override val compassRotation get() = MeasurePreferences.isCompassRotation()
    override val compassKey get() = MeasurePreferences.COMPASS_ROTATION
    override val lookKeys: Set<String> = setOf(
        MeasurePreferences.LABEL_MODE,
        MeasurePreferences.SATELLITE_MAP,
        MeasurePreferences.HIGH_CONTRAST_MAP,
        MeasurePreferences.SHOW_BUILDINGS,
    )
    private val measurePoints = mutableListOf<MeasurePoint>()
    override val points: List<MeasurePoint> get() = measurePoints
    private var restore: CameraPosition? = null
    private var path: Polyline? = null
    private var guide: Polyline? = null

    override fun look() = MapLook(
        MeasurePreferences.isLabelOn(),
        MeasurePreferences.isSatelliteOn(),
        MeasurePreferences.getHighContrastMap(),
        MeasurePreferences.getShowBuildingsOnMap(),
        traffic = false,
    )

    override fun startCamera(): CameraPosition = camera(
        LatLng(MeasurePreferences.getLastLatitude(), MeasurePreferences.getLastLongitude()),
        MeasurePreferences.getMapZoom(),
        MeasurePreferences.getMapTilt(),
        MeasurePreferences.getMapBearing(),
    )

    override fun onCameraIdle(position: CameraPosition) {
        MeasurePreferences.setMapZoom(position.zoom)
        MeasurePreferences.setMapTilt(position.tilt)
        MeasurePreferences.setMapBearing(position.bearing)
        MeasurePreferences.setLastLatitude(position.target.latitude)
        MeasurePreferences.setLastLongitude(position.target.longitude)
    }

    override fun onCameraMoved() {
        drawGuide()
        reportDistance()
    }

    override fun onReady() {
        drawLines()
        drawPuck()
        reportDistance()
        if (MeasurePreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) wrap(animate = false)
    }

    override fun updateLocation(location: Location) {
        this.location = location
        drawPuck()
    }

    override fun resetCamera(zoom: Float) {
        location?.let { moveTo(it.latitude, it.longitude, zoom) }
    }

    override fun showMeasure(measure: Measure) {
        measurePoints.clear()
        measurePoints.addAll(measure.points)
        drawLines()
        if (MeasurePreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) wrap(animate = false)
    }

    override fun addPoint() {
        val target = target() ?: return
        val point = MeasurePoint(target.latitude, target.longitude, measurePoints.size + 1)
        measurePoints.add(point)
        drawLines()
        mapCallbacks?.onLineAdded(point)
        reportDistance()
    }

    override fun removeLast() {
        val last = measurePoints.removeLastOrNull() ?: return
        drawLines()
        mapCallbacks?.onLineDeleted(last)
        reportDistance()
    }

    override fun moveTo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, zoom: Float) {
        restore = null
        MeasurePreferences.setPolylinesWrapped(false)
        animateTo(camera(LatLng(latitude, longitude), zoom, MeasurePreferences.getMapTilt(), MeasurePreferences.getMapBearing()))
    }

    override fun clear() {
        measurePoints.clear()
        drawLines()
    }

    private fun wrap(animate: Boolean) {
        if (restore == null) restore = map?.cameraPosition
        fit(measurePoints.map { LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude) }, animate)
    }

    private fun drawLines() {
        val positions = measurePoints.map { LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude) }
        path?.let { it.points = positions } ?: run { path = line(positions) }
        drawGuide()
    }

    private fun drawGuide() {
        val last = measurePoints.lastOrNull()
        val target = target()
        val positions = if (last == null || target == null) emptyList() else listOf(LatLng(last.latitude, last.longitude), target)
        guide?.let { it.points = positions } ?: run { guide = line(positions, Color.BLACK, GUIDE_WIDTH, pattern = GUIDE_PATTERN) }
    }

    private fun reportDistance() {
        target()?.let { mapCallbacks?.onCameraDistance(it) }
    }

    override fun bindPreferences(preferences: MapPreferences) {
        super.bindPreferences(preferences)
        preferences.watch(MeasurePreferences.POLYLINES_WRAPPED, read = MeasurePreferences::arePolylinesWrapped) { wrapped ->
            if (wrapped) {
                wrap(animate = true)
            } else {
                restore?.let(::animateTo)
                restore = null
            }
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val GUIDE_WIDTH = 7f
        val GUIDE_PATTERN = listOf(Dash(20f), Gap(10f), Dash(30f), Gap(20f), Dash(40f), Gap(30f))
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/utils/DelayedTask.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.utils

import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

class DelayedTask(private val delayMillis: Long, private val action: () -> Unit) {
    private var job: Job? = null

    fun schedule(scope: CoroutineScope?) {
        cancel()
        job = scope?.launch {
            delay(delayMillis)
            action()
        }
    }

    fun cancel() {
        job?.cancel()
        job = null
    }
}

fun CoroutineScope.child(): CoroutineScope = CoroutineScope(coroutineContext + SupervisorJob(coroutineContext.job))

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/utils/MapAnimations.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.utils

import android.animation.ValueAnimator
import android.view.animation.PathInterpolator
import androidx.core.graphics.ColorUtils
import app.simple.positional.math.MapInterpolation
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Circle
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.Marker

private const val DURATION = 1000L

private fun animate(update: (Float) -> Unit): ValueAnimator = ValueAnimator.ofFloat(0f, 1f).apply {
    duration = DURATION
    interpolator = PathInterpolator(0f, 0f, 0.2f, 1f)
    addUpdateListener { animator -> runCatching { update(animator.animatedFraction) } }
    start()
}

private fun LatLng.toward(end: LatLng, fraction: Float): LatLng {
    val (latitude, longitude) = MapInterpolation.position(fraction, latitude, longitude, end.latitude, end.longitude)
    return LatLng(latitude, longitude)
}

fun Marker.animateTo(end: LatLng, rotation: Float?): ValueAnimator {
    val start = position
    val startRotation = this.rotation
    return animate { fraction ->
        position = start.toward(end, fraction)
        if (rotation != null) this.rotation = MapInterpolation.angle(fraction, startRotation, rotation)
    }
}

fun Circle.animateTo(end: LatLng, endRadius: Double): ValueAnimator {
    val start = center
    val startRadius = radius
    return animate { fraction ->
        center = start.toward(end, fraction)
        radius = MapInterpolation.linear(fraction, startRadius, endRadius)
    }
}

fun Circle.animateColors(fill: Int, stroke: Int): ValueAnimator {
    val startFill = fillColor
    val startStroke = strokeColor
    return animate { fraction ->
        fillColor = ColorUtils.blendARGB(startFill, fill, fraction)
        strokeColor = ColorUtils.blendARGB(startStroke, stroke, fraction)
    }
}

open class CustomWebView @JvmOverloads constructor(context: Context, attributeSet: AttributeSet? = null) : WebView(context, attributeSet) {
    init {
        setBackgroundColor(0)
        settings.setSupportZoom(true)
        val night = context.resources.configuration.uiMode and Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES
        if (WebViewFeature.isFeatureSupported(WebViewFeature.ALGORITHMIC_DARKENING)) {
            WebSettingsCompat.setAlgorithmicDarkeningAllowed(settings, night)
        } else if (night && WebViewFeature.isFeatureSupported(WebViewFeature.FORCE_DARK)) {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            WebSettingsCompat.setForceDark(settings, WebSettingsCompat.FORCE_DARK_ON)
        }
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.extensions.activity

import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
import android.graphics.PixelFormat
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.text.format.DateFormat
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.view.WindowManager
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.core.os.ConfigurationCompat
import androidx.core.view.ViewCompat
import androidx.core.view.WindowInsetsCompat
import androidx.core.view.updateLayoutParams
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.AccentThemes
import app.simple.positional.util.ContextUtils
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import app.simple.positional.widgets.ClockWidgets

open class BaseActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    override fun attachBaseContext(newBase: Context) {
        SharedPreferences.init(newBase)
        super.attachBaseContext(ContextUtils.wrap(newBase, ContextUtils.language()))
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        setTheme(accentTheme())
        enableEdgeToEdge()
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)

        if (MainPreferences.getLaunchCount() == 0 && DateFormat.is24HourFormat(this)) {
            ClockPreferences.setDefaultClockTime(false)
        }
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            window.colorMode = ActivityInfo.COLOR_MODE_WIDE_COLOR_GAMUT
        }
        window.setFormat(PixelFormat.RGBA_8888)
        if (MainPreferences.isScreenOn()) {
            window.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)
        }
        keepContentAboveNavigationBar()
        ConfigurationCompat.getLocales(resources.configuration)[0]?.let(LocaleHelper::setAppLocale)
    }

    override fun onStop() {
        super.onStop()
        if (!isChangingConfigurations) ClockWidgets.refresh(this)
    }

    private fun accentTheme(): Int = AccentThemes.of(this) ?: R.style.Positional.also {
        MainPreferences.setAccentColor(getColor(R.color.positional))
    }

    private fun keepContentAboveNavigationBar() {
        ViewCompat.setOnApplyWindowInsetsListener(findViewById(android.R.id.content)) { view, windowInsets ->
            val insets = windowInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
            view.updateLayoutParams<ViewGroup.MarginLayoutParams> {
                leftMargin = insets.left
                bottomMargin = insets.bottom
                rightMargin = insets.right
            }
            WindowInsetsCompat.CONSUMED
        }
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/extensions/panel/PanelController.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.extensions.panel

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.res.Configuration
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultLauncher
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContract
import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.DisposableEffect
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleEventObserver
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleOwner
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleRegistry
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.LocalLifecycleOwner
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.lifecycle.repeatOnLifecycle
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.viewModelFactory
import app.simple.positional.constants.HtmlPage
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.AppNavigator
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.SearchResult
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.DialogHost
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ErrorDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MessageDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MenuSheet
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MenuSheetState
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.LocationViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlin.reflect.KClass

abstract class PanelController(val activity: ComponentActivity) : LifecycleOwner {
    private val registry = LifecycleRegistry(this)
    private val launchers = mutableListOf<ActivityResultLauncher<*>>()
    private var destroyed = false
    private val parentObserver = LifecycleEventObserver { _, event ->
        if (event == Lifecycle.Event.ON_DESTROY) return@LifecycleEventObserver
        registry.handleLifecycleEvent(event)
        when (event) {
            Lifecycle.Event.ON_RESUME -> onResume()
            Lifecycle.Event.ON_PAUSE -> onPause()
            else -> Unit
        }
    }

    override val lifecycle: Lifecycle get() = registry
    protected val owner: LifecycleOwner get() = this
    protected val dialogs = DialogHost()
    protected val menuSheet = MenuSheetState()
    protected val locationModel: LocationViewModel by lazy { ViewModelProvider(activity)[LocationViewModel::class.java] }
    protected val isLandscapeOrientation: Boolean = activity.resources.configuration.orientation == Configuration.ORIENTATION_LANDSCAPE

    @Composable
    abstract fun Content(topInset: Dp)

    protected open fun onResume() {}

    protected open fun onPause() {}

    protected open fun onCleared() {}

    protected fun getString(@StringRes id: Int): String = activity.getString(id)

    protected fun getString(@StringRes id: Int, vararg args: Any): String = activity.getString(id, *args)

    protected fun startActivity(intent: Intent) = activity.startActivity(intent)

    protected fun navigate(route: String) {
        (activity as? AppNavigator)?.navigate(route)
    }

    protected fun openPage(page: HtmlPage) = navigate(Routes.web(page))

    protected fun search() = navigate(Routes.MAP_SEARCH)

    open fun onSearchResult(result: SearchResult) {}

    protected fun showError(message: String) = dialogs.show(ErrorDialog(message))

    @Composable
    protected open fun DialogContent(dialog: PanelDialog) {
        if (dialog is ErrorDialog) MessageDialog(dialog.message, dialogs::dismiss)
    }

    protected fun <T : ViewModel> panelModel(type: KClass<T>, create: (Application, LocationViewModel) -> T): T =
        ViewModelProvider(activity, viewModelFactory { addInitializer(type) { create(activity.application, locationModel) } })[type.java]

    protected fun <T> observe(flow: Flow<T>, action: (T) -> Unit) {
        lifecycleScope.launch { repeatOnLifecycle(Lifecycle.State.STARTED) { flow.collect { action(it) } } }
    }

    protected fun <T> bind(flow: StateFlow<T>, action: (T) -> Unit) {
        action(flow.value)
        observe(flow, action)
    }

    protected fun <I, O> register(contract: ActivityResultContract<I, O>, callback: (O) -> Unit): ActivityResultLauncher<I> =
        activity.activityResultRegistry.register("${javaClass.name}#${launchers.size}", contract, callback).also { launchers += it }

    private fun follow(parent: LifecycleOwner) {
        if (!destroyed) parent.lifecycle.addObserver(parentObserver)
    }

    private fun unfollow(parent: LifecycleOwner) {
        parent.lifecycle.removeObserver(parentObserver)
        if (destroyed) return
        if (registry.currentState.isAtLeast(Lifecycle.State.RESUMED)) onPause()
        if (registry.currentState.isAtLeast(Lifecycle.State.STARTED)) registry.currentState = Lifecycle.State.CREATED
    }

    fun destroy() {
        if (destroyed) return
        destroyed = true
        launchers.forEach { it.unregister() }
        if (registry.currentState.isAtLeast(Lifecycle.State.CREATED)) registry.currentState = Lifecycle.State.DESTROYED
        onCleared()
    }

    @Composable
    fun Host(topInset: Dp) {
        val parent = LocalLifecycleOwner.current
        DisposableEffect(this, parent) {
            follow(parent)
            onDispose { unfollow(parent) }
        }
        Content(topInset)
        MenuSheet(menuSheet)
        dialogs.current?.let { DialogContent(it) }
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/extensions/panel/SensorPanel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.extensions.panel

import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.hardware.SensorManager
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableIntStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CalibrationDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelDialog

abstract class SensorPanel(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity) {
    private var calibrationShown = false
    private var accelerometerAccuracy by mutableIntStateOf(-1)
    private var magnetometerAccuracy by mutableIntStateOf(-1)

    private data object Calibration : PanelDialog

    protected fun showSensorError() = showError(getString(R.string.sensor_error))

    protected fun showCalibration() = dialogs.show(Calibration)

    protected fun checkCalibration(type: Int, accuracy: Int) {
        when (type) {
            Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER -> accelerometerAccuracy = accuracy
            Sensor.TYPE_MAGNETIC_FIELD -> magnetometerAccuracy = accuracy
        }
        if (!calibrationShown && accuracy <= SensorManager.SENSOR_STATUS_ACCURACY_LOW) {
            calibrationShown = true
            showCalibration()
        }
    }

    @Composable
    override fun DialogContent(dialog: PanelDialog) {
        if (dialog == Calibration) CalibrationDialog(accelerometerAccuracy, magnetometerAccuracy, dialogs::dismiss) else super.DialogContent(dialog)
    }
}

        localized?.let { (code, context) -> if (code == language) return context }

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/location/AndroidLocationSource.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.location

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.location.LocationManager
import android.os.Looper
import androidx.core.content.getSystemService
import androidx.core.location.LocationListenerCompat
import androidx.core.location.LocationManagerCompat
import androidx.core.location.LocationRequestCompat
import app.simple.positional.util.PermissionUtils
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.awaitClose
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.callbackFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.distinctUntilChanged
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.emptyFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flatMapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.onStart

class AndroidLocationSource(private val context: Context) : LocationSource {
    @OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
    override fun events(): Flow<LocationEvent> {
        val manager = context.getSystemService<LocationManager>() ?: return emptyFlow()
        if (!PermissionUtils.checkPermission(context)) return emptyFlow()
        val fine = PermissionUtils.hasFineLocation(context)
        return context.providerChanges()
            .onStart { emit(Unit) }
            .map { bestProvider(fine, manager::isProviderEnabled) }
            .distinctUntilChanged()
            .flatMapLatest { provider -> updates(manager, provider).onStart { emit(LocationEvent.Provider(provider)) } }
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private fun updates(manager: LocationManager, provider: String?): Flow<LocationEvent> = if (provider == null) emptyFlow() else callbackFlow {
        manager.getLastKnownLocation(provider)?.let { trySend(it.asLastKnown()) }
        val listener = LocationListenerCompat { trySend(LocationEvent.Fix(it)) }
        LocationManagerCompat.requestLocationUpdates(manager, provider, request, listener, Looper.getMainLooper())
        awaitClose { LocationManagerCompat.removeUpdates(manager, listener) }
    }

    companion object {
        private val request = LocationRequestCompat.Builder(LocationSource.INTERVAL)
            .setQuality(LocationRequestCompat.QUALITY_HIGH_ACCURACY)
            .setMinUpdateIntervalMillis(LocationSource.INTERVAL)
            .build()

        fun bestProvider(fine: Boolean, enabled: (String) -> Boolean): String? = when {
            fine && enabled(LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER) -> LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER
            enabled(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER) -> LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER
            fine -> LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER
            else -> null
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/location/FusedLocationSource.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.location

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Looper
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsLabels
import app.simple.positional.util.PermissionUtils
import com.google.android.gms.location.LocationCallback
import com.google.android.gms.location.LocationRequest
import com.google.android.gms.location.LocationResult
import com.google.android.gms.location.LocationServices
import com.google.android.gms.location.Priority
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.awaitClose
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.callbackFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.emptyFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.merge

class FusedLocationSource(private val context: Context) : LocationSource {
    override fun events(): Flow<LocationEvent> =
        if (!PermissionUtils.checkPermission(context)) emptyFlow() else merge(
            context.providerChanges().map { LocationEvent.Provider(SettingsLabels.PROVIDER_FUSED) },
            fixes(),
        )

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private fun fixes(): Flow<LocationEvent> = callbackFlow {
        val client = LocationServices.getFusedLocationProviderClient(context)
        var fresh = false
        val callback = object : LocationCallback() {
            override fun onLocationResult(result: LocationResult) {
                val location = result.lastLocation ?: return
                fresh = true
                trySend(LocationEvent.Fix(location))
            }
        }
        client.lastLocation.addOnSuccessListener { last -> if (!fresh && last != null) trySend(last.asLastKnown()) }
        client.requestLocationUpdates(request, callback, Looper.getMainLooper())
        awaitClose { client.removeLocationUpdates(callback) }
    }

    private companion object {
        val request: LocationRequest = LocationRequest.Builder(Priority.PRIORITY_HIGH_ACCURACY, LocationSource.INTERVAL)
            .setMinUpdateIntervalMillis(LocationSource.INTERVAL)
            .build()
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/location/LocationSource.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.location

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.location.Location
import android.location.LocationManager
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsLabels
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.awaitClose
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.callbackFlow

sealed interface LocationEvent {
    data class Fix(val location: Location) : LocationEvent
    data class Provider(val name: String?) : LocationEvent
}

fun interface LocationSource {
    fun events(): Flow<LocationEvent>

    companion object {
        const val INTERVAL = 1_000L
        const val LAST_KNOWN = "Last Location"

        fun of(context: Context, provider: String): LocationSource =
            if (provider == SettingsLabels.PROVIDER_FUSED) FusedLocationSource(context) else AndroidLocationSource(context)
    }
}

internal fun Location.asLastKnown(): LocationEvent = LocationEvent.Fix(apply { provider = LocationSource.LAST_KNOWN })

internal fun Context.providerChanges(): Flow<Unit> = callbackFlow {
    val receiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
        override fun onReceive(context: Context?, intent: Intent?) {
            trySend(Unit)
        }
    }
    val context = this@providerChanges
    ContextCompat.registerReceiver(context, receiver, IntentFilter(LocationManager.PROVIDERS_CHANGED_ACTION), ContextCompat.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED)
    awaitClose { context.unregisterReceiver(receiver) }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/math/Angle.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import kotlin.math.acos
import kotlin.math.sqrt

object Angle {
    fun getAngle(xTouch: Double, yTouch: Double, dialerWidth: Float, dialerHeight: Float): Float {
        val centerX = dialerWidth / 2.0F
        val centerY = dialerHeight / 2.0F
        val x2 = xTouch - centerX
        val y2 = yTouch - centerY
        val d1 = sqrt((centerY * centerY).toDouble())
        val d2 = sqrt(x2 * x2 + y2 * y2)
        return if (xTouch >= centerX) {
            Math.toDegrees(acos(-centerY * y2 / (d1 * d2))).toFloat()
        } else
            (360 - Math.toDegrees(acos(-centerY * y2 / (d1 * d2)))).toFloat()
    }

    fun Float.normalizeEulerAngle(inverseResult: Boolean): Float {
        var normalized = this % 360
        if (normalized < 0) normalized += 360
        return if (inverseResult) normalized - 360F else normalized
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/math/ClockMotion.kt

package app.simple.positional.math

enum class ClockMotion(
    val key: String,
    val intervalMillis: Long,
    val fractional: Boolean,
    val animated: Boolean,
    val inertia: Float = RotationPhysics.INERTIA,
    val damping: Float = RotationPhysics.DAMPING,
    val magnetic: Float = RotationPhysics.MAGNETIC,
) {
    TICK("tick", 1000L, false, false),
    MECHANICAL("mechanical", 240L, true, false),
    SMOOTH("smooth", 0L, true, false),
    TICK_SMOOTH("tick_smooth", 1000L, true, true, damping = 5f),
    OSCILLATE("oscillate", 1000L, true, true, inertia = 1f, magnetic = 25000f);

    companion object {
        fun of(key: String?): ClockMotion = entries.firstOrNull { it.key == key } ?: TICK
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/math/CompassAzimuth.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import android.view.Surface
import android.view.WindowManager
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.normalizeAngle
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.toDegrees
import kotlin.math.atan2

object CompassAzimuth {
    fun adjustForRotation(azimuth: Float, rotation: Int): Float = when (rotation) {
        Surface.ROTATION_90 -> azimuth - 270f
        Surface.ROTATION_180 -> azimuth - 180f
        Surface.ROTATION_270 -> azimuth - 90f
        else -> azimuth
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    fun adjustAzimuthForDisplayRotation(azimuth: Float, windowManager: WindowManager): Float =
        adjustForRotation(azimuth, windowManager.defaultDisplay.rotation)

    fun calculate(gravity: Vector3, magneticField: Vector3): Float {
        val normGravity = gravity.normalize()
        val normMagField = magneticField.normalize()
        val east = normMagField.cross(normGravity)
        val normEast = east.normalize()

        if (gravity.magnitude() * magneticField.magnitude() * east.magnitude() < 0.1f) {
            return 0F
        }

        val north = normMagField.minus(normGravity * normGravity.dot(normMagField))
        val normNorth = north.normalize()

        val sin = normEast.y - normNorth.x
        val cos = normEast.x + normNorth.y
        val azimuth = if (!(sin == 0f && sin == cos)) atan2(sin, cos) else 0f

        return if (azimuth.isNaN() || !azimuth.isFinite()) 0f else normalizeAngle(azimuth.toDegrees())
    }

    fun calculate(gravity: Vector3, magneticField: Vector3, windowManager: WindowManager): Float {
        return adjustAzimuthForDisplayRotation(calculate(gravity, magneticField), windowManager)
    }

    fun inverse(azimuth: Float): Float {
        return azimuth + 180F
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/math/GeoDistance.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import kotlin.math.asin
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.sin
import kotlin.math.sqrt

object GeoDistance {
    private const val EARTH_RADIUS = 6_371_008.8

    fun meters(fromLatitude: Double, fromLongitude: Double, toLatitude: Double, toLongitude: Double): Double {
        val dLat = Math.toRadians(toLatitude - fromLatitude)
        val dLon = Math.toRadians(toLongitude - fromLongitude)
        val a = sin(dLat / 2).let { it * it } +
                cos(Math.toRadians(fromLatitude)) * cos(Math.toRadians(toLatitude)) * sin(dLon / 2).let { it * it }
        return 2 * EARTH_RADIUS * asin(sqrt(a.coerceIn(0.0, 1.0)))
    }

    fun path(points: List<Pair<Double, Double>>): Double =
        points.zipWithNext { a, b -> meters(a.first, a.second, b.first, b.second) }.sum()
}

class MovementThreshold(private val meters: Double) {
    private var last: Pair<Double, Double>? = null

    fun accept(latitude: Double, longitude: Double): Boolean {
        val previous = last
        if (previous != null && GeoDistance.meters(previous.first, previous.second, latitude, longitude) < meters) return false
        last = latitude to longitude
        return true
    }

    fun reset() {
        last = null
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/math/Latency.kt
