package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import android.location.Location
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.callbacks.BottomBarHost
import app.simple.positional.decorations.maps.MeasureMap
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.PanelController
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapsCallbacks
import app.simple.positional.math.GeoDistance
import app.simple.positional.model.MeasurePoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MeasurePreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MeasureActions
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MeasureScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MeasureState
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.NameNoteDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelMenus
import app.simple.positional.util.DistanceText
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension.getLocationStatus
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationPrompt
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.MapTools
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.MeasureViewModel
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterNotNull
import app.simple.positional.model.Measure as MeasureModel

class Measure(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity), MeasureActions, MapsCallbacks {
    private val state = MeasureState()
    private val measureViewModel = ViewModelProvider(activity)[MeasureViewModel::class.java]
    private val maps: MeasureMap
    private var location: Location? = null
    private var shown: Long? = null

    init {
        bind(measureViewModel.tools, ::applySettings)
        maps = MeasureMap.create(activity).apply { setCallbacks(this@Measure) }
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        val summary by measureViewModel.summary.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
        MeasureScreen(state, summary, maps.view, topInset, this)
    }

    init {
        owner.lifecycle.addObserver(maps.lifecycleObserver)
        if (state.fullScreen) bottomBar(visible = false)
        observe(locationModel.provider) { state.enabled = getLocationStatus(activity) }
        observe(locationModel.location.filterNotNull()) {
            location = it
            state.fixed = true
            state.enabled = getLocationStatus(activity)
            maps.updateLocation(it)
        }
        observe(measureViewModel.measure, ::showMeasure)
    }

    private fun showMeasure(measure: MeasureModel?) {
        if (measure == null) {
            clearMeasure()
            return
        }
        if (measure.dateCreated != shown || maps.points != measure.points) {
            maps.showMeasure(measure)
            state.current = ""
        }
        shown = measure.dateCreated
        state.selected = true
        state.name = measure.name.orEmpty()
        state.hasPoints = measure.points.isNotEmpty()
    }

    override fun onMapClicked() {
        if (isLandscapeOrientation) return
        state.fullScreen = !state.fullScreen
        bottomBar(!state.fullScreen)
    }

    override fun onLineAdded(measurePoint: MeasurePoint) {
        measureViewModel.addMeasurePoint(measurePoint)
    }

    override fun onLineDeleted(measurePoint: MeasurePoint?) {
        measureViewModel.removeMeasurePoint(measurePoint)
    }

    override fun onCameraDistance(latLng: LatLng?) {
        val last = maps.points.lastOrNull()
        state.current = if (latLng == null || last == null) "" else {
            distance(GeoDistance.meters(last.latitude, last.longitude, latLng.latitude, latLng.longitude))
        }
    }

    private fun distance(meters: Double) = DistanceText.format(activity, meters, MainPreferences.isMetric())

    private fun clearMeasure() {
        shown = null
        maps.clear()
        state.selected = false
        state.hasPoints = false
        state.current = ""
    }

    private fun applySettings(tools: MapTools) {
        state.toolsLeft = tools.toolsLeft
        state.compass = tools.compass
        state.wrapped = tools.wrapped
    }

    private fun bottomBar(visible: Boolean) {
        (activity as? BottomBarHost)?.showBottomBar(visible)
    }

    override fun addMeasure() = dialogs.show(NewMeasure)

    @Composable
    override fun DialogContent(dialog: PanelDialog) {
        if (dialog == NewMeasure) {
            NameNoteDialog(stringResource(R.string.create_new), isValid = { it.isNotEmpty() }, onDismiss = dialogs::dismiss) { name, note ->
                measureViewModel.addMeasure(name, note)
                dialogs.dismiss()
            }
        } else {
            super.DialogContent(dialog)
        }
    }

    private data object NewMeasure : PanelDialog

    override fun measures() = navigate(Routes.MEASURES)

    override fun menu() = menuSheet.show { PanelMenus.measure() }

    override fun locate(close: Boolean) {
        if (!getLocationStatus(activity)) {
            LocationPrompt.displayLocationSettingsRequest(activity)
            return
        }
        val current = location ?: return
        if (close) {
            maps.resetCamera(CLOSE_ZOOM)
        } else {
            maps.moveTo(current.latitude, current.longitude, MeasurePreferences.getMapZoom())
        }
    }

    override fun addPoint() = maps.addPoint()

    override fun removeLast() = maps.removeLast()

    override fun wrap() = MeasurePreferences.invertPolylinesWrapped()

    override fun toggleCompass() = MeasurePreferences.invertCompassRotation()

    override fun toggleToolsSide() = MeasurePreferences.invertToolsGravity()

    private companion object {
        const val CLOSE_ZOOM = 18F
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.Settings as AndroidSettings
import android.view.WindowManager
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import androidx.core.net.toUri
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import app.simple.positional.constants.HtmlPage
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.PanelController
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsActions
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.SettingsViewModel

class Settings(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity), SettingsActions {
    private val viewModel = ViewModelProvider(activity)[SettingsViewModel::class.java]
    private var appSettingsOpened = false
    private val permissionLauncher = register(ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()) { result ->
        if (result.values.any { !it } && !appSettingsOpened) {
            appSettingsOpened = true
            startActivity(Intent(AndroidSettings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS).apply {
                data = Uri.fromParts("package", activity.packageName, null)
            })
        }
        viewModel.refresh()
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        val state by viewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
        SettingsScreen(state, this, topInset)
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        appSettingsOpened = false
        viewModel.refresh()
    }

    override fun requestPermission() {
        permissionLauncher.launch(arrayOf(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION))
    }

    override fun setThemeMode(mode: Int) {
        viewModel.setThemeMode(mode)
        activity.recreate()
    }

    override fun openAccent() = navigate(Routes.ACCENT)

    override fun setIcon(index: Int) = viewModel.setIcon(index)

    override fun setCornerRadius(value: Int) {
        if (viewModel.setCornerRadius(value)) activity.recreate()
    }

    override fun setSkipSplashScreen(enabled: Boolean) = viewModel.setSkipSplashScreen(enabled)

    override fun openArts() = navigate(Routes.ARTS)

    override fun setMetric(metric: Boolean) = viewModel.setMetric(metric)

    override fun setCoordinatesFormat(format: Int) = viewModel.setCoordinatesFormat(format)

    override fun setLocationProvider(provider: String) = viewModel.setLocationProvider(provider)

    override fun setMapSource(source: Int) = viewModel.setMapSource(source)

    override fun openCustomLocations() = navigate(Routes.CUSTOM_LOCATIONS)

    override fun setCustomLocation(enabled: Boolean) {
        if (enabled) openCustomLocations() else viewModel.disableCustomLocation()
    }

    override fun setKeepScreenOn(enabled: Boolean) {
        viewModel.setKeepScreenOn(enabled)
        if (enabled) {
            activity.window.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)
        } else {
            activity.window.clearFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)
        }
    }

    override fun setLanguage(code: String) {
        if (viewModel.setLanguage(code)) activity.recreate()
    }

    override fun openLegal(page: Int) {
        HtmlPage.forTitle(page)?.let(::openPage)
    }

    override fun openReleases() = openUrl("$REPOSITORY_URL/releases")

    override fun openRepository() = openUrl(REPOSITORY_URL)

    override fun openIssues() = openUrl("$REPOSITORY_URL/issues")

    private fun openUrl(url: String) {
        runCatching { startActivity(Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, url.toUri())) }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val REPOSITORY_URL = "https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional"
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.PanelController
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.Choice
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ChoiceDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ClockNeedleDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ClockScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ClockState
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.LocationSummaryDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelMenus
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.ClockSettings
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.ClockViewModel

class Time(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity) {
    private val state = ClockState()
    private val viewModel = panelModel(ClockViewModel::class, ::ClockViewModel)

    init {
        bind(viewModel.settings, ::applySettings)
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        val zone by viewModel.zone.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
        val time by viewModel.time.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
        val sky by viewModel.sky.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
        ClockScreen(
            state,
            zone,
            time,
            sky,
            topInset,
            onTimezone = { navigate(Routes.TIMEZONE) },
            onMenu = ::showMenu,
            onLocation = { dialogs.show(ClockDialog.Location) },
        )
    }

    private fun showMenu() = menuSheet.show {
        PanelMenus.clock(onNeedles = { dialogs.show(ClockDialog.Needles) }, onMotionType = { dialogs.show(ClockDialog.Motion) })
    }

    @Composable
    override fun DialogContent(dialog: PanelDialog) {
        when (dialog) {
            ClockDialog.Location -> {
                val position by viewModel.position.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
                LocationSummaryDialog(position.latitude, position.longitude, state.customLocation, dialogs::dismiss)
            }
            ClockDialog.Needles -> ClockNeedleDialog(ClockPreferences.getClockNeedleTheme(), dialogs::dismiss) { ClockPreferences.setClockNeedleTheme(it) }
            ClockDialog.Motion -> ChoiceDialog(
                stringResource(R.string.motion_type),
                motionTypes.map { (value, label) -> Choice(value, stringResource(label)) },
                ClockPreferences.getMovementType(),
                onDismiss = dialogs::dismiss,
            ) {
                ClockPreferences.setMovementType(it)
                dialogs.dismiss()
            }
            else -> super.DialogContent(dialog)
        }
    }

    private enum class ClockDialog : PanelDialog { Location, Needles, Motion }

    private fun applySettings(settings: ClockSettings) {
        state.motion = settings.motion
        state.face24 = settings.face24
        state.needles = settings.needles
        state.pin = settings.pin
        state.customLocation = settings.customLocation
    }

    private companion object {
        val motionTypes = listOf(
            "smooth" to R.string.motion_type_smooth,
            "tick" to R.string.motion_type_tick,
            "oscillate" to R.string.tick_oscillate,
            "tick_smooth" to R.string.tick_smooth,
            "mechanical" to R.string.mechanical,
        )
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import android.content.ClipData
import android.content.ClipboardManager
import android.location.Location
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableIntStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.rememberSaveable
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import androidx.core.content.getSystemService
import androidx.activity.viewModels
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.callbacks.BottomBarHost
import app.simple.positional.decorations.maps.TrailMap
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.PanelController
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapsCallbacks
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailEntry
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailPoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.TrailPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.TrailActions
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.TrailPointItem
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.TrailScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.TrailState
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MarkerIconRow
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.NameNoteDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelMenus
import app.simple.positional.constants.HtmlPage
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension.getLocationStatus
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationPrompt
import app.simple.positional.util.MapIntents
import app.simple.positional.util.TrailNames
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.MapTools
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.TrailDataViewModel
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.TrailsViewModel
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterNotNull

class Trail(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity), TrailActions, MapsCallbacks {
    private val state = TrailState()
    private val trailData: TrailDataViewModel by activity.viewModels()
    private val trails: TrailsViewModel by activity.viewModels()
    private val maps: TrailMap
    private var location: Location? = null
    private var pending: Triple<Double, Double, Float>? = null

    init {
        bind(trailData.tools, ::applySettings)
        maps = TrailMap.create(activity).apply { setCallbacks(this@Trail) }
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        val info by trailData.info.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
        TrailScreen(state, info, maps.view, topInset, isLandscapeOrientation, this)
    }

    init {
        owner.lifecycle.addObserver(maps.lifecycleObserver)
        if (state.fullScreen) bottomBar(visible = false)

        observe(locationModel.location.filterNotNull()) {
            location = it
            state.fixed = true
            state.enabled = getLocationStatus(activity)
            maps.updateLocation(it)
        }
        observe(locationModel.provider) { state.enabled = getLocationStatus(activity) }
        observe(trailData.points, maps::showPoints)
    }

    private fun applySettings(tools: MapTools) {
        state.toolsLeft = tools.toolsLeft
        state.compass = tools.compass
        state.wrapped = tools.wrapped
    }

    private fun bottomBar(visible: Boolean) {
        (activity as? BottomBarHost)?.showBottomBar(visible)
    }

    private fun requestMarker(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, accuracy: Float) {
        if (TrailPreferences.getCurrentTrail().isEmpty()) {
            addTrail()
            return
        }
        pending = Triple(latitude, longitude, accuracy)
        state.pickingMarker = true
    }

    override fun onMapClicked() {
        if (isLandscapeOrientation) return
        state.fullScreen = !state.fullScreen
        bottomBar(!state.fullScreen)
    }

    override fun onMapLongClicked(latLng: LatLng) = requestMarker(latLng.latitude, latLng.longitude, 0f)

    override fun onLineDeleted(trailPoint: TrailPoint?) = trailData.delete(trailPoint)

    override fun trails() = navigate(Routes.TRAILS)

    override fun addTrail() = dialogs.show(NewTrail)

    override fun menu() = menuSheet.show { PanelMenus.trail { openPage(HtmlPage.TRAIL_HELP) } }

    override fun locate(close: Boolean) {
        if (!getLocationStatus(activity)) {
            LocationPrompt.displayLocationSettingsRequest(activity)
            return
        }
        val current = location ?: return
        if (close) {
            maps.resetCamera(CLOSE_ZOOM)
        } else {
            maps.moveTo(current.latitude, current.longitude, TrailPreferences.getMapZoom())
        }
    }

    override fun addPoint() {
        val current = location
        if (current == null && TrailPreferences.getCurrentTrail().isNotEmpty()) {
            Toast.makeText(activity, R.string.location_not_available, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            return
        }
        requestMarker(current?.latitude ?: 0.0, current?.longitude ?: 0.0, current?.accuracy ?: 0f)
    }

    override fun removeLast() = maps.removeLast()

    override fun wrap() = maps.toggleWrap()

    override fun toggleCompass() = TrailPreferences.setCompassRotation(!TrailPreferences.isCompassRotation())

    override fun toggleToolsSide() = TrailPreferences.setToolsGravityToLeft(!TrailPreferences.isToolsGravityToLeft())

    override fun sheet(open: Boolean) {
        state.sheet = open
    }

    override fun markerPicked(icon: Int, quick: Boolean) {
        val (latitude, longitude, accuracy) = pending ?: return
        pending = null
        if (quick) {
            save(TrailPoint(latitude, longitude, System.currentTimeMillis(), icon, null, null, accuracy))
        } else {
            dialogs.show(NewMarker(icon, latitude, longitude, accuracy, System.currentTimeMillis()))
        }
    }

    @Composable
    override fun DialogContent(dialog: PanelDialog) {
        when (dialog) {
            NewTrail -> NewTrailDialog()
            is NewMarker -> NewMarkerDialog(dialog)
            else -> super.DialogContent(dialog)
        }
    }

    @Composable
    private fun NewTrailDialog() {
        val existing by trails.trails.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
        NameNoteDialog(
            stringResource(R.string.enter_trail_name),
            isValid = { name -> TrailNames.isValid(name, existing.orEmpty().mapNotNull { it.trailName }) },
            onDismiss = dialogs::dismiss,
            initial = TrailPreferences.getLastTrailName() to TrailPreferences.getLastTrailNote(),
            onDraft = { name, note ->
                TrailPreferences.setLastTrailName(name)
                TrailPreferences.setLastTrailNote(note)
            },
        ) { name, note ->
            trails.addTrail(TrailEntry(System.currentTimeMillis(), name, note))
            TrailPreferences.setLastTrailName("")
            TrailPreferences.setLastTrailNote("")
            dialogs.dismiss()
        }
    }

    @Composable
    private fun NewMarkerDialog(marker: NewMarker) {
        var selected by rememberSaveable(marker) { mutableIntStateOf(marker.icon) }
        NameNoteDialog(
            stringResource(R.string.enter_marker_name),
            isValid = { true },
            onDismiss = dialogs::dismiss,
            initial = TrailPreferences.getLastMarkerName() to TrailPreferences.getLastMarkerNote(),
            onDraft = { name, note ->
                TrailPreferences.setLastMarkerName(name)
                TrailPreferences.setLastMarkerNote(note)
            },
            extra = { MarkerIconRow(selected) { selected = it } },
        ) { name, note ->
            save(TrailPoint(marker.latitude, marker.longitude, marker.time, selected, note.ifEmpty { null }, name.ifEmpty { null }, marker.accuracy))
            TrailPreferences.setLastMarkerName("")
            TrailPreferences.setLastMarkerNote("")
            dialogs.dismiss()
        }
    }

    private data object NewTrail : PanelDialog

    private data class NewMarker(val icon: Int, val latitude: Double, val longitude: Double, val accuracy: Float, val time: Long) : PanelDialog

    private fun save(point: TrailPoint) {
        trailData.save(point)
        if (!TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()) {
            maps.moveTo(point.latitude, point.longitude, TrailPreferences.getMapZoom())
        }
    }

    override fun focus(item: TrailPointItem) {
        state.sheet = false
        maps.moveTo(item.point.latitude, item.point.longitude, FOCUS_ZOOM)
    }

    override fun delete(item: TrailPointItem) = trailData.delete(item.point)

    override fun copy(item: TrailPointItem) {
        val text = listOf(item.name, item.note, "geo:${item.point.latitude},${item.point.longitude}", item.date).joinToString("\n\n")
        activity.getSystemService<ClipboardManager>()?.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText(item.name, text))
    }

    override fun navigate(item: TrailPointItem) = MapIntents.navigate(activity, item.point.latitude, item.point.longitude)

    override fun share(item: TrailPointItem) = MapIntents.open(activity, item.point.latitude, item.point.longitude)

    private companion object {
        const val CLOSE_ZOOM = 18F
        const val FOCUS_ZOOM = 15F
    }
}

object AccentThemes {
    private val themes = listOf(
        R.color.positional to R.style.Positional,
        R.color.blue to R.style.Blue,
        R.color.blueGrey to R.style.BlueGrey,
        R.color.darkBlue to R.style.DarkBlue,
        R.color.red to R.style.Red,
        R.color.green to R.style.Green,
        R.color.orange to R.style.Orange,
        R.color.purple to R.style.Purple,
        R.color.yellow to R.style.Yellow,
        R.color.caribbeanGreen to R.style.CaribbeanGreen,
        R.color.persianGreen to R.style.PersianGreen,
        R.color.amaranth to R.style.Amaranth,
        R.color.indian_red to R.style.IndianRed,
        R.color.light_coral to R.style.LightCoral,
        R.color.pink_flare to R.style.PinkFlare,
        R.color.makeup_tan to R.style.MakeupTan,
        R.color.egg_yellow to R.style.EggYellow,
        R.color.medium_green to R.style.MediumGreen,
        R.color.olive to R.style.Olive,
        R.color.copperfield to R.style.Copperfield,
        R.color.mineral_green to R.style.MineralGreen,
        R.color.lochinvar to R.style.Lochinvar,
        R.color.beach_grey to R.style.BeachGrey,

        if (MainPreferences.isMaterialYouAccentColor() && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) return R.style.MaterialYou
        val accent = MainPreferences.getAccentColor()
        return themes.firstOrNull { context.getColor(it.first) == accent }?.second
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.ComponentName
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import app.simple.positional.R

object AppIcons {
    private const val ALIAS_PREFIX = "app.simple.positional.activities.alias."

    val icons = listOf(
        "IconOneAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_default,
        "IconLegacyAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_legacy,
        "IconTwoAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_two,
        "IconThreeAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_three,
        "IconFourAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_four,
        "IconFiveAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_five,
        "IconSixAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_six,
        "IconSevenAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_seven,
        "IconEightAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_eight,
        "IconNineAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_nine,
        "IconTenAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_ten,
        "IconElevenAlias" to R.mipmap.ic_launcher_eleven,
    )

    fun componentName(alias: String) = ALIAS_PREFIX + alias

    fun current(context: Context): Int = icons.indexOfFirst { (alias, _) ->
        context.packageManager.getComponentEnabledSetting(ComponentName(context, componentName(alias))) ==
                PackageManager.COMPONENT_ENABLED_STATE_ENABLED
    }.coerceAtLeast(0)

    fun select(context: Context, index: Int) {
        icons.forEachIndexed { i, (alias, _) ->
            context.packageManager.setComponentEnabledSetting(
                ComponentName(context, componentName(alias)),
                if (i == index) PackageManager.COMPONENT_ENABLED_STATE_ENABLED else PackageManager.COMPONENT_ENABLED_STATE_DISABLED,
                PackageManager.DONT_KILL_APP,
            )
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/BitmapHelper.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.LinearGradient
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.PorterDuff
import android.graphics.PorterDuffXfermode
import android.graphics.Shader
import androidx.annotation.IntRange
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat

object BitmapHelper {
    fun Bitmap.addLinearGradient(array: IntArray): Bitmap {
        val updatedBitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(width, height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val canvas = Canvas(updatedBitmap)
        canvas.drawBitmap(this, 0f, 0f, null)
        val paint = Paint()
        paint.shader = LinearGradient(0f, 0f, 0f, height.toFloat(), array[0], array[1], Shader.TileMode.CLAMP)
        paint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), paint)
        return updatedBitmap
    }

    fun Int.toBitmap(context: Context, size: Int): Bitmap {
        val drawable = ContextCompat.getDrawable(context, this)
        val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(size, size, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        drawable?.setBounds(0, 0, size, size)
        drawable?.draw(Canvas(bitmap))
        return bitmap
    }

    fun Int.toBitmapKeepingSize(context: Context, incrementFactor: Int, alpha: Int): Bitmap {
        val drawable = ContextCompat.getDrawable(context, this)
        val intrinsicWidth = drawable!!.intrinsicWidth * incrementFactor
        val intrinsicHeight = drawable.intrinsicHeight * incrementFactor
        drawable.setBounds(0, 0, intrinsicWidth, intrinsicHeight)
        val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(intrinsicWidth, intrinsicHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        drawable.alpha = alpha
        drawable.draw(Canvas(bitmap))
        return bitmap
    }

    fun Int.toBitmapKeepingSize(context: Context, @IntRange(from = 1, to = 10) incrementFactor: Int): Bitmap {
        val drawable = ContextCompat.getDrawable(context, this)
        val intrinsicWidth = drawable!!.intrinsicWidth * incrementFactor
        val intrinsicHeight = drawable.intrinsicHeight * incrementFactor
        drawable.setBounds(0, 0, intrinsicWidth, intrinsicHeight)
        val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(intrinsicWidth, intrinsicHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        drawable.draw(Canvas(bitmap))
        return bitmap
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/ColorUtils.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import androidx.annotation.AttrRes
import androidx.annotation.ColorInt

object ColorUtils {
    @ColorInt
    fun Context.resolveAttrColor(@AttrRes attr: Int): Int {
        val a = theme.obtainStyledAttributes(intArrayOf(attr))
        return try {
            a.getColor(0, 0)
        } finally {
            a.recycle()
        }
    }

    fun Int.toHex(): String = String.format("#%06x", this and 0xffffff)
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/ContextUtils.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.content.res.Configuration
import android.os.LocaleList
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import java.util.Locale

object ContextUtils {
    fun wrap(base: Context, languageCode: String, applyNightMode: Boolean = true): Context {
        val locale = when {
            languageCode != "default" -> Locale.forLanguageTag(languageCode)
            LocaleHelper.isOneOfTraditionalChinese() -> Locale.forLanguageTag("zh-TW")
            else -> Locale.forLanguageTag(LocaleHelper.getSystemLanguageCode())
        }
        val configuration = Configuration(base.resources.configuration)
        val locales = LocaleList(locale)
        LocaleList.setDefault(locales)
        configuration.setLocales(locales)
        if (applyNightMode) {
            val night = if (NightMode.isNight(base)) Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES else Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_NO
            configuration.uiMode = (configuration.uiMode and Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK.inv()) or night
        }
        return base.createConfigurationContext(configuration)
    }

    fun language(): String = MainPreferences.getAppLanguage() ?: "default"
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/CoordinateFormats.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import gov.nasa.worldwind.geom.Angle
import gov.nasa.worldwind.geom.coords.MGRSCoord

object CoordinateFormats {
    fun sections(context: Context, latitude: Double, longitude: Double): List<Pair<String, List<InfoRow>>> {
        val lat = context.getString(R.string.gps_latitude)
        val lon = context.getString(R.string.gps_longitude)
        val utm = UTMConverter.getUTM(latitude, longitude)
        val mgrs = runCatching { MGRSCoord.fromLatLon(Angle.fromDegreesLatitude(latitude), Angle.fromDegreesLongitude(longitude)).toString() }
            .getOrDefault(context.getString(R.string.not_available))
        return listOf(
            "DD°MM'SS.SSS" to listOf(InfoRow(lat, DMSConverter.latitudeAsDMS(latitude, context)), InfoRow(lon, DMSConverter.longitudeAsDMS(longitude, context))),
            "DD°MM.MMM'" to listOf(InfoRow(lat, DMSConverter.latitudeAsDM(latitude, context)), InfoRow(lon, DMSConverter.longitudeAsDM(longitude, context))),
            "DD.DDD°" to listOf(InfoRow(lat, DMSConverter.latitudeAsDD(latitude)), InfoRow(lon, DMSConverter.longitudeAsDD(longitude))),
            "MGRS" to listOf(InfoRow(mgrs)),
            "UTM" to listOf(
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.utm_zone), utm.zone),
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.utm_easting), utm.easting),
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.utm_northing), utm.northing),
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.utm_meridian), utm.centralMeridian),
            ),
        )
    }

    fun text(sections: List<Pair<String, List<InfoRow>>>): String = sections.joinToString("\n\n") { (title, rows) ->
        (listOf(title) + rows.map { "${it.label} ${it.value}".trim() }).joinToString("\n")
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/DMSConverter.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.location.Location
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import kotlin.math.abs

object DMSConverter {
    private const val DD_DDDDDD_PLACES = 6

    fun getFormattedCoordinates(location: Location, context: Context): Array<String> {
        return when (MainPreferences.getCoordinatesFormat()) {
            0 -> {
                arrayOf(
                        latitudeAsDD(location.latitude),
                        longitudeAsDD(location.longitude)
                )
            }
            1 -> {
                arrayOf(
                        latitudeAsDM(location.latitude, context),
                        longitudeAsDM(location.longitude, context)
                )
            }
            2 -> {
                arrayOf(
                        latitudeAsDMS(location.latitude, context),
                        longitudeAsDMS(location.longitude, context)
                )
            }
            else -> {
                arrayOf(
                        latitudeAsDD(location.latitude),
                        longitudeAsDD(location.longitude)
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun getFormattedCoordinates(latLng: LatLng, context: Context): Array<String> {
        return when (MainPreferences.getCoordinatesFormat()) {
            0 -> {
                arrayOf(
                        latitudeAsDD(latLng.latitude),
                        longitudeAsDD(latLng.longitude)
                )
            }
            1 -> {
                arrayOf(
                        latitudeAsDM(latLng.latitude, context),
                        longitudeAsDM(latLng.longitude, context)
                )
            }
            2 -> {
                arrayOf(
                        latitudeAsDMS(latLng.latitude, context),
                        longitudeAsDMS(latLng.longitude, context)
                )
            }
            else -> {
                arrayOf(
                        latitudeAsDD(latLng.latitude),
                        longitudeAsDD(latLng.longitude)
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fun getFormattedLatitude(latitude: Double, context: Context): String {
        return when (MainPreferences.getCoordinatesFormat()) {
            0 -> {
                latitudeAsDD(latitude)
            }
            1 -> {
                latitudeAsDM(latitude, context)
            }
            2 -> {
                latitudeAsDMS(latitude, context)
            }
            else -> {
                latitudeAsDD(latitude)
            }
        }
    }

    fun getFormattedLongitude(longitude: Double, context: Context): String {
        return when (MainPreferences.getCoordinatesFormat()) {
            0 -> {
                longitudeAsDD(longitude)
            }
            1 -> {
                longitudeAsDM(longitude, context)
            }
            2 -> {
                longitudeAsDMS(longitude, context)
            }
            else -> {
                longitudeAsDD(longitude)
            }
        }
    }

    fun latitudeAsDMS(latitude: Double, context: Context): String {
        val direction = if (latitude < 0) context.resources.getString(R.string.south_S) else context.resources.getString(R.string.north_N)
        var strLatitude = toDMS(abs(latitude))
        strLatitude += " $direction"
        return strLatitude
    }

    fun longitudeAsDMS(longitude: Double, context: Context): String {
        val direction = if (longitude < 0) context.resources.getString(R.string.west_W) else context.resources.getString(R.string.east_E)
        var strLongitude = toDMS(abs(longitude))
        strLongitude += " $direction"
        return strLongitude
    }

    fun getDMS(latLng: LatLng, context: Context): String {
        return latitudeAsDMS(latLng.latitude, context) + " | " + longitudeAsDMS(latLng.longitude, context)
    }

    fun latitudeAsDD(latitude: Double): String {
        return if (latitude > 0)
            "${MathExtensions.round(latitude, DD_DDDDDD_PLACES)}°"
        else
            "${MathExtensions.round(latitude, DD_DDDDDD_PLACES)}°"
    }

    fun longitudeAsDD(longitude: Double): String {
        return if (longitude > 0)
            "${MathExtensions.round(longitude, DD_DDDDDD_PLACES)}°"
        else
            "${MathExtensions.round(longitude, DD_DDDDDD_PLACES)}°"
    }

    fun latitudeAsDM(lat: Double, context: Context): String {
        val ddmLat = replaceDelimiters(Location.convert(lat, Location.FORMAT_MINUTES))
        return if (lat >= 0.0) {
            "$ddmLat ${context.resources.getString(R.string.north_N)}"
        } else {
            "${ddmLat.replaceFirst("-".toRegex(), "")} ${context.resources.getString(R.string.south_S)}"
        }
    }

    fun longitudeAsDM(longitude: Double, context: Context): String {
        val ddLongitude = replaceDelimiters(Location.convert(longitude, Location.FORMAT_MINUTES))
        return if (longitude >= 0.0) {
            "$ddLongitude ${context.resources.getString(R.string.east_E)}"
        } else {
            "${ddLongitude.replaceFirst("-".toRegex(), "")} ${context.resources.getString(R.string.west_W)}"
        }
    }

    private fun replaceDelimiters(string: String): String {
        return string.replaceFirst(":".toRegex(), "° ").replaceFirst(":".toRegex(), "' ") + "\""
    }

    private fun toDMS(number: Double): String {
        val degree = kotlin.math.floor(number)
        val minutes = (number - kotlin.math.floor(number)) * 60.0
        val seconds = (minutes - kotlin.math.floor(minutes)) * 60.0

        return "%1\$d%2\$s %3\$d%4\$s %5\$.${2}f%6\$s".format(
                degree.toInt(), "°",
                minutes.toInt(), "'",
                seconds, "\""
        )
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/Direction.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import app.simple.positional.R

object Direction {
    private val codes = intArrayOf(
        R.string.north_N, R.string.north_east_NE, R.string.east_E, R.string.south_east_SE,
        R.string.south_S, R.string.south_west_SW, R.string.west_W, R.string.north_west_NW
    )

    private val names = intArrayOf(
        R.string.north, R.string.north_east, R.string.east, R.string.south_east,
        R.string.south, R.string.south_west, R.string.west, R.string.north_west
    )

    fun sector(azimuth: Double): Int = when {
        azimuth.isNaN() -> -1
        azimuth <= 10.0 || azimuth > 349.0 -> 0
        azimuth <= 80.0 -> 1
        azimuth <= 100.0 -> 2
        azimuth <= 170.0 -> 3
        azimuth <= 190.0 -> 4
        azimuth <= 260.0 -> 5
        azimuth <= 280.0 -> 6
        else -> 7
    }

    fun getDirectionCodeFromAzimuth(context: Context, azimuth: Double): String {
        return context.getString(codes.getOrElse(sector(azimuth)) { R.string.not_available })
    }

    fun getDirectionNameFromAzimuth(context: Context, azimuth: Double): String {
        return context.getString(names.getOrElse(sector(azimuth)) { R.string.not_available })
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/DistanceText.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round

object DistanceText {
    private const val KILOMETER = 1000.0
    private const val MILE = 1609.344
    private const val FEET_PER_METER = 3.28084

    fun parts(meters: Double, metric: Boolean): Pair<Double, Int> = when {
        metric && meters < KILOMETER -> round(meters, 2) to R.string.meter
        metric -> round(meters / KILOMETER, 2) to R.string.kilometer
        meters < MILE -> round(meters * FEET_PER_METER, 2) to R.string.feet
        else -> round(meters / MILE, 2) to R.string.miles
    }

    fun format(context: Context, meters: Double, metric: Boolean): String {
        val (value, @StringRes unit) = parts(meters, metric)
        return "$value ${context.getString(unit)}"
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/LocaleHelper.kt

package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.res.Resources
import android.view.View
import app.simple.positional.model.Locales
import java.util.Locale

object LocaleHelper {
    private var appLocale = Locale.getDefault()

    val localeList = arrayListOf(
            Locales("autoSystemLanguageString"  , "default"),
            Locales("English (US)", "en"),
            Locales("български", "bg"),
            Locales("Français", "fr"),
            Locales("Čeština", "cs"),
            Locales("हिन्दी", "hi"),
            Locales("Română", "ro"),
            Locales("Русский", "ru"),
            Locales("اردو", "ur"),
            Locales("Português (Brasil)", "pt-BR"),
            Locales("繁體中文 (Traditional Chinese)", "zh-TW"),
            Locales("Deutsch (German)", "de-DE"),
            Locales("Hungarian (Magyar)", "hu-HU"),
            Locales("Spanish (Español)", "es-ES"),
        Locales("Malayalam (മലയാളം)", "ml-IN"),
    )

    fun isOneOfTraditionalChinese(): Boolean {
        return with(getSystemLanguageCode()) {
            this == "zh" ||
                    this == "zh-HK" ||
                    this == "zh-MO" ||
                    this == "zh-TW" ||
                    this == "zh-Hant" ||
                    this == "zh-Hant-HK" ||
                    this == "zh-Hant-MO" ||
                    this == "zh-Hant-TW" ||
                    this == "zh-Hant-CN" ||
                    this == "zh-Hant-SG"
        }
    }

    fun getSystemLanguageCode(): String {
        return Resources.getSystem().configuration.locales[0].language
    }

    fun getAppLocale(): Locale {
        return synchronized(this) {
            appLocale
        }
    }

    fun setAppLocale(value: Locale) {
        synchronized(this) {
            appLocale = value
        }
    }

    fun Resources.isRTL(): Boolean {
        return configuration.layoutDirection == View.LAYOUT_DIRECTION_RTL
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/LocationExtension.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.location.Location
import android.location.LocationManager
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.sin

object LocationExtension {
    fun getLocationStatus(context: Context): Boolean {
        val locationManager = context.getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE) as LocationManager
        return if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= android.os.Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
            locationManager.isLocationEnabled
        } else {
            locationManager.isProviderEnabled(LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER) &&
            locationManager.isProviderEnabled(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER)
        }
    }

    fun isValidLongitude(longitude: Double): Boolean {
        return longitude >= -180 && longitude <= 180
    }

    fun isValidLatitude(latitude: Double): Boolean {
        return latitude >= -90 && latitude <= 90
    }

    fun measureDisplacement(list: Array<LatLng>): Float {
        val result = floatArrayOf(0f)
        var distance = 0F

        for (i in list.indices) {
            if (i < list.size - 1) {
                Location.distanceBetween(
                    list[i].latitude,
                    list[i].longitude,
                    list[i + 1].latitude,
                    list[i + 1].longitude,
                    result
                )

                distance += result[0]
            }
        }

        return distance
    }

    fun calculateBearingAngle(startLatitude: Double, startLongitude: Double, endLatitude: Double, endLongitude: Double): Double {
        val phi1 = Math.toRadians(startLatitude)
        val phi2 = Math.toRadians(endLatitude)
        val deltaLambda = Math.toRadians(endLongitude - startLongitude)
        val theta: Double =
            atan2(sin(deltaLambda) * cos(phi2), cos(phi1) * sin(phi2) - sin(phi1) * cos(phi2) * cos(deltaLambda))
        return Math.toDegrees(theta)
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/LocationPrompt.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.IntentSender
import android.util.Log
import com.google.android.gms.common.ConnectionResult
import com.google.android.gms.common.GoogleApiAvailability
import com.google.android.gms.common.api.ApiException
import com.google.android.gms.location.*

object LocationPrompt {
    private const val TAG = "Location"

    fun checkGooglePlayServices(context: Context): Boolean {
        return when (GoogleApiAvailability.getInstance().isGooglePlayServicesAvailable(context)) {
            ConnectionResult.SUCCESS -> {
                true
            }
            else -> {
                false
            }
        }
    }

    fun displayLocationSettingsRequest(activity: Activity) {
        val locationRequest = LocationRequest.Builder(Priority.PRIORITY_HIGH_ACCURACY, 10000).build()

        val builder = LocationSettingsRequest.Builder().addLocationRequest(locationRequest)
        builder.setAlwaysShow(true)

        LocationServices.getSettingsClient(activity).checkLocationSettings(builder.build()).addOnCompleteListener { task ->
            try {
                val response: LocationSettingsResponse = task.getResult(ApiException::class.java)
                Log.i(TAG, "${response.locationSettingsStates}: All location settings are satisfied.")
            } catch (e: ApiException) {
                when (e.statusCode) {
                    LocationSettingsStatusCodes.RESOLUTION_REQUIRED -> {
                        Log.i(TAG, "Location settings are not satisfied. Show the user a dialog to upgrade location settings ")
                        try {
                            e.status.startResolutionForResult(activity, 2)
                        } catch (e: IntentSender.SendIntentException) {
                            Log.i(TAG, "PendingIntent unable to execute request.")
                        }
                    }
                    LocationSettingsStatusCodes.SETTINGS_CHANGE_UNAVAILABLE -> {
                        Log.i(TAG, "Location settings are inadequate, and cannot be fixed here. Dialog not created.")
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/LocationShare.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round

object LocationShare {
    fun text(label: String, latitude: Double, longitude: Double, address: String): String {
        val lat = round(latitude, 6)
        val lon = round(longitude, 6)
        return "$label: $lat, $lon\n\n$address\n\nhttps://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=$lat,$lon\n\ngeo:$lat,$lon?q=$lat,$lon"
    }

    fun share(context: Context, latitude: Double, longitude: Double, address: String) {
        val text = text(context.getString(R.string.gps_coordinates), latitude, longitude, address)
        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).setType("text/plain").putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, text)
        context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, context.getString(R.string.share)))
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/MapIntents.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.net.toUri
import app.simple.positional.R

object MapIntents {
    private const val GOOGLE_MAPS = "com.google.android.apps.maps"

    fun navigate(context: Context, latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        val navigation = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, "google.navigation:q=$latitude,$longitude&mode=d".toUri()).setPackage(GOOGLE_MAPS)
        runCatching { context.startActivity(navigation) }
            .recoverCatching { context.startActivity(geo(latitude, longitude)) }
            .onFailure { Toast.makeText(context, R.string.error, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
    }

    fun open(context: Context, latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        runCatching { context.startActivity(geo(latitude, longitude)) }
            .onFailure { Toast.makeText(context, R.string.error, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
    }

    private fun geo(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, "geo:$latitude,$longitude?q=$latitude,$longitude".toUri())
}

object MapSource {

    const val GOOGLE = 1
    const val OSM = 2

    val choices = listOf(AUTO, GOOGLE, OSM)

    fun useOsm(choice: Int, hasGoogleKey: Boolean) = choice == OSM || (choice != GOOGLE && !hasGoogleKey)

    fun isOsm() = useOsm(MainPreferences.getMapSource(), BuildConfig.HAS_MAPS_KEY)
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import app.simple.positional.R

object MoonAngle {
    fun getMoonPhase(context: Context, moonAngle: Double): String {
        return if (moonAngle > -180.0 && moonAngle < -135.0) {
            context.getString(R.string.new_moon)
        } else if (moonAngle > -135 && moonAngle < -90) {
            context.getString(R.string.waxing_crescent)
        } else if (moonAngle > -90 && moonAngle < -45) {
            context.getString(R.string.first_quarter)
        } else if (moonAngle > -45 && moonAngle < 0) {
            context.getString(R.string.waxing_gibbous)
        } else if (moonAngle > 0 && moonAngle < 45) {
            context.getString(R.string.full_moon)
        } else if (moonAngle > 45 && moonAngle < 90) {
            context.getString(R.string.waning_gibbous)
        } else if (moonAngle > 90 && moonAngle < 135) {
            context.getString(R.string.third_quarter)
        } else if (moonAngle > 135 && moonAngle < 180) {
            context.getString(R.string.waning_crescent)
        } else {
            context.getString(R.string.not_available)
        }
    }

    fun getMoonPhaseGraphics(moonAngle: Double): Int {
        return when (moonAngle) {
            in -180.0..-168.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon00
            }
            in -168.1..-156.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon01
            }
            in -156.1..-144.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon02
            }
            in -144.1..-132.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon03
            }
            in -132.1..-120.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon04
            }
            in -120.1..-108.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon05
            }
            in -108.1..-96.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon06
            }
            in -96.1..-84.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon07
            }
            in -84.1..-72.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon08
            }
            in -72.1..-60.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon09
            }
            in -60.1..-48.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon10
            }
            in -48.1..-36.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon11
            }
            in -36.1..-24.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon12
            }
            in -24.1..-12.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon13
            }
            in -12.1..0.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon14
            }
            in 0.1..12.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon15
            }
            in 12.1..24.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon16
            }
            in 24.1..36.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon17
            }
            in 36.1..48.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon18
            }
            in 48.1..60.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon19
            }
            in 60.1..72.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon20
            }
            in 72.1..84.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon21
            }
            in 84.1..96.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon22
            }
            in 96.1..108.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon23
            }
            in 108.1..120.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon24
            }
            in 120.1..132.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon25
            }
            in 132.1..144.0 -> {

            }
            in 144.1..156.0 -> {

            }
            in 156.1..168.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon28
            }
            in 168.1..180.0 -> {
                R.drawable.moon29
            }
            else -> {
                R.drawable.moon15
            }
        }
    }

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/MoonTimeFormatter.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import java.text.ParseException
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter

object MoonTimeFormatter {
    fun formatMoonDate(zonedDateTime: ZonedDateTime): String {
        return try {
            zonedDateTime.format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(getMoonTimePattern()).withLocale(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale()))
        } catch (e: ParseException) {
            "N/A"
        }
    }

    private fun getMoonTimePattern(): String {
        return if (ClockPreferences.getDefaultClockTimeFormat()) {
            if (ClockPreferences.isUsingSecondsPrecision()) {
                "dd MMM, yyyy, hh:mm:ss a"
            } else {
                "dd MMM, yyyy, hh:mm a"
            }
        } else {
            if (ClockPreferences.isUsingSecondsPrecision()) {
                "dd MMM, yyyy, HH:mm:ss"
            } else {
                "dd MMM, yyyy, HH:mm"
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/NetworkCheck.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.os.Build

object NetworkCheck {
    @Suppress("deprecation")
    fun isNetworkAvailable(context: Context): Boolean {
        val connectivityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            val capabilities = connectivityManager.getNetworkCapabilities(connectivityManager.activeNetwork)
                    ?: return false
            return when {
                capabilities.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI) -> true
                capabilities.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_ETHERNET) -> true
                capabilities.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_CELLULAR) -> true
                else -> false
            }
        }
        else {
            val netInfo = connectivityManager.activeNetworkInfo
            return netInfo != null && netInfo.isConnectedOrConnecting
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/NightMode.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.content.res.Configuration
import android.os.PowerManager
import androidx.core.content.getSystemService
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import java.util.Calendar

object NightMode {
    const val FOLLOW_SYSTEM = -1
    const val NO = 1
    const val YES = 2
    const val AUTO_BATTERY = 3
    const val AUTO_TIME = 4
    private const val DAY_START = 6
    private const val DAY_END = 18

    fun resolve(mode: Int, byTime: Boolean, systemNight: Boolean, powerSave: Boolean, hour: Int): Boolean = when {
        byTime || mode == AUTO_TIME -> hour !in DAY_START until DAY_END
        mode == YES -> true
        mode == NO -> false
        mode == AUTO_BATTERY -> powerSave || systemNight
        else -> systemNight
    }

    fun isNight(context: Context): Boolean = resolve(
        MainPreferences.getTheme(),
        MainPreferences.isDayNightOn(),
        context.resources.configuration.uiMode and Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES,
        context.getSystemService<PowerManager>()?.isPowerSaveMode == true,
        Calendar.getInstance()[Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY],
    )
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import android.icu.text.MessageFormat

object Ordinal {
    fun Number?.toOrdinal(): String {
        if (this == null) {
            return "N/A"
        }

        val testArgs = arrayOf<Any>(this)
        val messageFormat = MessageFormat("{0,ordinal}", LocaleHelper.getAppLocale())

        return messageFormat.format(testArgs)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat

object PermissionUtils {
    fun checkPermission(context: Context): Boolean =
        granted(context, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION) || hasFineLocation(context)

    fun hasFineLocation(context: Context): Boolean = granted(context, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)

    private fun granted(context: Context, permission: String) =
        ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, permission) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/ReverseGeocoder.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import android.content.Context
import android.location.Address
import android.location.Geocoder
import android.os.Build
import androidx.annotation.RequiresApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.suspendCancellableCoroutine
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlin.coroutines.resume

object ReverseGeocoder {
    suspend fun addressLine(context: Context, latitude: Double, longitude: Double): String? {
        if (!Geocoder.isPresent()) return null
        val geocoder = Geocoder(context.applicationContext, LocaleHelper.getAppLocale())
        val addresses = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            geocoder.lookup(latitude, longitude)
        } else {
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                runCatching { geocoder.getFromLocation(latitude, longitude, 1) }.getOrNull().orEmpty()
            }
        }
        return addresses.firstOrNull()?.getAddressLine(0)
    }

    @RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU)
    private suspend fun Geocoder.lookup(latitude: Double, longitude: Double): List<Address> = suspendCancellableCoroutine { continuation ->
        runCatching {
            getFromLocation(latitude, longitude, 1, object : Geocoder.GeocodeListener {
                override fun onGeocode(addresses: MutableList<Address>) {
                    if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(addresses)
                }

                override fun onError(errorMessage: String?) {
                    if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(emptyList())
                }
            })
        }.onFailure { if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(emptyList()) }
    }
}

object TextViewUtils {
    fun String.capitalizeText(): String {
        return lowercase(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale()).split(" ").joinToString(" ") { word ->
            if (word.length <= 1) word else word.replaceFirstChar { if (it.isLowerCase()) it.titlecase(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale()) else it.toString() }
        }
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/TimeFormatter.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import java.text.DateFormat
import java.time.Duration
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.util.Date

object TimeFormatter {
    fun Long.formatDate(): String {
        return DateFormat.getDateTimeInstance().format(Date(this))
    }

    fun getDuration(start: Instant, end: Instant): String {
        val duration = Duration.between(start, end).abs()
        return String.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", duration.toHours(), duration.toMinutesPart(), duration.toSecondsPart())
    }

    fun getDuration(start: ZonedDateTime?, end: ZonedDateTime?): String {
        val duration = Duration.between(start, end).abs()
        return String.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", duration.toHours(), duration.toMinutesPart(), duration.toSecondsPart())
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import app.simple.positional.database.instances.TrailDatabase

object TrailNames {
    fun isValid(name: String, existing: Collection<String>): Boolean {
        val trimmed = name.trim()
        return trimmed.isNotEmpty() &&
            trimmed.none { it == '/' || it == '\\' } &&
            !trimmed.equals(TrailDatabase.NAME, ignoreCase = true) &&
            existing.none { it.equals(trimmed, ignoreCase = true) }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/Twilight.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import app.simple.positional.R
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.SunTimes
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZonedDateTime

enum class TwilightPhase(@StringRes val label: Int, val color: Long) {
    ASTRONOMICAL(R.string.twilight_astronomical_dusk, 0xFF1A237E),
    NAUTICAL(R.string.twilight_nautical_dusk, 0xFF0D47A1),
    BLUE_HOUR(R.string.twilight_blue_hour, 0xFF1565C0),
    CIVIL(R.string.twilight_civil_dusk, 0xFF1E88E5),
    VISUAL(R.string.twilight_visual, 0xFF42A5F5),
    HORIZON(R.string.twilight_horizon, 0xFF64B5F6),
    VISUAL_LOWER(R.string.twilight_visual_lower, 0xFF90CAF9),
    GOLDEN_HOUR(R.string.twilight_golden_hour, 0xFFFFCC80),
}

data class TwilightSpan(val phase: TwilightPhase, val start: ZonedDateTime?, val end: ZonedDateTime?, val range: Boolean)

data class TwilightDay(val morning: List<TwilightSpan>, val evening: List<TwilightSpan>)

object Twilight {
    private const val GOLDEN_HOUR_EDGE = -4.0
    private val angles = listOf(
        SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL, SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL, SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL,
        SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR, SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR, SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON,
        SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL, SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER, SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR,
    )

    fun compute(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, timezone: String, at: Instant = Instant.now(), altitude: Double = 0.0): TwilightDay {
        fun base() = SunTimes.compute().timezone(timezone).on(at).latitude(latitude).longitude(longitude).elevation(altitude)
        val times = angles.associateWith { base().twilight(it).execute() }
        val golden = base().twilight(GOLDEN_HOUR_EDGE).execute()
        fun rise(t: SunTimes.Twilight) = times.getValue(t).rise
        fun set(t: SunTimes.Twilight) = times.getValue(t).set
        fun span(phase: TwilightPhase, start: ZonedDateTime?, end: ZonedDateTime?) = TwilightSpan(phase, start, end, true)
        fun moment(phase: TwilightPhase, time: ZonedDateTime?) = TwilightSpan(phase, time, time, false)
        return TwilightDay(
            morning = listOf(
                span(TwilightPhase.ASTRONOMICAL, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL)),
                span(TwilightPhase.NAUTICAL, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL)),
                span(TwilightPhase.BLUE_HOUR, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR)),
                span(TwilightPhase.CIVIL, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON)),
                moment(TwilightPhase.VISUAL, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL)),
                moment(TwilightPhase.HORIZON, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON)),
                moment(TwilightPhase.VISUAL_LOWER, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER)),
                span(TwilightPhase.GOLDEN_HOUR, golden.rise, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR)),
            ),
            evening = listOf(
                span(TwilightPhase.GOLDEN_HOUR, set(SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR), golden.set),
                moment(TwilightPhase.VISUAL_LOWER, set(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER)),
                moment(TwilightPhase.HORIZON, set(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON)),
                moment(TwilightPhase.VISUAL, set(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL)),
                span(TwilightPhase.CIVIL, set(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON), set(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL)),
                span(TwilightPhase.BLUE_HOUR, set(SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR), set(SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR)),
                span(TwilightPhase.NAUTICAL, set(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL), set(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL)),
                span(TwilightPhase.ASTRONOMICAL, set(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL), set(SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL)),
            ),
        )
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/UTMConverter.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import gov.nasa.worldwind.geom.Angle
import gov.nasa.worldwind.geom.coords.UTMCoord

object UTMConverter {
    fun getUTM(latitude: Double, longitude: Double): UTM {
        return UTM(
                getUtmZone(latitude, longitude),
                getUtmEasting(latitude, longitude),
                getUtmNorthing(latitude, longitude),
                getUtmCoord(latitude, longitude).centralMeridian.toString()
        )
    }

    private fun getUtmEasting(lat: Double, lon: Double): String {
        return "${
            String.format(
                    LocaleHelper.getAppLocale(),
                    "%7.0f",
                    getUtmCoord(lat, lon).easting
            )
        }m E"
    }

    private fun getUtmCoord(lat: Double, lon: Double): UTMCoord {
        return UTMCoord.fromLatLon(Angle.fromDegreesLatitude(lat), Angle.fromDegreesLongitude(lon))
    }

    private fun getUtmZone(lat: Double, lon: Double): String {
        return "${getUtmCoord(lat, lon).zone}${getUtmLatBand(lat)}"
    }

    private fun getUtmLatBand(lat: Double): String {
        return when {
            -80.0 <= lat && lat < -72.0 -> "C"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < -64.0 -> "D"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < -56.0 -> "E"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < -48.0 -> "F"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < -40.0 -> "G"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < -32.0 -> "H"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < -24.0 -> "J"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < -16.0 -> "K"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < -8.0 -> "L"
            -72.0 <= lat && lat < 0.0 -> "M"
            00.0 <= lat && lat < 08.0 -> "N"
            08.0 <= lat && lat < 16.0 -> "P"
            16.0 <= lat && lat < 24.0 -> "Q"
            24.0 <= lat && lat < 32.0 -> "R"
            32.0 <= lat && lat < 40.0 -> "S"
            40.0 <= lat && lat < 48.0 -> "T"
            48.0 <= lat && lat < 56.0 -> "U"
            56.0 <= lat && lat < 64.0 -> "V"
            64.0 <= lat && lat < 72.0 -> "W"
            72.0 <= lat && lat < 84.0 -> "X"
            else -> ""
        }
    }

    private fun getUtmNorthing(lat: Double, lon: Double): String {
        val utmCoordinate = getUtmCoord(lat, lon)
        return "${
            String.format(
                    LocaleHelper.getAppLocale(),
                    "%7.0f",
                    utmCoordinate.northing
            )
        }m N"
    }

    open class UTM(
            val zone: String,

            val easting: String,

            val northing: String,

            val centralMeridian: String)
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/ClockViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.extensions.viewmodel.WrappedViewModel
import app.simple.positional.math.ClockMotion
import app.simple.positional.math.GeoDistance
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences.changes
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ClockTime
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SkyReport
import app.simple.positional.ui.panels.ClockReport
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.distinctUntilChanged
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterNotNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flatMapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import java.time.ZoneId

data class SkyPosition(val latitude: Double, val longitude: Double, val altitude: Double) {
    fun isNear(other: SkyPosition, meters: Double) = GeoDistance.meters(latitude, longitude, other.latitude, other.longitude) < meters
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class ClockViewModel(application: Application, location: LocationViewModel) : WrappedViewModel(application) {
    private val custom = preference(MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate, MainPreferences.latitude, MainPreferences.longitude, read = ::customPosition)

    val zone: StateFlow<ZoneId> = preference(ClockPreferences.timezone, read = ::storedZone)

    val settings: StateFlow<ClockSettings> = preference(
        ClockPreferences.clockNeedleMovementType,
        ClockPreferences.clockNeedle,
        ClockPreferences.is24HourFace,
        MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate,
        GPSPreferences.pinSkin,
    ) {
        ClockSettings(
            motion = ClockMotion.of(ClockPreferences.getMovementType()),
            face24 = ClockPreferences.isClockFace24Hour(),
            needles = ClockSettings.needles(ClockPreferences.getClockNeedleTheme()),
            pin = ClockSettings.pin(GPSPreferences.getPinSkin()),
            customLocation = MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate(),
        )
    }

    val position: StateFlow<SkyPosition> = custom
        .flatMapLatest { fixed ->
            if (fixed != null) flowOf(fixed) else location.location.filterNotNull().map { SkyPosition(it.latitude, it.longitude, it.altitude) }
        }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), customPosition() ?: storedPosition())

    val time: StateFlow<ClockTime> = combine(zone, changes(ClockPreferences.isUsingAmPm)) { zone, _ -> zone }
        .flatMapLatest { zone -> ticker(SECOND) { report().time(zone) } }
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), ClockTime())

    val sky: StateFlow<SkyReport?> = combine(
        position.distinctUntilChanged { old, new -> old.isNear(new, SKY_DISTANCE) },
        zone,
        changes(MainPreferences.unit, ClockPreferences.isUsingAmPm, ClockPreferences.isUsingSecondsPrecision),
    ) { position, zone, _ -> position to zone }
        .flatMapLatest { (position, zone) -> ticker(MINUTE) { report().sky(position.latitude, position.longitude, position.altitude, zone) } }
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), null)

    private fun report() = ClockReport(getContext())

    private fun <T> ticker(period: Long, value: () -> T): Flow<T> = flow {
        while (true) {
            emit(value())
            delay(period - System.currentTimeMillis() % period)
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
        const val SECOND = 1_000L
        const val MINUTE = 60_000L
        const val SKY_DISTANCE = 1_000.0

        fun storedZone(): ZoneId = runCatching { ZoneId.of(ClockPreferences.getTimeZone()) }.getOrDefault(ZoneId.systemDefault())

        fun customPosition(): SkyPosition? {
            if (!MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) return null
            val coordinates = MainPreferences.getCoordinates()
            return SkyPosition(coordinates[0].toDouble(), coordinates[1].toDouble(), 0.0)
        }

        fun storedPosition(): SkyPosition {
            val coordinates = MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()
            return SkyPosition(coordinates[0].toDouble(), coordinates[1].toDouble(), MainPreferences.getLastAltitude().toDouble())
        }
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.hardware.GeomagneticField
import android.location.Location
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.extensions.viewmodel.WrappedViewModel
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round
import app.simple.positional.preferences.CompassPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterNotNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn

class CompassViewModel(application: Application, location: LocationViewModel) : WrappedViewModel(application) {
    val field: StateFlow<List<InfoRow>> = location.location.filterNotNull()
        .map(::fieldRows)
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), emptyList())

    val settings: StateFlow<CompassSettings> = preference(
        CompassPreferences.direction_code,
        CompassPreferences.flowerBloom,
        CompassPreferences.flowerBloomTheme,
        CompassPreferences.dampingCoefficient,
        CompassPreferences.magneticCoefficient,
        CompassPreferences.rotationalInertia,
        CompassPreferences.usePhysicalProperties,
        CompassPreferences.useGimbalLock,
    ) {
        CompassSettings(
            gimbalLock = CompassPreferences.isUsingGimbalLock(),
            physics = CompassPreferences.isUsingPhysicalProperties(),
            directionCode = CompassPreferences.getDirectionCode(),
            bloom = CompassSettings.bloom(CompassPreferences.isFlowerBloomOn(), CompassPreferences.getFlowerBloomTheme()),
            inertia = CompassPreferences.getRotationalInertia(),
            damping = CompassPreferences.getDampingCoefficient(),
            magnetic = CompassPreferences.getMagneticCoefficient(),
        )
    }

    private fun fieldRows(location: Location): List<InfoRow> {
        val field = GeomagneticField(location.latitude.toFloat(), location.longitude.toFloat(), location.altitude.toFloat(), location.time)
        val context = getContext()
        return listOf(
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.compass_inclination), "${round(field.inclination.toDouble(), 2)}°"),
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.compass_declination), "${round(field.declination.toDouble(), 2)}°"),
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.compass_field_strength), "${round(field.fieldStrength.toDouble(), 2)} nT"),
        )
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/DirectionViewModel.kt
