package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.location.Location
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.extensions.viewmodel.WrappedViewModel
import app.simple.positional.preferences.DirectionPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences.changes
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.DirectionInfo
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.util.DMSConverter
import app.simple.positional.util.DistanceText
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn

class DirectionViewModel(application: Application, location: LocationViewModel) : WrappedViewModel(application) {
    val info: StateFlow<DirectionInfo> = combine(changes(*KEYS), location.location) { _, fix -> info(fix) }
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), DirectionInfo())

    val gimbalLock: StateFlow<Boolean> = preference(DirectionPreferences.directionGimbalLock) { DirectionPreferences.isGimbalLock() }

    private fun info(fix: Location?): DirectionInfo {
        val context = getContext()
        val maps = DirectionPreferences.isUsingMapsTarget() && GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet()
        val stored = if (maps) GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates() else DirectionPreferences.getTargetCoordinates()
        val target = LatLng(stored[0].toDouble(), stored[1].toDouble())
        val formatted = DMSConverter.getFormattedCoordinates(target, context)
        val info = DirectionInfo(
            target = if (maps) context.getString(R.string.using_maps_target) else DirectionPreferences.getTargetLabel() ?: context.getString(R.string.not_available),
            coordinates = listOf(
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_latitude), formatted[0]),
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_longitude), formatted[1]),
            ),
        )
        fix ?: return info
        val meters = LocationExtension.measureDisplacement(arrayOf(LatLng(fix.latitude, fix.longitude), target)).toDouble()
        return info.copy(
            displacement = DistanceText.format(context, meters, MainPreferences.isMetric()),
            bearing = LocationExtension.calculateBearingAngle(fix.latitude, fix.longitude, target.latitude, target.longitude).toFloat(),
        )
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
        val KEYS = arrayOf(
            DirectionPreferences.directionLatitude,
            DirectionPreferences.directionLongitude,
            DirectionPreferences.directionLabel,
            DirectionPreferences.useMapsTarget,
            GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarker,
            GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarkerLatitude,
            GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarkerLongitude,
            MainPreferences.unit,
            MainPreferences.coordinatesFormat,
        )
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/DirectionsViewModel.kt

package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.DirectionDatabase
import app.simple.positional.model.DirectionModel
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CoordinateInput
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CustomLocationForm
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class DirectionsViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val dao = DirectionDatabase.getInstance(application).directionDao()

    val form = CustomLocationForm()

    var editing by mutableStateOf<Long?>(null)
        private set

    val directions: StateFlow<List<DirectionModel>?> = dao.observeAll()
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), null)

    fun open(name: String?, latitude: Double, longitude: Double, dateAdded: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()) {
        form.fill(name.orEmpty(), CoordinateInput.format(latitude), CoordinateInput.format(longitude))
        editing = dateAdded
    }

    fun close() {
        editing = null
    }

    fun save(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        val dateAdded = editing ?: return
        val direction = DirectionModel(latitude, longitude, form.address.trim(), dateAdded)
        persist { dao.insertDirection(direction) }
        close()
    }

    fun delete(direction: DirectionModel) = persist { dao.deleteDirection(direction) }

    private fun persist(block: suspend () -> Unit) {
        viewModelScope.launch { withContext(NonCancellable) { block() } }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/GpsViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Context
import android.location.Location
import androidx.compose.ui.text.AnnotatedString
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.extensions.viewmodel.WrappedViewModel
import app.simple.positional.math.Latency
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round
import app.simple.positional.math.MovementThreshold
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toFeet
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toKiloMetersPerHour
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toMilesPerHour
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences.changes
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.GpsInfo
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.toAnnotatedString
import app.simple.positional.util.DMSConverter
import app.simple.positional.util.Direction.getDirectionNameFromAzimuth
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension.getLocationStatus
import app.simple.positional.util.NetworkCheck.isNetworkAvailable
import app.simple.positional.util.ReverseGeocoder
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.distinctUntilChanged
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filter
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterNotNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.mapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.onStart
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class GpsViewModel(application: Application, source: LocationViewModel) : WrappedViewModel(application) {
    private val addressThreshold = MovementThreshold(ADDRESS_DISTANCE)

    private val point: Flow<Pair<Boolean, LatLng?>> = combine(
        changes(MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate, MainPreferences.latitude, MainPreferences.longitude),
        source.location,
    ) { _, fix -> customPoint()?.let { true to it } ?: (false to fix?.let { LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude) }) }
        .distinctUntilChanged()

    private val address: Flow<String> = point
        .onStart { addressThreshold.reset() }
        .map { it.second }
        .filterNotNull()
        .filter { addressThreshold.accept(it.latitude, it.longitude) }
        .mapLatest(::lookup)
        .onStart { emit(PENDING) }

    private val fix: Flow<FixRows> = combine(source.location, source.provider, source.latency, changes(MainPreferences.unit)) { fix, provider, latency, _ ->
        fixRows(getContext(), fix, provider, latency)
    }

    private val coordinates: Flow<Pair<Boolean, List<InfoRow>>> = combine(point, changes(MainPreferences.coordinatesFormat)) { (custom, latLng), _ ->
        custom to coordinateRows(getContext(), latLng)
    }

    val info: StateFlow<GpsInfo> = combine(fix, coordinates, address, source.target) { rows, (custom, coords), addressLine, target ->
        GpsInfo(
            provider = rows.provider,
            location = rows.location,
            movement = rows.movement,
            coordinates = coords,
            address = addressLine,
            target = target?.toAnnotatedString() ?: AnnotatedString(""),
            speed = rows.speed,
            fixed = rows.fixed,
            enabled = rows.enabled,
            custom = custom,
        )
    }
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), GpsInfo(custom = MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()))

    val settings: StateFlow<GpsSettings> = preference(
        GPSPreferences.pinSkin,
        GPSPreferences.toolsGravity,
        GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerMode,
        GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarker,
        GPSPreferences.compassRotation,
        GPSPreferences.useBearingRotation,
        GPSPreferences.useVolumeKeys,
    ) {
        GpsSettings(
            pin = LocationPins.getLocationPin(),
            targetMode = GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerMode(),
            targetSet = GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet(),
            toolsLeft = GPSPreferences.isToolsGravityLeft(),
            volumeKeys = GPSPreferences.isUsingVolumeKeys(),
            rotation = GpsSettings.rotation(GPSPreferences.isCompassRotation(), GPSPreferences.isBearingRotation()),
        )
    }

    private suspend fun lookup(point: LatLng): String {
        val context = getContext()
        if (!isNetworkAvailable(context)) {
            addressThreshold.reset()
            return context.getString(R.string.internet_connection_alert)
        }
        return ReverseGeocoder.addressLine(context, point.latitude, point.longitude) ?: context.getString(R.string.no_address_found)
    }

    private fun customPoint(): LatLng? {
        if (!MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) return null
        val coordinates = MainPreferences.getCoordinates()
        return LatLng(coordinates[0].toDouble(), coordinates[1].toDouble())
    }

    private class FixRows(
        val provider: List<InfoRow>,
        val location: List<InfoRow>,
        val movement: List<InfoRow>,
        val speed: Float,
        val fixed: Boolean,
        val enabled: Boolean,
    )

    private fun fixRows(context: Context, fix: Location?, provider: String?, latency: Latency?): FixRows {
        val enabled = getLocationStatus(context)
        val metric = MainPreferences.isMetric()
        val length = context.getString(if (metric) R.string.meter else R.string.feet)
        val source = fix?.provider?.uppercase(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale()) ?: provider ?: PENDING
        val latencyRow = if (latency == null) {
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_latency), PENDING)
        } else {
            val unit = context.getString(if (latency.inSeconds) R.string.seconds else R.string.milliseconds)
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_latency), "${latency.value} $unit", latency.isLate)
        }
        val providerRows = listOf(
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_status), context.getString(if (enabled) R.string.gps_enabled else R.string.gps_disabled)),
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_source), source),
        )
        if (fix == null) {
            return FixRows(
                providerRows,
                listOf(InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_accuracy), PENDING), InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_altitude), PENDING), latencyRow),
                listOf(R.string.gps_bearing, R.string.gps_direction, R.string.gps_speed).map { InfoRow(context.getString(it), PENDING) },
                0f,
                false,
                enabled,
            )
        }
        val accuracy = if (metric) fix.accuracy.toDouble() else fix.accuracy.toDouble().toFeet()
        val altitude = if (metric) fix.altitude else fix.altitude.toFeet()
        val kmh = fix.speed.toDouble().toKiloMetersPerHour()
        val speed = if (metric) kmh else kmh.toMilesPerHour()
        val direction = if (fix.speed > 0f) getDirectionNameFromAzimuth(context, fix.bearing.toDouble()) else context.getString(R.string.not_available)
        return FixRows(
            providerRows,
            listOf(
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_accuracy), "${round(accuracy, 2)} $length"),
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_altitude), "${round(altitude, 2)} $length"),
                latencyRow,
            ),
            listOf(
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_bearing), "${fix.bearing}°"),
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_direction), direction),
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_speed), "${round(speed, 2)} ${context.getString(if (metric) R.string.kilometer_hour else R.string.miles_hour)}"),
            ),
            if (metric) fix.speed.toKiloMetersPerHour() else fix.speed.toMilesPerHour(),
            true,
            enabled,
        )
    }

    private fun coordinateRows(context: Context, point: LatLng?): List<InfoRow> = listOf(
        InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_latitude), point?.let { DMSConverter.getFormattedLatitude(it.latitude, context) } ?: PENDING),
        InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_longitude), point?.let { DMSConverter.getFormattedLongitude(it.longitude, context) } ?: PENDING),
    )

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
        const val ADDRESS_DISTANCE = 25.0
        const val PENDING = "-"
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

class LevelViewModel : ViewModel() {

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/LocationViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Context
import android.location.Location
import android.os.SystemClock
import android.text.Spanned
import android.text.TextUtils
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.extensions.viewmodel.WrappedViewModel
import app.simple.positional.location.LocationEvent
import app.simple.positional.location.LocationSource
import app.simple.positional.math.Latency
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round
import app.simple.positional.math.MovementThreshold
import app.simple.positional.math.SeriesStats
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.Direction
import app.simple.positional.util.DistanceText
import app.simple.positional.util.HtmlHelper
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension
import app.simple.positional.util.ReverseGeocoder
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterIsInstance
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.filterNotNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flatMapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.onEach
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.onStart
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.shareIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.transformLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import java.util.Locale

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class LocationViewModel(application: Application) : WrappedViewModel(application) {
    private val providerSetting = preference(MainPreferences.locationProvider) { MainPreferences.getLocationProvider() }
    private val restarts = MutableStateFlow(0)
    private val saved = MovementThreshold(SAVE_DISTANCE)
    private val mutableLatency = MutableStateFlow<Latency?>(null)
    private val mutableAccuracy = MutableStateFlow(emptyList<Float>())
    private val mutableAltitude = MutableStateFlow(emptyList<Float>())
    private val targetPoint = preference(GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarker, GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarkerLatitude, GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarkerLongitude, read = ::currentTarget)
    private var lastFix = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()

    private val events = combine(providerSetting, restarts) { provider, _ -> provider }
        .flatMapLatest { provider -> LocationSource.of(application, provider).events().onStart { lastFix = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() } }
        .onEach(::record)
        .shareIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT))

    val location: StateFlow<Location?> = events.filterIsInstance<LocationEvent.Fix>()
        .map { it.location }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(), null)
    val provider: StateFlow<String?> = events.filterIsInstance<LocationEvent.Provider>()
        .map { it.name?.uppercase(Locale.getDefault()) }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(), null)
    val latency: StateFlow<Latency?> = mutableLatency.asStateFlow()
    val accuracySeries: StateFlow<List<Float>> = mutableAccuracy.asStateFlow()
    val altitudeSeries: StateFlow<List<Float>> = mutableAltitude.asStateFlow()

    private val targetAddress = targetPoint.transformLatest<LatLng?, String?> { point ->
        emit(null)
        if (point != null) emit(ReverseGeocoder.addressLine(getApplication<Application>(), point.latitude, point.longitude))
    }

    val target: StateFlow<Spanned?> = combine(location.filterNotNull(), targetPoint, targetAddress) { fix, point, address -> targetText(fix, point, address) }
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), null)

    fun restart() = restarts.update { it + 1 }

    private fun record(event: LocationEvent) {
        if (event !is LocationEvent.Fix) return
        val fix = event.location
        val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
        mutableLatency.value = Latency(now - lastFix)
        lastFix = now
        mutableAccuracy.update { SeriesStats.append(it, fix.accuracy) }
        mutableAltitude.update { SeriesStats.append(it, fix.altitude.toFloat()) }
        if (saved.accept(fix.latitude, fix.longitude)) {
            MainPreferences.setLastLocation(fix.latitude.toFloat(), fix.longitude.toFloat(), fix.altitude.toFloat())
        }
    }

    private fun currentTarget(): LatLng? =
        if (GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet()) GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) } else null

    private fun targetText(fix: Location, target: LatLng?, address: String?): Spanned {
        val context = getContext()
        val missing = context.getString(R.string.not_available)
        val values = if (target == null) List(ROWS.size) { missing } else {
            val origin = if (MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) {
                MainPreferences.getCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }
            } else {
                LatLng(fix.latitude, fix.longitude)
            }
            val start = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerStartCoordinates().let { LatLng(it[0].toDouble(), it[1].toDouble()) }
            val metric = MainPreferences.isMetric()
            val distance = LocationExtension.measureDisplacement(arrayOf(target, origin)).toDouble()
            val bearing = (LocationExtension.calculateBearingAngle(origin.latitude, origin.longitude, target.latitude, target.longitude) % 360 + 360) % 360
            listOf(
                DistanceText.format(context, distance, metric),
                DistanceText.format(context, LocationExtension.measureDisplacement(arrayOf(start, origin)).toDouble(), metric),
                "${round(bearing, 2)}°",
                Direction.getDirectionNameFromAzimuth(context, bearing),
                if (fix.speed > 0f) eta(context, (distance / fix.speed).toLong()) else missing,
            )
        }
        val rows = ROWS.zip(values) { label, value -> "<b>${context.getString(label)} </b>$value" } +
            "<b>${context.getString(R.string.gps_address)}: </b>${address?.let(TextUtils::htmlEncode) ?: missing}"
        return HtmlHelper.fromHtml(rows.joinToString("<br>"))
    }

    private fun eta(context: Context, seconds: Long): String = when {
        seconds < MINUTE -> context.getString(R.string.eta_seconds, seconds.toString())
        seconds < HOUR -> context.getString(R.string.eta_minutes, (seconds / MINUTE).toString())
        seconds < DAY -> context.getString(R.string.eta_hours, (seconds / HOUR).toString(), (seconds / MINUTE % 60).toString())
        else -> context.getString(
            R.string.eta_days,
            (seconds / DAY).toString(),
            (seconds / HOUR % 24).toString(),
            (seconds / MINUTE % 60).toString(),
        )
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
        const val SAVE_DISTANCE = 50.0
        const val MINUTE = 60L
        const val HOUR = 3_600L
        const val DAY = 86_400L
        val ROWS = listOf(
            R.string.gps_displacement_from_dest,
            R.string.gps_displacement_from_origin,
            R.string.gps_bearing,
            R.string.gps_direction,
            R.string.eta,
        )
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/MapSearchViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.location.Address
import android.location.Geocoder
import android.os.Build
import androidx.annotation.RequiresApi
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CoordinateInput
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CustomLocationForm
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MapSearchQuery
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.FlowPreview
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.debounce
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.distinctUntilChanged
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.mapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.suspendCancellableCoroutine
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlin.coroutines.resume

@OptIn(FlowPreview::class, ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class MapSearchViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    val form = CustomLocationForm()
    private val query = MutableStateFlow("")

    val suggestions: StateFlow<List<Address>> = query
        .debounce { if (it.isEmpty()) 0L else MapSearchQuery.DELAY }
        .map(MapSearchQuery::normalize)
        .distinctUntilChanged()
        .mapLatest { text -> text?.let { search(it) }.orEmpty().filter { it.hasLatitude() && it.hasLongitude() } }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5_000), emptyList())

    init {
        val last = MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()
        form.fill("", CoordinateInput.format(last[0].toDouble()), CoordinateInput.format(last[1].toDouble()))
    }

    fun onAddressChange(text: String) {
        form.address = text
        query.value = text
    }

    fun onCameraIdle(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        form.latitude = CoordinateInput.format(latitude)
        form.longitude = CoordinateInput.format(longitude)
    }

    fun pick(address: Address) {
        query.value = ""
        form.fill(address.getAddressLine(0) ?: form.address, CoordinateInput.format(address.latitude), CoordinateInput.format(address.longitude))
    }

    private suspend fun search(text: String): List<Address> {
        if (!Geocoder.isPresent()) return emptyList()
        val geocoder = Geocoder(getApplication())
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            geocoder.searchAsync(text)
        } else {
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                runCatching { geocoder.getFromLocationName(text, MapSearchQuery.MAX_RESULTS) }.getOrNull().orEmpty()
            }
        }
    }

    @RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU)
    private suspend fun Geocoder.searchAsync(text: String): List<Address> = suspendCancellableCoroutine { continuation ->
        getFromLocationName(text, MapSearchQuery.MAX_RESULTS, object : Geocoder.GeocodeListener {
            override fun onGeocode(addresses: MutableList<Address>) {
                if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(addresses)
            }

            override fun onError(errorMessage: String?) {
                if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(emptyList())
            }
        })
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/MeasureViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.MeasureDatabase
import app.simple.positional.extensions.viewmodel.WrappedViewModel
import app.simple.positional.math.GeoDistance
import app.simple.positional.model.Measure
import app.simple.positional.model.MeasurePoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MeasurePreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences.changes
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.util.DistanceText
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flatMapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class MeasureViewModel(application: Application) : WrappedViewModel(application) {
    private val dao = MeasureDatabase.getInstance(application).measureDao()
    private val selected = preference(MeasurePreferences.LAST_SELECTED_MEASURE) { MeasurePreferences.getLastSelectedMeasure() }
    private val writes = Mutex()

    val measures: StateFlow<List<Measure>?> = dao.observeAll()
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), null)

    val measure: StateFlow<Measure?> = selected
        .flatMapLatest { dao.observeById(it) }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), null)

    val summary: StateFlow<List<InfoRow>> = combine(measure, changes(MainPreferences.unit)) { measure, _ -> summary(measure?.points.orEmpty()) }
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), emptyList())

    val tools: StateFlow<MapTools> = preference(MeasurePreferences.TOOLS_GRAVITY, MeasurePreferences.COMPASS_ROTATION, MeasurePreferences.POLYLINES_WRAPPED) {
        MapTools(MeasurePreferences.isToolsGravityToLeft(), MeasurePreferences.isCompassRotation(), MeasurePreferences.arePolylinesWrapped())
    }

    private fun summary(points: List<MeasurePoint>): List<InfoRow> {
        val context = getContext()
        val meters = GeoDistance.path(points.map { it.latitude to it.longitude })
        return listOf(
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.total), points.size.toString()),
            InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_displacement), DistanceText.format(context, meters, MainPreferences.isMetric())),
        )
    }

    fun addMeasure(name: String, note: String) {
        val created = Measure(System.currentTimeMillis(), name, note)
        persist {
            dao.insertMeasure(created)
            MeasurePreferences.setLastSelectedMeasure(created.dateCreated)
        }
    }

    fun addMeasurePoint(point: MeasurePoint) = editPoints { it + point }

    fun removeMeasurePoint(point: MeasurePoint?) {
        point ?: return
        editPoints { it - point }
    }

    fun deleteMeasure(measure: Measure) = persist {
        dao.deleteMeasure(measure)
        if (MeasurePreferences.getLastSelectedMeasure() == measure.dateCreated) MeasurePreferences.setLastSelectedMeasure(-1)
    }

    private fun editPoints(change: (List<MeasurePoint>) -> List<MeasurePoint>) {
        val id = MeasurePreferences.getLastSelectedMeasure()
        persist {
            val current = dao.getMeasureById(id) ?: return@persist
            dao.updateMeasure(Measure(current.dateCreated, current.name, current.note, ArrayList(change(current.points))))
        }
    }

    private fun persist(block: suspend () -> Unit) {
        viewModelScope.launch { withContext(NonCancellable) { writes.withLock { block() } } }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import androidx.annotation.DrawableRes
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.constants.ClockSkinsConstants.clockNeedleSkins
import app.simple.positional.constants.CompassBloom.compassBloomRes
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.math.ClockMotion
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MapRotation
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn

data class MapTools(val toolsLeft: Boolean, val compass: Boolean, val wrapped: Boolean)

data class GpsSettings(
    @param:DrawableRes val pin: Int,
    val targetMode: Boolean,
    val targetSet: Boolean,
    val toolsLeft: Boolean,
    val volumeKeys: Boolean,
    val rotation: MapRotation,
) {
    companion object {
        fun rotation(compass: Boolean, bearing: Boolean): MapRotation = when {
            compass -> MapRotation.Compass
            bearing -> MapRotation.Bearing
            else -> MapRotation.North
        }
    }
}

data class CompassSettings(
    val gimbalLock: Boolean,
    val physics: Boolean,
    val directionCode: Boolean,
    val bloom: Int?,
    val inertia: Float,
    val damping: Float,
    val magnetic: Float,
) {
    companion object {
        fun bloom(on: Boolean, theme: Int): Int? = if (on) compassBloomRes.getOrNull(theme) else null
    }
}

data class ClockSettings(val motion: ClockMotion, val face24: Boolean, val needles: List<Int>, @param:DrawableRes val pin: Int, val customLocation: Boolean) {
    companion object {
        fun needles(theme: Int): List<Int> = clockNeedleSkins.getOrElse(theme) { clockNeedleSkins[0] }.toList()

        fun pin(skin: Int): Int = LocationPins.pin(skin)
    }
}

private const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L

fun <T> ViewModel.preference(vararg keys: String, read: () -> T): StateFlow<T> =
    SharedPreferences.watch(*keys, read = read).stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), read())

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/SettingsViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.BuildConfig
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsLabels
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsState
import app.simple.positional.util.AppIcons
import app.simple.positional.util.PermissionUtils
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn

class SettingsViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val refreshes = MutableStateFlow(0)

    val state: StateFlow<SettingsState> = combine(SharedPreferences.changes(*keys), refreshes) { _, _ -> read() }
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), read())

    fun refresh() {
        refreshes.value++
    }

    fun setThemeMode(mode: Int) {
        MainPreferences.setDayNight(mode == SettingsLabels.THEME_AUTO)
        if (mode != SettingsLabels.THEME_AUTO) MainPreferences.setTheme(mode)
    }

    fun setIcon(index: Int) {
        AppIcons.select(getApplication(), index)
        refresh()
    }

    fun setCornerRadius(value: Int): Boolean {
        if (value == cornerRadius(MainPreferences.getCornerRadius())) return false
        MainPreferences.setCornerRadius(value)
        return true
    }

    fun setLanguage(code: String): Boolean {
        if (code == MainPreferences.getAppLanguage()) return false
        MainPreferences.setAppLanguage(code)
        return true
    }

    fun setSkipSplashScreen(enabled: Boolean) = MainPreferences.setSkipSplashScreen(enabled)

    fun setMetric(metric: Boolean) = MainPreferences.setUnit(metric)

    fun setCoordinatesFormat(format: Int) = MainPreferences.setCoordinatesFormat(format)

    fun setLocationProvider(provider: String) = MainPreferences.setLocationProvider(provider)

    fun setMapSource(source: Int) = MainPreferences.setMapSource(source)

    fun disableCustomLocation() = MainPreferences.setCustomCoordinates(false)

    fun setKeepScreenOn(enabled: Boolean) = MainPreferences.setScreenOn(enabled)

    private fun read(): SettingsState {
        val context = getApplication<Application>()
        return SettingsState(
            hasLocationPermission = PermissionUtils.checkPermission(context),
            themeMode = themeMode(MainPreferences.isDayNightOn(), MainPreferences.getTheme()),
            isMetric = MainPreferences.isMetric(),
            coordinatesFormat = MainPreferences.getCoordinatesFormat(),
            locationProvider = MainPreferences.getLocationProvider(),
            mapSource = MainPreferences.getMapSource(),
            language = MainPreferences.getAppLanguage() ?: SettingsLabels.LANGUAGE_DEFAULT,
            isCustomLocation = MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate(),
            keepScreenOn = MainPreferences.isScreenOn(),
            skipSplashScreen = MainPreferences.getSkipSplashScreen(),
            iconIndex = AppIcons.current(context),
            cornerRadius = cornerRadius(MainPreferences.getCornerRadius()),
            versionName = BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME,
        )
    }

    companion object {
        private const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
        private const val CORNER_STEP = 5

        private val keys = arrayOf(
            MainPreferences.dayNightMode,
            MainPreferences.theme,
            MainPreferences.unit,
            MainPreferences.coordinatesFormat,
            MainPreferences.locationProvider,
            MainPreferences.mapSource,
            MainPreferences.appLanguage,
            MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate,
            MainPreferences.screenOn,
            MainPreferences.skipSplashScreen,
            MainPreferences.appCornerRadius,
        )

        fun themeMode(dayNight: Boolean, theme: Int): Int = if (dayNight) SettingsLabels.THEME_AUTO else theme

        fun cornerRadius(stored: Int): Int = stored * CORNER_STEP
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/TrailDataViewModel.kt

package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Context
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.TrailIcons
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.TrailPointDatabase
import app.simple.positional.extensions.viewmodel.WrappedViewModel
import app.simple.positional.math.GeoDistance
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailPoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.TrailPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences.changes
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.TrailInfo
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.TrailPointItem
import app.simple.positional.util.DMSConverter
import app.simple.positional.util.DistanceText
import app.simple.positional.util.TimeFormatter.formatDate
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flatMapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOn
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class TrailDataViewModel(application: Application) : WrappedViewModel(application) {
    private val name = preference(TrailPreferences.selectedTrail) { TrailPreferences.getCurrentTrail() }

    val tools: StateFlow<MapTools> = preference(TrailPreferences.toolsMenuGravity, TrailPreferences.compass, TrailPreferences.wrapped) {
        MapTools(TrailPreferences.isToolsGravityToLeft(), TrailPreferences.isCompassRotation(), TrailPreferences.arePolylinesWrapped())
    }

    val points: StateFlow<List<TrailPoint>> = name
        .flatMapLatest { if (it.isEmpty()) flowOf(emptyList()) else dao(it).observeAll() }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), emptyList())

    val info: StateFlow<TrailInfo> = combine(name, points, changes(MainPreferences.unit, MainPreferences.coordinatesFormat)) { trail, points, _ ->
        info(getContext(), trail, points)
    }
        .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), TrailInfo())

    fun save(point: TrailPoint) = persist { dao(it).insertTrailData(point) }

    fun delete(point: TrailPoint?) {
        point ?: return
        persist { dao(it).deleteTrailData(point) }
    }

    private fun info(context: Context, trail: String, points: List<TrailPoint>): TrailInfo {
        val metric = MainPreferences.isMetric()
        val distance = GeoDistance.path(points.map { it.latitude to it.longitude })
        return TrailInfo(
            trail = trail,
            summary = listOf(
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.total), points.size.toString()),
                InfoRow(context.getString(R.string.gps_displacement), DistanceText.format(context, distance, metric)),
            ),
            points = points.asReversed().map { item(context, it, metric) },
        )
    }

    private fun item(context: Context, point: TrailPoint, metric: Boolean): TrailPointItem {
        val (accuracy, unit) = DistanceText.parts(point.accuracy.toDouble(), metric)
        return TrailPointItem(
            point = point,
            icon = TrailIcons.icons.getOrElse(point.iconPosition) { TrailIcons.icons[0] },
            name = point.name.orEmpty().ifEmpty { "--" },
            note = point.note.orEmpty().ifEmpty { context.getString(R.string.not_available) },
            coordinates = context.getString(
                R.string.coordinates_format,
                DMSConverter.getFormattedLatitude(point.latitude, context),
                DMSConverter.getFormattedLongitude(point.longitude, context),
            ),
            accuracy = context.getString(R.string.trail_data_accuracy, round(accuracy, 1).toString(), context.getString(unit)),
            date = point.timeAdded.formatDate(),
        )
    }

    private fun dao(trail: String) = TrailPointDatabase.getInstance(getApplication(), trail).trailDataDao()

    private fun persist(block: suspend (String) -> Unit) {
        val trail = TrailPreferences.getCurrentTrail()
        if (trail.isEmpty()) return
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { withContext(NonCancellable) { block(trail) } }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.TrailDatabase
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.TrailPointDatabase
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailEntry
import app.simple.positional.preferences.TrailPreferences
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class TrailsViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val dao = TrailDatabase.getInstance(application).trailDao()

    val trails: StateFlow<List<TrailEntry>?> = dao.observeAll()
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), null)

    fun addTrail(trailEntry: TrailEntry) = persist {
        dao.insertTrail(trailEntry)
        TrailPreferences.setCurrentTrailName(trailEntry.trailName.orEmpty())
    }

    fun deleteTrail(trailEntry: TrailEntry) = persist {
        dao.deleteTrail(trailEntry)
        val name = trailEntry.trailName ?: return@persist
        if (TrailPreferences.getCurrentTrail() == name) TrailPreferences.setCurrentTrailName("")
        TrailPointDatabase.close(name)
        getApplication<Application>().deleteDatabase("$name.db")
    }

    private fun persist(block: suspend () -> Unit) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { withContext(NonCancellable) { block() } }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.widgets

import android.app.AlarmManager

class ClockWidget2x2 : ClockWidget(ClockWidgetSpec(R.layout.widget_clock_2x2, 110, 10, 15, "2x2", 1522))
class ClockWidget3x3 : ClockWidget(ClockWidgetSpec(R.layout.widget_clock_3x3, 180, 25, 25, "3x3", 1533))
class ClockWidget4x4 : ClockWidget(ClockWidgetSpec(R.layout.widget_clock, 250, 25, 30, "4x4", 1544))

data class ClockWidgetSpec(val layout: Int, val sizeDp: Int, val handInsetDp: Int, val indicatorDp: Int, val legacyService: String, val legacyAlarm: Int)

abstract class ClockWidget(private val spec: ClockWidgetSpec) : AppWidgetProvider() {
    override fun onUpdate(context: Context, appWidgetManager: AppWidgetManager, appWidgetIds: IntArray) {
        val pending = goAsync()
        val provider = javaClass
        CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default).launch {
            try {
                cancelLegacyAlarm(context)
                appWidgetManager.updateAppWidget(appWidgetIds, ClockWidgetRenderer.render(context, spec))
                ClockWidgets.schedule(context, provider, appWidgetIds)
            } finally {
                pending.finish()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun cancelLegacyAlarm(context: Context) {
        val intent = Intent().setClassName(context, "app.simple.positional.services.ClockWidgetService${spec.legacyService}")
        PendingIntent.getService(context, spec.legacyAlarm, intent, PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_NO_CREATE)?.let {
            context.getSystemService(AlarmManager::class.java).cancel(it)
            it.cancel()
        }
    }

    override fun onDisabled(context: Context) {
        context.getSystemService(AlarmManager::class.java).cancel(ClockWidgets.updateIntent(context, javaClass, intArrayOf()))
    }
}

object ClockWidgets {
    private val providers = listOf(ClockWidget2x2::class.java, ClockWidget3x3::class.java, ClockWidget4x4::class.java)

    fun refresh(context: Context) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.S) return
        val manager = AppWidgetManager.getInstance(context) ?: return
        providers.forEach { provider ->
            val ids = manager.getAppWidgetIds(ComponentName(context, provider))
            if (ids.isNotEmpty()) context.sendBroadcast(intent(context, provider, ids))
        }
    }

    internal fun schedule(context: Context, provider: Class<out ClockWidget>, ids: IntArray) {
        val next = DayNight.nextChange(ZonedDateTime.now()).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
        context.getSystemService(AlarmManager::class.java).set(AlarmManager.RTC, next, updateIntent(context, provider, ids))
    }

    internal fun updateIntent(context: Context, provider: Class<out ClockWidget>, ids: IntArray): PendingIntent =
        PendingIntent.getBroadcast(context, provider.name.hashCode(), intent(context, provider, ids), PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT)

    private fun intent(context: Context, provider: Class<out ClockWidget>, ids: IntArray) = Intent(context, provider)
        .setAction(AppWidgetManager.ACTION_APPWIDGET_UPDATE)
        .putExtra(AppWidgetManager.EXTRA_APPWIDGET_IDS, ids)
}

object DayNight {
    private const val DAY_START = 6
    private const val NIGHT_START = 19

    fun isDay(hour: Int) = hour in DAY_START until NIGHT_START

    fun nextChange(now: ZonedDateTime): ZonedDateTime {
        val today = now.toLocalDate()
        return when {
            now.hour < DAY_START -> today.atTime(DAY_START, 0)
            now.hour < NIGHT_START -> today.atTime(NIGHT_START, 0)
            else -> today.plusDays(1).atTime(DAY_START, 0)
        }.atZone(now.zone)
    }
}

private object ClockWidgetRenderer {
    private const val FACE_RADIUS = 0.47f
    private const val TRAIL_INSET_DP = 5
    private const val INDICATOR_SCALE = 2

    fun render(context: Context, spec: ClockWidgetSpec): RemoteViews {
        SharedPreferences.init(context)
        val themed = ContextThemeWrapper(context, AccentThemes.of(context) ?: R.style.Positional)
        val density = context.resources.displayMetrics.density
        val size = (spec.sizeDp * density).roundToInt()
        val inset = (spec.handInsetDp * density).roundToInt()
        val indicator = (spec.indicatorDp * density).roundToInt() * INDICATOR_SCALE
        val needles = ClockSkinsConstants.clockNeedleSkins.getOrElse(ClockPreferences.getClockNeedleTheme()) { ClockSkinsConstants.clockNeedleSkins[0] }
        val dayNight = if (DayNight.isDay(ZonedDateTime.now().hour)) R.drawable.ic_day else R.drawable.ic_night
        return RemoteViews(context.packageName, spec.layout).apply {
            setImageViewBitmap(R.id.widget_clock_face, themed.bitmapOf(R.drawable.clock_face, size))
            setImageViewBitmap(R.id.widget_day_night_indicator, themed.bitmapOf(dayNight, indicator))
            setIcon(R.id.widget_clock, "setDial", Icon.createWithBitmap(Bitmap.createBitmap(size, size, Bitmap.Config.ALPHA_8)))
            setIcon(R.id.widget_clock, "setHourHand", Icon.createWithBitmap(themed.bitmapOf(needles[0], size, inset)))
            setIcon(R.id.widget_clock, "setMinuteHand", Icon.createWithBitmap(themed.bitmapOf(needles[1], size, inset)))
            setIcon(R.id.widget_clock, "setSecondHand", Icon.createWithBitmap(themed.secondHand(needles[2], size, inset, (TRAIL_INSET_DP * density).roundToInt())))
        }
    }

    private fun Context.bitmapOf(@DrawableRes id: Int, size: Int, inset: Int = 0): Bitmap {
        val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(size, size, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        drawInto(Canvas(bitmap), id, size, inset)
        return bitmap
    }

    private fun Context.secondHand(@DrawableRes id: Int, size: Int, inset: Int, trailInset: Int): Bitmap {
        val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(size, size, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val canvas = Canvas(bitmap)
        val center = size / 2f
        canvas.save()
        canvas.rotate(-90f, center, center)
        drawInto(canvas, R.drawable.clock_trail, size, trailInset)
        canvas.restore()
        canvas.drawCircle(center, center, size * FACE_RADIUS, Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.CLEAR) })
        drawInto(canvas, id, size, inset)
        return bitmap
    }

    private fun Context.drawInto(canvas: Canvas, @DrawableRes id: Int, size: Int, inset: Int) {
        ContextCompat.getDrawable(this, id)?.run {
            setBounds(inset, inset, size - inset, size - inset)
            draw(canvas)
        }
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.widgets

import android.content.Context

import androidx.glance.layout.padding

import app.simple.positional.util.MoonAngle

import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.MoonIllumination
import java.time.Instant
import java.util.TimeZone

class MoonPhaseWidget : GlanceAppWidgetReceiver() {
    override val glanceAppWidget: GlanceAppWidget = MoonPhaseGlance()
}

class MoonPhaseGlance : GlanceAppWidget() {
    override suspend fun provideGlance(context: Context, id: GlanceId) {
        val phase = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            MoonIllumination.compute().timezone(TimeZone.getDefault().id).on(Instant.now()).execute().phase
        }
        val art = MoonAngle.getMoonPhaseGraphics(MathExtensions.round(phase, 2))
        val label = MoonAngle.getMoonPhase(context, phase)
        provideContent {
            Column(
                GlanceModifier.fillMaxSize().background(ImageProvider(R.drawable.bg_widget)).padding(20.dp),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                Image(ImageProvider(art), contentDescription = label, modifier = GlanceModifier.size(120.dp))
                Text(label, style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.textSecondary)))
            }
        }
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.widgets

import android.content.Context
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.glance.GlanceId
import androidx.glance.GlanceModifier
import androidx.glance.ImageProvider
import androidx.glance.appwidget.GlanceAppWidget
import androidx.glance.appwidget.GlanceAppWidgetReceiver
import androidx.glance.appwidget.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.glance.appwidget.lazy.items
import androidx.glance.appwidget.provideContent
import androidx.glance.background
import androidx.glance.layout.Column
import androidx.glance.layout.Row
import androidx.glance.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.glance.layout.fillMaxWidth
import androidx.glance.layout.padding
import androidx.glance.text.FontWeight
import androidx.glance.text.Text
import androidx.glance.text.TextStyle
import androidx.glance.unit.ColorProvider
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.Twilight
import app.simple.positional.util.TwilightSpan
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.time.ZonedDateTime

class TwilightWidget : GlanceAppWidgetReceiver() {
    override val glanceAppWidget: GlanceAppWidget = TwilightGlance()
}

private data class TwilightLine(val color: Long, val label: String, val value: String)

class TwilightGlance : GlanceAppWidget() {
    override suspend fun provideGlance(context: Context, id: GlanceId) {
        SharedPreferences.init(context)
        val pattern = WidgetSky.pattern()
        val notAvailable = context.getString(R.string.not_available)
        fun time(value: ZonedDateTime?) = value?.format(pattern) ?: notAvailable
        fun lines(spans: List<TwilightSpan>) = spans.map {
            TwilightLine(it.phase.color, context.getString(it.phase.label), if (it.range) "${time(it.start)} ~ ${time(it.end)}" else time(it.start))
        }
        val day = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            val (latitude, longitude) = WidgetSky.coordinates()
            Twilight.compute(latitude, longitude, ClockPreferences.getTimeZone())
        }
        val morning = lines(day.morning)
        val evening = lines(day.evening)
        val title = context.getString(R.string.twilight)
        val morningTitle = context.getString(R.string.twilight_morning)
        val eveningTitle = context.getString(R.string.twilight_evening)
        val updated = WidgetSky.updated(context, pattern)
        provideContent {
            Column(GlanceModifier.fillMaxSize().background(ImageProvider(R.drawable.bg_widget)).padding(10.dp)) {
                WidgetHeader(R.drawable.ic_twilight, title, updated)
                LazyColumn(GlanceModifier.fillMaxSize().padding(horizontal = 10.dp)) {
                    item { Heading(morningTitle) }
                    items(morning) { Line(it) }
                    item { Heading(eveningTitle) }
                    items(evening) { Line(it) }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun Heading(text: String) {
    Text(
        text,
        style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.textPrimary), fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 15.sp),
        modifier = GlanceModifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 8.dp, bottom = 4.dp),
    )
}

@Composable
private fun Line(line: TwilightLine) {
    val style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.textSecondary), fontSize = 12.sp)
    Row(GlanceModifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 1.dp)) {
        Text("⬤ ", style = style.copy(color = ColorProvider(Color(line.color))))
        Text("${line.label} ", style = style.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold))
        Text(line.value, style = style, modifier = GlanceModifier.defaultWeight())
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/widgets/WidgetParts.kt

package app.simple.positional.widgets

import android.content.Context
import androidx.annotation.DrawableRes
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.glance.GlanceModifier
import androidx.glance.Image
import androidx.glance.ImageProvider
import androidx.glance.layout.Alignment
import androidx.glance.layout.Column
import androidx.glance.layout.Row
import androidx.glance.layout.fillMaxWidth
import androidx.glance.layout.padding
import androidx.glance.layout.size
import androidx.glance.text.FontWeight
import androidx.glance.text.Text
import androidx.glance.text.TextStyle
import androidx.glance.unit.ColorProvider
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.SunTimes
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.util.TimeZone

internal object WidgetSky {
    fun coordinates(): Pair<Double, Double> {
        val c = if (MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) MainPreferences.getCoordinates() else MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()
        return c[0].toDouble() to c[1].toDouble()
    }

    @DrawableRes
    fun pin(): Int = if (MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) R.drawable.ic_place_custom else LocationPins.getLocationPin()

    fun pattern(): DateTimeFormatter =
        if (ClockPreferences.getDefaultClockTimeFormat()) DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("hh:mm a").withLocale(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale())
        else DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm")

    fun updated(context: Context, pattern: DateTimeFormatter) = context.getString(R.string.last_updated) + " • " + pattern.format(ZonedDateTime.now())

    fun sunTimes(pattern: DateTimeFormatter): List<Pair<Int, String>> {
        val (latitude, longitude) = coordinates()
        val sun = SunTimes.compute().timezone(TimeZone.getDefault().id).on(Instant.now()).latitude(latitude).longitude(longitude).execute()
        return listOf(
            R.drawable.ic_widget_sunrise to sun.rise,
            R.drawable.ic_widget_sun_noon to sun.noon,
            R.drawable.ic_widget_sun_set to sun.set,
            R.drawable.ic_widget_sun_nadir to sun.nadir,
        ).map { (icon, time) -> icon to (time?.let(pattern::format) ?: "--") }
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun WidgetHeader(@DrawableRes icon: Int, title: String, caption: String) {
    Row(GlanceModifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(10.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Image(ImageProvider(icon), contentDescription = null, modifier = GlanceModifier.size(20.dp))
        Text(
            title,
            style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.textTertiary), fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium),
            modifier = GlanceModifier.defaultWeight().padding(horizontal = 10.dp),
        )
        Text(caption, style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.dividerColor), fontSize = 11.sp))
    }
}

@Composable
internal fun SunTimesRow(times: List<Pair<Int, String>>, modifier: GlanceModifier) {
    Row(modifier, verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        times.forEach { (icon, time) ->
            Column(GlanceModifier.defaultWeight(), horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                Image(ImageProvider(icon), contentDescription = null, modifier = GlanceModifier.size(36.dp))
                Text(time, style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.textTertiary), fontSize = 12.sp), modifier = GlanceModifier.padding(top = 6.dp))
            }
        }
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/constants/HtmlPageTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.constants

import app.simple.positional.R
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

class HtmlPageTest {
    @Test
    fun everyPageHasAnAsset() {
        HtmlPage.entries.forEach { assertTrue(it.asset, File("src/main/assets/html/${it.asset}.html").isFile) }
    }

    @Test
    fun everyAssetHasAPage() {
        val assets = File("src/main/assets/html").listFiles().orEmpty().map { it.nameWithoutExtension }.toSet()
        assertEquals(HtmlPage.entries.map { it.asset }.toSet(), assets)
    }

    @Test
    fun legalTitlesResolve() {
        assertEquals(HtmlPage.PRIVACY_POLICY, HtmlPage.forTitle(R.string.privacy_policy))
        assertEquals(HtmlPage.TERMS, HtmlPage.forTitle(R.string.terms_of_use))
        assertEquals(HtmlPage.CREDITS, HtmlPage.forTitle(R.string.credits))
        assertNull(HtmlPage.forTitle(R.string.app_name))
    }

    @Test
    fun namesRoundTrip() {
        HtmlPage.entries.forEach { assertEquals(it, HtmlPage.forName(it.name)) }
        assertNull(HtmlPage.forName(null))
        assertNull(HtmlPage.forName("unknown"))
    }

    @Test
    fun urlPointsToAndroidAssets() {
        assertEquals("file:///android_asset/html/credits.html", HtmlPage.CREDITS.url)
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/constants/LocationPinsTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.constants

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class LocationPinsTest {
    @Test
    fun unknownSkinFallsBackToFirst() {
        assertEquals(LocationPins.locationsPins[0], LocationPins.pin(-1))
        assertEquals(LocationPins.locationsPins[0], LocationPins.pin(LocationPins.locationsPins.size))
        assertEquals(LocationPins.locationsPins[3], LocationPins.pin(3))
    }

    @Test
    fun fillIsTranslucentStroke() {
        LocationPins.locationsPins.indices.forEach { skin ->
            val fill = LocationPins.fillColor(skin)
            val stroke = LocationPins.strokeColor(skin)
            assertEquals(0x4D, fill ushr 24)
            assertEquals(0xFF, stroke ushr 24)
            assertEquals(stroke and 0xFFFFFF, fill and 0xFFFFFF)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun firstSkinKeepsOriginalColors() {
        assertEquals(0xFF1B9CFF.toInt(), LocationPins.strokeColor(0))
        assertEquals(0x4D1B9CFF, LocationPins.fillColor(0))
        assertEquals(LocationPins.strokeColor(0), LocationPins.strokeColor(99))
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/database/converters/MeasurePointConverterTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.database.converters

import app.simple.positional.model.Measure
import app.simple.positional.model.MeasurePoint
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertSame
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class MeasurePointConverterTest {
    private val converter = MeasurePointConverter()

    @Test
    fun roundTrip() {
        val points = arrayListOf(MeasurePoint(1.5, -2.25, 1), MeasurePoint(-45.0, 170.125, 2))
        val data = converter.fromMeasurePoints(points)
        assertEquals("1.5,-2.25,1;-45.0,170.125,2;", data)
        assertEquals(points, converter.toMeasurePoints(data))
    }

    @Test
    fun emptyAndNull() {
        assertEquals("", converter.fromMeasurePoints(arrayListOf()))
        assertNull(converter.fromMeasurePoints(null))
        assertNull(converter.toMeasurePoints(null))
        assertTrue(converter.toMeasurePoints("")!!.isEmpty())
    }

    @Test
    fun pointsCompareByValue() {
        val list = arrayListOf(MeasurePoint(3.0, 4.0, 1))
        list.remove(MeasurePoint(3.0, 4.0, 1))
        assertTrue(list.isEmpty())
    }

    @Test
    fun measureCreatesPointsOnce() {
        val measure = Measure(1L, "a", null)
        val points = measure.points
        points.add(MeasurePoint(1.0, 2.0, 1))
        assertSame(points, measure.measurePoints)
        assertEquals(1, measure.points.size)
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/GoogleMapStyleTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import app.simple.positional.R
import com.google.android.gms.maps.GoogleMap
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class GoogleMapStyleTest {
    @Test
    fun mapTypeFollowsSatelliteAndLabels() {
        assertEquals(GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_NORMAL, GoogleMapStyle.type(satellite = false, label = true))
        assertEquals(GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_NORMAL, GoogleMapStyle.type(satellite = false, label = false))
        assertEquals(GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_HYBRID, GoogleMapStyle.type(satellite = true, label = true))
        assertEquals(GoogleMap.MAP_TYPE_SATELLITE, GoogleMapStyle.type(satellite = true, label = false))
    }

    @Test
    fun highContrastWinsOverNightMode() {
        assertEquals(R.raw.map_high_contrast_labelled, GoogleMapStyle.raw(label = true, highContrast = true, night = true))
        assertEquals(R.raw.map_high_contrast_non_labelled, GoogleMapStyle.raw(label = false, highContrast = true, night = false))
    }

    @Test
    fun styleFollowsNightMode() {
        assertEquals(R.raw.maps_dark_labelled, GoogleMapStyle.raw(label = true, highContrast = false, night = true))
        assertEquals(R.raw.maps_dark_no_label, GoogleMapStyle.raw(label = false, highContrast = false, night = true))
        assertEquals(R.raw.maps_light_labelled, GoogleMapStyle.raw(label = true, highContrast = false, night = false))
        assertEquals(R.raw.maps_light_no_label, GoogleMapStyle.raw(label = false, highContrast = false, night = false))
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/MapPreferencesTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class MapPreferencesTest {
    @Test
    fun appliesOnlyChangedValues() = runBlocking {
        val applied = mutableListOf<Int>()
        val preferences = MapPreferences()
        preferences.add(1, flowOf(1, 2, 2, 3)) { applied += it }
        preferences.launchIn(this).join()
        assertEquals(listOf(2, 3), applied)
    }

    @Test
    fun resumeAppliesChangeMadeWhilePausedOnce() = runBlocking {
        var stored = 1
        val applied = mutableListOf<Int>()
        val preferences = MapPreferences()
        preferences.add(stored, flow { emit(stored) }) { applied += it }
        preferences.launchIn(this).join()
        stored = 5
        preferences.launchIn(this).join()
        preferences.launchIn(this).join()
        assertEquals(listOf(5), applied)
    }

    @Test
    fun bindingsRunIndependently() = runBlocking {
        val applied = mutableListOf<String>()
        val preferences = MapPreferences()
        preferences.add(false, flowOf(true)) { applied += "compass" }
        preferences.add("a", flowOf("a")) { applied += "look" }
        preferences.launchIn(this).join()
        assertEquals(listOf("compass"), applied)
    }

    @Test
    fun emptyBindingsFinish() = runBlocking {
        MapPreferences().launchIn(this).join()
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/maps/OsmStyleTest.kt

package app.simple.positional.decorations.maps

import app.simple.positional.math.GeoDistance
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class OsmStyleTest {
    @Test
    fun styleUsesOsmTilesAndOverlayLayers() {
        val json = OsmStyle.json(dark = false)
        assertTrue(json.contains("https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"))
        assertTrue(json.contains("OpenStreetMap contributors"))
        listOf(OsmStyle.PIN, OsmStyle.ACCURACY, OsmStyle.LINE, OsmStyle.GUIDE, OsmStyle.MARKERS).forEach {
            assertTrue(json.contains("\"id\":\"$it\""))
            assertTrue(json.contains("\"$it\":{\"type\":\"geojson\""))
        }
        assertFalse(json.contains("raster-brightness-min"))
    }

    @Test
    fun darkStyleInvertsTiles() {
        assertTrue(OsmStyle.json(dark = true).contains("\"raster-brightness-min\":1"))
    }

    @Test
    fun geometryUsesLongitudeFirst() {
        assertEquals("""{"type":"Point","coordinates":[2.5,48.5]}""", OsmStyle.point(48.5, 2.5))
        assertEquals("""{"type":"LineString","coordinates":[[2.0,1.0],[4.0,3.0]]}""", OsmStyle.line(listOf(1.0 to 2.0, 3.0 to 4.0)))
    }

    @Test
    fun shortLinesAreEmpty() {
        assertEquals(OsmStyle.EMPTY_FEATURES, OsmStyle.line(emptyList()))
        assertEquals(OsmStyle.EMPTY_FEATURES, OsmStyle.line(listOf(1.0 to 2.0)))
    }

    @Test
    fun markersCarryTheirIcon() {
        assertEquals(
            """{"type":"FeatureCollection","features":[{"type":"Feature","geometry":{"type":"Point","coordinates":[2.0,1.0]},"properties":{"icon":"trail-3"}}]}""",
            OsmStyle.markers(listOf(Triple(1.0, 2.0, "trail-3"))),
        )
        assertEquals(OsmStyle.EMPTY_FEATURES, OsmStyle.markers(emptyList()))
    }

    @Test
    fun circleIsClosedAndAtRadius() {
        val points = OsmStyle.circlePoints(37.0, -76.5, 250.0)
        assertEquals(points.first().first, points.last().first, 1e-9)
        assertEquals(points.first().second, points.last().second, 1e-9)
        points.forEach { assertEquals(250.0, GeoDistance.meters(37.0, -76.5, it.first, it.second), 1.0) }
    }

    @Test
    fun polygonWrapsTheRing() {
        assertTrue(OsmStyle.circle(0.0, 0.0, 10.0).startsWith("""{"type":"Polygon","coordinates":[[["""))
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/decorations/utils/DelayedTaskTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.decorations.utils

import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.TestScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.advanceTimeBy
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runCurrent
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class DelayedTaskTest {
    private val scope = TestScope()
    private var runs = 0
    private val task = DelayedTask(DELAY) { runs++ }

    private fun advance(millis: Long) {
        scope.advanceTimeBy(millis)
        scope.runCurrent()
    }

    @Test
    fun runsOnceAfterDelay() {
        task.schedule(scope)
        advance(DELAY - 1)
        assertEquals(0, runs)
        advance(1)
        assertEquals(1, runs)
        advance(DELAY * 3)
        assertEquals(1, runs)
    }

    @Test
    fun rescheduleRestartsDelay() {
        task.schedule(scope)
        advance(DELAY - 1)
        task.schedule(scope)
        advance(DELAY - 1)
        assertEquals(0, runs)
        advance(1)
        assertEquals(1, runs)
    }

    @Test
    fun cancelStopsPendingRun() {
        task.schedule(scope)
        advance(DELAY / 2)
        task.cancel()
        advance(DELAY * 2)
        assertEquals(0, runs)
    }

    @Test
    fun withoutScopeNothingRuns() {
        task.schedule(null)
        advance(DELAY * 2)
        assertEquals(0, runs)
    }

    @Test
    fun cancelledChildStopsTaskButNotParent() {
        val child = scope.child()
        task.schedule(child)
        child.cancel()
        advance(DELAY * 2)
        assertEquals(0, runs)
        assertTrue(scope.isActive)
        task.schedule(scope)
        advance(DELAY)
        assertEquals(1, runs)
    }

    @Test
    fun cancelledParentCancelsChild() {
        val parent = TestScope()
        val child = parent.child()
        parent.cancel()
        assertFalse(child.isActive)
    }

    private companion object {
        const val DELAY = 500L
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/location/AndroidLocationSourceTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.location

import android.location.LocationManager
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class AndroidLocationSourceTest {
    private val all = setOf(LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER, LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER, LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER)

    @Test
    fun prefersGpsWithFinePermission() {
        assertEquals(LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER, AndroidLocationSource.bestProvider(true) { it in all })
    }

    @Test
    fun fallsBackToNetworkWhenGpsIsOff() {
        assertEquals(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER, AndroidLocationSource.bestProvider(true) { it != LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER })
    }

    @Test
    fun usesPassiveWhenLocationIsOff() {
        assertEquals(LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER, AndroidLocationSource.bestProvider(true) { it == LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER })
    }

    @Test
    fun approximatePermissionSkipsGps() {
        assertEquals(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER, AndroidLocationSource.bestProvider(false) { it in all })
    }

    @Test
    fun approximatePermissionWithoutNetworkHasNoProvider() {
        assertNull(AndroidLocationSource.bestProvider(false) { it == LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER })
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/math/GeoDistanceTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class GeoDistanceTest {
    @Test
    fun samePointIsZero() {
        assertEquals(0.0, GeoDistance.meters(37.0, -76.4, 37.0, -76.4), 1e-9)
    }

    @Test
    fun oneDegreeOfLatitude() {
        assertEquals(111_195.0, GeoDistance.meters(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0), 5.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun antipodesAreHalfCircumference() {
        assertEquals(20_015_115.0, GeoDistance.meters(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 180.0), 50.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun distanceIsSymmetric() {
        val forward = GeoDistance.meters(51.5, -0.12, 48.85, 2.35)
        val backward = GeoDistance.meters(48.85, 2.35, 51.5, -0.12)
        assertEquals(forward, backward, 1e-6)
        assertEquals(343_500.0, forward, 1_500.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun thresholdIgnoresSmallMoves() {
        val threshold = MovementThreshold(25.0)
        assertTrue(threshold.accept(10.0, 10.0))
        assertFalse(threshold.accept(10.0001, 10.0))
        assertTrue(threshold.accept(10.001, 10.0))
        assertFalse(threshold.accept(10.001, 10.0))
    }

    @Test
    fun resetAcceptsAgain() {
        val threshold = MovementThreshold(25.0)
        assertTrue(threshold.accept(1.0, 1.0))
        threshold.reset()
        assertTrue(threshold.accept(1.0, 1.0))
    }

    @Test
    fun pathSumsSegments() {
        val a = 0.0 to 0.0
        val b = 1.0 to 0.0
        val c = 2.0 to 0.0
        assertEquals(GeoDistance.meters(0.0, 0.0, 2.0, 0.0), GeoDistance.path(listOf(a, b, c)), 1e-6)
        assertEquals(0.0, GeoDistance.path(listOf(a)), 0.0)
        assertEquals(0.0, GeoDistance.path(emptyList()), 0.0)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class LatencyTest {
    @Test
    fun shortGapsStayInMilliseconds() {
        val latency = Latency(850)
        assertFalse(latency.inSeconds)
        assertEquals(850L, latency.value)
        assertFalse(latency.isLate)
    }

    @Test
    fun longGapsSwitchToSeconds() {
        val latency = Latency(1234)
        assertTrue(latency.inSeconds)
        assertEquals(1.234, latency.value.toDouble(), 1e-9)
        assertFalse(latency.isLate)
    }

    @Test
    fun gapsOverFiveSecondsAreLate() {
        assertTrue(Latency(5001).isLate)
        assertFalse(Latency(5000).isLate)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Test

class LowPassFilterTest {
    @Test
    fun blendsReadingsByAlpha() {
        val readings = floatArrayOf(0f, 10f, 20f)
        LowPassFilter.smoothAndSetReadings(readings, floatArrayOf(10f, 20f, 40f), 0.5f)
        assertArrayEquals(floatArrayOf(5f, 15f, 30f), readings, 1e-6f)
    }

    @Test
    fun alphaOneReplacesReadings() {
        val readings = floatArrayOf(1f, 2f, 3f)
        LowPassFilter.smoothAndSetReadings(readings, floatArrayOf(7f, 8f, 9f), 1f)
        assertArrayEquals(floatArrayOf(7f, 8f, 9f), readings, 0f)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class MapInterpolationTest {
    @Test
    fun positionMovesLinearly() {
        val (latitude, longitude) = MapInterpolation.position(0.5f, 0.0, 0.0, 10.0, 20.0)
        assertEquals(5.0, latitude, 1e-9)
        assertEquals(10.0, longitude, 1e-9)
    }

    @Test
    fun positionCrossesAntimeridianTheShortWay() {
        assertEquals(180.0, MapInterpolation.position(0.5f, 0.0, 179.0, 0.0, -179.0).second, 1e-9)
        assertEquals(-180.0, MapInterpolation.position(0.5f, 0.0, -179.0, 0.0, 179.0).second, 1e-9)
    }

    @Test
    fun angleTurnsTheShortWay() {
        assertEquals(0f, MapInterpolation.angle(0.5f, 350f, 10f), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(0f, MapInterpolation.angle(0.5f, 10f, 350f), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(10f, MapInterpolation.angle(1f, 350f, 10f), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(350f, MapInterpolation.angle(0f, 350f, 10f), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(90f, MapInterpolation.angle(0.5f, 0f, 180f), 1e-4f)
    }

    @Test
    fun radiusIsNotWrappedLikeAnAngle() {
        assertEquals(205.0, MapInterpolation.linear(0.5f, 10.0, 400.0), 1e-9)
    }
}
